# Avatar HRMS — Productivity & Break Tracker (Avatar India)

Standalone productivity and break tracker. Multi-tenant across three platforms: **Take Health, Avatar, Co-Lab**.
Deliberately **not sales-specific** — it serves multiple companies. Keep features and wording generic.
**Hard deadline: 2 days.**

## How to work with me

I'm a non-expert. Before implementing anything, give me four things up front:
1. What it does, in plain layman terms
2. What the feature does
3. The logic — call triggers, chain of actions
4. How the visuals will look

Whenever we build something a senior or an interviewer could ask about, explain it in simplified, interview-ready language — proactively, without being asked.

**Never make an architecture, infrastructure, cost or dependency decision silently.** Present options with tradeoffs and let me choose.

Act as a senior software developer / Forward Deployed Engineer. English only.

## Stack and layout

- **Backend** (`backend/`): TypeScript, Express 5, Prisma **7.10.0 (PINNED — do not upgrade)**, PostgreSQL on Neon, `bcryptjs`, `jsonwebtoken`. Runs on port **5001**.
- **Frontend** (`frontend/`): Next.js **16.3.5** App Router, React 19, Tailwind 4. Runs on port **3000**. API base comes from `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` (default `http://localhost:5001/api`), see `frontend/lib/api.ts`.
- CORS allows 3000 and 3001 (`backend/src/server.ts`).
- **ESM** — every relative import needs the `.js` extension.
- Backend is organised by module: `src/modules/<name>/<name>.routes.ts` + `<name>.service.ts`. Prisma schema is split across files in `prisma/schema/` (`schema.prisma` holds only generator + datasource).
- Next.js 16 differs from older versions — `frontend/CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md` say to read `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` before writing frontend code.

### Commands (from `backend/`)
- `npm run dev` — tsx watch
- `npm run db:migrate` then **`npm run db:generate`** (migrate does not generate)
- `npm run db:seed` — same as `npx tsx prisma/seed.ts`, safe to re-run
- `npm run db:studio`

Frontend: `npm run dev` in `frontend/`.

## Hard-won traps — read before touching these

- **Prisma 7:** `url` / `directUrl` are NOT allowed in the datasource block. They live in `prisma.config.ts`.
- **Prisma 7 needs a driver adapter:** `@prisma/adapter-pg`, `new PrismaClient({ adapter })`.
- **Generator must be `prisma-client` with an explicit `output`**, NOT `prisma-client-js`. The latter generates without error but exports no `PrismaClient`. Client is imported from `src/generated/prisma/client.js`.
- **`prisma migrate dev` does NOT run generate.** Always run both.
- **Never run `npm install prisma@latest`** — it resolves to 8.x, which renames `migrate` to `migration`.
- **ESM imports run before `dotenv.config()`.** Any module that reads `process.env` must have `import "dotenv/config"` as its FIRST line. `src/lib/prisma.ts` depends on this.
- **NEVER edit `backend/.env` from the terminal.** `Add-Content` corrupted it once by gluing a line on. Edit it in the editor only. It needs exactly 5 vars: `PORT`, `DATABASE_URL`, `DIRECT_URL`, `JWT_SECRET`, `JWT_EXPIRES_IN`.
- **Express 5:** `req.params.id` is typed `string | string[]`. Always wrap in `String()`.
- `"injected env (0)"` from tsx is tsx's own counter, not a dotenv failure. Ignore it.

## Conventions that must hold

- **Timezone:** a calendar day is derived from the PLATFORM's timezone via `Intl.DateTimeFormat` and stored as `Date.UTC(y, m, d)` midnight. `@db.Date` columns come back as UTC midnight, so `toISOString().slice(0, 10)` is the correct way to read the day. Never `getDate()`.
- **Recompute-on-write:** session totals are written to `work_sessions` columns whenever a break or meeting closes. Reports READ those columns and never recalculate.
- **Meeting time counts toward productive hours ONLY when `approvalStatus` is `APPROVED`.** Implemented as `productiveSeconds = elapsed − breaks` (approved meeting time stays inside it); `meetingSeconds` is a separate view of how much of that was meetings, never added on top. Sessions saved before 2026-10-28 used the old formula (meetings subtracted) and were not recomputed.
- **Cross-company actions are blocked by filtering on the actor's own `platformId`** (e.g. meeting invites skip anyone outside the manager's company). Exception by design: `OWNER_CODES` users can see and act across companies.
- **Service error codes must have an entry in the module's route `MESSAGES` map.** An unmapped code becomes a 500. Check this whenever you add a `throw new Error("X")`.
- **Dates from clients go through `parseIsoDay` (`src/lib/dates.ts`)** — strict `YYYY-MM-DD`, real calendar days only. Don't write a local `parseDay`.
- **Multi-tenancy:** `platformId` always comes from the JWT, never from a request body or query string. Every create injects it; every query filters on it.
- **Ownership checks** on every mutation that takes an id (IDOR guard).
- **All alert thresholds come from `TeamConfig`.** Nothing hardcoded.
- **Meetings are manager-initiated only.** The employee accepts, and the clock starts on acceptance. Employees cannot initiate.
- **Batch reads with `Promise.all` and join in memory.** No N+1.
- **Enum display names are mapped for the UI** (e.g. `MeetingType.TEAM` renders as "team meeting").

## UI theme (Hireism-style, applied project-wide 2026-10-28)

- **Palette lives in `frontend/app/globals.css`**, not in pages: primary `#5368ED`, background `#F1F2FC`, cards white, text `#1E293B`, secondary text `#718096`, borders `#E5E7F4`. The Tailwind `indigo-*` and `slate-900/500` colours are overridden in an `@theme` block, so `bg-indigo-600`, `text-slate-500` etc. already render in the brand palette. Change the brand there and nowhere else.
- **Cards:** `rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-[var(--shadow-card)]`, no border. Inputs/buttons use `rounded-xl`; borders are `border-indigo-100`. Primary buttons are `bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700`. Do not use `bg-slate-900` buttons or dark panels; hero panels are `bg-indigo-600` with white / `indigo-100` text.
- **Page width (big monitors, 2026-10-31):** do NOT cap a page with a fixed `max-w-3xl..7xl`. Use the shared `page-3xl/4xl/5xl/6xl/7xl` or `page-fluid` classes (tokens in `globals.css`, "PAGE WIDTH"). Below 1600 px they equal the old max-w sizes (laptops look unchanged); from 1600 / 1920 / 2400 px they grow and centre. Modals and forms keep their normal `max-w-*`. Verified at 1366, 1440, 1600, 1920 and 2560 px on 25 pages: laptops byte-identical, no sideways overflow.
- **Shell:** floating white sidebar + lavender canvas (`dashboard/layout.tsx`, tokens `--nav-*`). `--sidebar` (dark) is legacy; avoid it.
- **HR/Admin home:** `/dashboard` renders `HrDashboard.tsx` (parts in `dashboard/_hr/`, charts via `recharts`); other roles get `TodayView.tsx`. HR/Admin's own clock lives at `/dashboard/today`. The HR dashboard shows only Present/Absent (no lateness) and scopes every figure to the signed-in company.
- **Employee "Today" page (`TodayView.tsx`, redesigned 2026-10-28; parts in `dashboard/_timesheet/`):** header (welcome, role, company-timezone date/time, range preset Today/Yesterday/Last 7/Last 30/This Month/Custom ≤93 days; opens on TODAY every time, since 2026-11-02, where today is the company-timezone day from the server; it used to open on Last 7; there is no "This Week" option) → three cards: **Clock-in** (live timer = productive + break, Break menu / Resume / Clock-out), **Planned hours** (backend figure: productive hours × scheduled working days — the same as Reports; Details modal shows the real shift) and **Worked hours** (productive + break, break counted once) → **My timesheets** (newest-first day rows with a horizontal timeline: working / break / overtime / late, tooltips, click opens Entry details, Entry log button). Data comes from `GET /tracker/timesheet?from&to` (own data only, `timesheet.service.ts`; totals reconcile with `/reports`). Overtime = on-site time beyond `dailyWorkHours` (only when over by more than the overtime alert minutes); late = existing `wasLateLogin/lateByMinutes`. Nothing is invented: open/missing clock-out, no-activity, leave, holiday and day-off days are shown as such. Rules kept: **Pause = start a break, Resume = end it**; one daily break budget per team; no idle-time concept; clock-in takes an optional project (`work_sessions.teamId` → a team the person belongs to; migration `20261028120000_session_project`, hand-written because the DB has drifted from the migration files — `prisma migrate dev` wants to RESET it, never allow that; apply the SQL by hand / with `migrate deploy` after backing up). Shown as a chip on the timesheet day; invites/meetings banners unchanged. Buttons are enabled from session state and every handler re-checks it.
- **Add Employee + Import Employees** (HR/Admin; buttons on the HR dashboard and the Employees page; code in `dashboard/_employees/` and `backend/src/modules/admin/employeeImport.service.ts`, routes `/admin/employees/{lookups,template,parse,preview,commit}`). One pipeline serves the single form and the spreadsheet: **parse** (file read on the backend with `read-excel-file`; `.xlsx`/`.csv`) → **preview** (nothing written) → **commit** (re-validated, ONE transaction, all rows or none). Rules: IDs, names, dates and times are never changed or renumbered (duplicates are rejected, both rows); anything that needs interpreting (a time typed as `9.30`, a blank role/email/team/manager, an end time before the start, missing shifts) is **rejected until HR ticks that exact rule**, and each affected row says so; example/empty/note rows and rows above the heading are never imported; HR can only target their own company (owners — `OWNER_CODES` — any), and only an Admin can create HR/Admin roles. New accounts get a random one-time password (bcrypt-hashed, `mustChangePassword`), returned once in the commit response and never stored or logged. No-email people get a `<id>@<company-slug>.local` placeholder and sign in by employee ID. The HR workbook's yellow columns = required (Company, Full name, Employee ID, Work email, Designation, Team, Reports to name+ID, System role). Note: the sample template is CSV (the Excel library only reads).
- **Update Employees** (third button beside Add/Import; routes `/admin/employees/update/{preview,commit}`, service `employeeUpdate.service.ts`; same wizard with `mode="update"`): fills in details for people ALREADY in the system from the same sheet layout. People are matched by Employee ID only (names/IDs are never changed; a differing name is just noted). A blank cell = leave as is. An empty field is **filled**; a field that already has a different value is a **conflict** that is left alone unless HR ticks "overwrite" for that person + field (Employee role counts as "not set" for role). Same company isolation / HR-can't-grant-HR-or-Admin rules as Import; cycles are checked against the people as they would be after the update; commit is one transaction and writes a before/after `AuditLog` row per person (`path /api/admin/employees/update/applied`).
- **Leave requests** (`/leave` page for everyone; backend `modules/leave/`, routes `/api/leave/{mine,requests,requests/:id/cancel,requests/:id/decide,summary,person/:id}`): employee requests (Casual/Sick/Earned/Unpaid, full or half day, reason required, up to 30 days back, max 60 days, overlaps blocked) → PENDING → **HR/Admin of the SAME company** approves/rejects (never their own request; manager cannot decide). Approval runs in ONE transaction: status flips (only if still PENDING) and one `leave_days` row per working day is written (shift working days minus holidays, recomputed at approval) linked by `leaveRequestId`; from then on Reports/timesheet treat those days as leave, not absence. Pending/rejected/cancelled requests change nothing. An OWNER (`OWNER_CODES`) who is HR/Admin decides every company's requests (added 2026-10-31 so Sonali, CP-03, can handle Colab + Avatar India; off in a pinned deployment; the owner setting also reaches Admin, onboarding and payroll). HR sees pending requests with each person's leave already taken, and a per-person yearly table (Casual/Sick/Earned/Unpaid/Other, half day = 0.5, counted from `leave_days` so HR-marked days count too) with a drill-down. No yearly quota (count only). Tables: `leave_requests` + `leave_days.leaveRequestId`; enum `LeaveDayType` gained CASUAL/EARNED; migration `20261028130000_leave_requests` (hand-written/additive, apply to the live DB after a backup — see the session_project note).
- **Policies & induction** (`/policies` page for everyone, `modules/policies/`, routes `/api/policies/{,manage,:id/file,:id/acknowledge,:id/report,:id/archive,:id/restore}`; uses the existing `induction_documents` / `induction_acknowledgements` tables, no migration): HR/Admin upload a file (PDF/image/Word, 10 MB, stored privately via `saveUpload`, folder `induction`), for the whole company or one team, required or optional. Employees open it (records `openedAt`, file served `no-store`) and may only then click "I have read this" (`acknowledgedAt`). Replacing the file bumps `version`, so everyone must accept again; old acceptances stay as history. HR sees "x of y accepted" and a per-person report (accepted with date / opened / not opened). Archived documents vanish for employees. Not routed through the tracker gate, so locked joiners can read induction material. Not enforced: nothing blocks work until a policy is accepted.
- **Policy management, first delivery (built 2026-10-31; migration `20261031100000_policy_management`, NOT yet applied to any database):** extends the module above, it does not replace it. A policy is still an `induction_documents` row; new tables only (`policy_details` status DRAFT/PUBLISHED/ARCHIVED + category + effective date + download rule, `policy_versions` every version with its own file (old files are never deleted), `policy_audience` teams/employment types/roles (no rows = everyone), `policy_categories`, `policy_review_notes`). Acknowledgements stay in `induction_acknowledgements` per version number; a version published with "no re-confirmation" does not move the number people must have confirmed. Draft -> HR previews -> HR publishes (explicit confirm, and open review notes must be acknowledged) -> audience notified (notification inbox) + `policies:changed` signal on the SAME Socket.IO connection (`useRealtime`/`usePolicyRealtime`). Word files are shown by converting to sanitised HTML with `mammoth` (`policies.docs.ts`); the original stays downloadable. Files stay in `stored_blobs` (decision: no S3/R2 for now). On a database WITHOUT the new tables the old behaviour keeps working and new write actions return 503 `FEATURES_NOT_READY`. Imported HR policy: `scripts/import-policy.ts "<cp-policy.docx>" [--apply]` creates it as a DRAFT in Colab Platforms Limited with 7 HR review notes (blank company name, [3 Months], broken numbering, incomplete leave, attendance/deduction rules that differ from the app's 8h/4h thresholds, confidentiality wording, typos); the document is stored byte-for-byte and never edited. Managers cannot see acknowledgment status (HR/Admin only) until a permission rule exists. Tests: `tests/policy-docs.test.mts` (no DB), `tests/policies-v2.test.mjs` (branch only). NOT built yet: induction assignments/checklists (second delivery).
- **Notifications backbone (Phase 1 of Task Management/Approval/Notifications, built 2026-10-30; migration `20261101100000_notification_dedupe` NOT yet applied anywhere):** one inbox for the whole app. Backend: `notifyUsers()` (`lib/notify.ts`) stores an optional `dedupeKey` inside `notifications.data` and inserts with `skipDuplicates`; the unique expression index `("userId", data->>'dedupeKey')` (index only, no new column) makes repeats impossible (without the index it still works, just without the guarantee). A signal `notifications:changed` (no content) goes ONLY to the recipient's private Socket.IO room `inbox:<userId>` (`emitNotificationChange`, `lib/realtime.ts`), never to managers/HR who can see that person's tasks. `GET /api/notifications/page?filter=all|unread&before=&limit=` (cursor paging) feeds the notification centre. Task events (`modules/tasks/tasks.notify.ts`: assigned, proposed, submitted, proposal approved/returned, approved, not accepted) notify the assigner + `reportsToId` + each active team's manager, never the actor; failures are logged and never break the task action. Frontend: `NotificationsProvider` (`dashboard/_notifications/`) is the single source for the sidebar bell, the mobile-top-bar bell, popups (max 3, 8 s, only `POPUP_TYPES`, never for items that existed on first load) and `/dashboard/notifications` (All/Unread, load more, mark read/all). Browser push is NOT built (needs `web-push` + VAPID secrets + service worker; waiting for a decision). Test: `tests/realtime.test.mts` (26 checks, no DB). DB-backed task-notification tests still to be written/run on a disposable branch.
- **UI fixes 2026-10-30:** `.animate-in` uses fill-mode `backwards` (NOT `both`): a held `transform` made the page wrapper the containing block for every `position: fixed` child, so every modal was clipped to the page-content height. Never reintroduce a persistent transform/filter on a page-level wrapper. `/change-password` shows a cancel (X + button) only when the person opened it themselves (`mustChangePassword` false); forced changes stay mandatory. Meetings page: organizer "End meeting" (`POST /api/meetings/:id/end`, sets `endsAt = now` for a meeting happening now; cancel is for ones not started), Group / One-to-one toggle and a searchable people dropdown. My History: click a date to open that day's own tasks (`GET /api/tasks/day?date=`, read-only). Sidebar order: My Payslips before Tickets.
- **Late remark (built 2026-10-30; migration `20261101110000_late_remarks`, NOT yet applied to any database):** clocking in after the existing late cut-off (shift start + the team's `lateLoginGraceMinutes`, via `computeLate`; no new time rule) requires a 3–300 character reason. `POST /tracker/clock-in` without `lateRemark` answers 409 `LATE_REMARK_REQUIRED` (with `lateByMinutes`) and creates NOTHING; the Today page shows `LateRemarkModal` and sends the same clock-in again with the remark. Session + remark are written in one transaction into the NEW table `late_remarks` (one row per `work_sessions.id`; no column added to work_sessions). Resuming a same-day session never asks again. Until the table exists nobody is asked and every read returns no remark (verified read-only against production: `lateRemarksReady()` = false). Shown as `lateRemark` in `/tracker/timesheet`, `/tracker/history`, Live Team and Team Attendance (module `tracker/lateRemarks.ts`). `lib/api.ts` `apiPost` errors now carry `code` and `data`. The late time still counts; the remark only records why. Not covered: attendance corrections and Reports do not show/ask for a remark; no DB test written yet (needs a disposable branch).
- **Reports task counts (2026-10-30, no migration):** `GET /reports` now also returns `tasksCompleted`, `tasksPending`, `taskCompletion` per person, per period bucket and in totals; the Reports page shows them (Tasks done / Tasks pending / Completion, a "Tasks completed" tile, CSV columns). Counts are of TASKS, not weights. Only APPROVED, non-cancelled tasks count; completed = finished and accepted in full (same rule as the daily target: outcome null/COMPLETED); pending = approved but not fully done (open, partly done or sent back). The existing weighted "Task score" is unchanged (it still counts every task by weight). The attainment column/tile was relabelled "Productivity %" (still productive hours ÷ expected hours). Cross-checked against raw `daily_tasks` rows (read-only, production): match, but only 2 task rows were in scope, so it is a thin check.
- **Attendance report Excel export (2026-10-30; dependency `exceljs` added to the FRONTEND with the owner's approval, loaded only on click):** Reports > Attendance > Export > "Download Excel (.xlsx)" (`reports/_team/xlsx.ts`) next to the CSV; both use one table. Since 2026-11-02 the task columns run Total Tasks Planned Today, Tasks Approved Today, Pending Tasks, then "Tasks completed", then Remarks (same order in the CSV; the Pending formula finds its columns by name). Previously three columns sat right after "Tasks completed": **Total Tasks Planned Today** (that person's tasks for the day that are approved or waiting for approval; cancelled and rejected excluded), **Tasks Approved Today**, **Pending Tasks** (an Excel formula `=IF(P="","",P-O)`, planned minus completed). Counts come from `daily_tasks` in `getAttendanceReport` (`tasksPlanned`/`tasksApproved`, null = blank when the person had no tasks that day; nothing is assumed). "Tasks completed" is unchanged (approved + completed). Checked read-only against raw rows (match, but only 1 task row in scope) and by reading a generated file back (order, formulas, borders, frozen header). Not opened in desktop Excel yet.
- **Admin > Employees table (2026-10-30):** columns are Name, Code, Role / Designation, Team, Reports to, Status, Edit (Shift and Tracker columns removed from the LIST only; Shift and Tracker access are still editable in the Edit drawer). The Role / Designation column shows `designation` or "Not Assigned" (the system role stays in the Edit form as "System role" and in the cell tooltip); the Edit drawer has a designation box with suggestions (datalist, nothing auto-filled) and, for existing people, a Team / department editor that adds/removes memberships only when the admin presses Save (uses the existing `/admin/teams/:id/members` routes; removal is soft). Teams themselves are created in the Teams tab; none were created automatically. 7 dummy joiner accounts (Test Two/Three/Four/Six/seven/eight/Eight, codes AI-11/12/13, JAY-0002..0005, all INVITED, nothing attached) were deleted from production on 2026-10-30 with the owner's approval via `scripts/remove-test-accounts.ts` (dry run by default; strict code+name+status+email checks; backup JSON in `~/productivity-backups/`; users 70 -> 63).
- **Audit-driven fixes T1-T3 (2026-10-31; coded, NOT yet database-tested; staging branch still to be created by the owner; nothing deployed):** (T1) `/reports` now uses the SAME "Not tracked" rule as payroll: a working day on which nobody in the person's COMPANY clocked in (company-wide, not just the viewer's people) is `daysNotTracked`, owes no target and is not "Absent"; "Absent" = the company was tracking and this person did not clock in. (T2) Managers keep their attendance in `/reports` but are left out of the productivity figures (totals and period buckets; `totals.managersExcluded`); each person carries `teamName` (first team alphabetically) and the HR dashboard's Employee Performance table is grouped by team and has a Break column. The Reports page shows a "Not tracked" column. (T3) "Your team today" (`/dashboard/team`) and "Team Attendance" are ONE page, Attendance at `/dashboard/team-attendance` (old URL redirects): live board grouped by team with shift, current activity, clock-in, clock-out, break used/budget, meetings, working time, plus the alerts panel and meeting invites. `/team-attendance/live` rows gained `company, shiftName/Start/End, logoutAt, pendingInvite`. A break dash means no session today. Audit finding worth remembering: `audit_logs` rows are immutable, so retiring a duplicate person must NOT delete the account (the FK would null the audit attribution); mark it exited instead.
- **In-person meeting participation / offline stand-ups (built 2026-10-31; migration `20261101120000_meeting_participation`, NOT applied to any database; staging DB tests written but NOT yet run):** a scheduled meeting can be created as "In-person stand-up (track who joins)" (no link, agenda = description, "late after N minutes" set per meeting, default 5). Invited people press "I'm in the Meeting" then "Meeting Ended"; the SERVER stamps both times, a person can never send or edit their own times. SELF-REPORTED: it records what people declare, not physical presence. Completely separate from attendance, clock-in/out, breaks, working hours and productivity (nothing reads or writes `work_sessions`/breaks/`tracker_meetings`/tasks; a staging test asserts that). New tables only: `meeting_tracking` (per meeting, late threshold), `meeting_participation` (unique meeting+person: one join and one end per person; a return needs a manager correction), `meeting_participation_changes` (every correction: before/after/reason/who; rows only added). Code: `modules/meetings/participation.logic.ts` (pure rules), `participation.service.ts`, routes under `/api/meetings` (`POST /:id/join`, `POST /:id/leave`, `GET /:id/participation` organizer only, `GET /:id/participation-counts` HR/Admin counts only, `PATCH /:id/participation/:userId` organizer correction with a reason of 5+ chars, `GET /participation/history[?userId=]`). Rules: join window = 15 min before the start until the expected end, both inclusive; after that only the organizing manager can add someone; late = joined MORE than N min after the start; still-active-past-end is a FLAG, never auto-closed; "not joined" is never presumed present. HR/Admin see only counts (invited, joined), never names/times/durations (the oversight list and `/participation-counts`); details stay private to the organizer and invitees. Signal `meetings:changed` goes only to the private `inbox:` rooms of the organizer and the person concerned (not managers/HR who can merely see that person). A corrected person gets a `MEETING_PARTICIPATION_CORRECTED` notification and an Audit-log line. Before the migration is applied nobody can create a tracked meeting (503 `FEATURES_NOT_READY`) and every Meetings screen behaves exactly as before (verified read-only against production). Not built by decision: recurring weekday stand-ups, extra reminders. Tests: `tests/participation-logic.test.mts` (28, no DB), `tests/realtime.test.mts` (30), `tests/meeting-participation.test.cjs` (80 checks, disposable branch only).
- **Stand-up follow-ups (2026-10-31, built after the first real use; migration `20261101120000_meeting_participation` WAS applied to production on 2026-10-31 05:45 UTC by the owner, before the staging run):** (1) the dashboard (`TodayView`, so also HR's /dashboard/today) shows a stand-up card (`dashboard/_standup/StandupCard.tsx`): the join button once the window opens, a clear "You are in a meeting" state with the live timer, and your completed record; meeting invitations now also pop up on screen. (2) The ORGANIZER can mark himself in and out too (same buttons, same one-join rule). His record is shown apart in the live list ("you · organizer") and is NEVER counted in invited / joined / late (HR counts, the oversight list and `joinedCount` all exclude him); his own history includes meetings he organized. (3) An organizer can turn an ORDINARY meeting into an in-person one while it has not ended (`PATCH /meetings/:id` with `inPerson: true`; link removed, invitees told, cannot be switched off). Reason: on 2026-10-31 Ankit's two meetings ("Standup call", "new meeting") were created WITHOUT the In-person box, so no one had a join button. Tests: `tests/meeting-participation.test.cjs` now has 98 checks (staging only, still not run).
- **Dark theme, a per-person choice (built 2026-10-31; migration `20261101130000_user_preferences`, NOT applied to any database):** light stays the default for everyone; each person can switch to dark with the toggle (sidebar footer "Light/Dark theme" switch, a sun/moon icon on the mobile top bar). The choice is saved in the person's ACCOUNT (new table `user_preferences`, one row per person, theme LIGHT|DARK; `GET/PATCH /api/preferences`, own row only, any role, id from the sign-in never the body) so it follows them to every device, and is mirrored in this browser (`lib/theme.ts`, key `hrms-theme`) so the page paints in the right theme before anything loads (boot script in `app/layout.tsx`, no white flash). On load the account value decides (nothing saved = light, even if another person left dark in this browser); signing out clears the browser copy. Before the migration is applied `GET` answers `{ready:false}` and the toggle falls back to the browser only (a save returns 503 `FEATURES_NOT_READY`, verified read-only against production). HOW the dark colours work: ONLY the `html.dark` block at the end of `globals.css` (variables for the slate/indigo/status steps used as surfaces, borders and text, plus explicit rules for `bg-white`, the six `bg-[#...]` colours and the text steps that are also button fills such as `text-indigo-700`/`text-red-600`). The brand and red/green button fills (`bg-indigo-600` etc.) are deliberately NOT remapped so white button text stays readable. Charts read `--chart-*` variables (fallbacks = the original light colours). NEVER add dark-specific colours inside a page; change the `html.dark` block. When you add a new fixed colour that is not in that block, check it in dark. Checked in a real browser with the real components: the contrast scan found no text that is worse in dark than in light (7 low-contrast items in dark vs 48 in light in the sample, all existing light-theme choices such as `text-slate-400`). Not covered: printing (a browser print of a dark screen prints dark; payslip PDFs are generated by the server and are unaffected). Test: `tests/preferences.test.cjs` (17 checks, staging only, not yet run).
- **My performance, managers only (2026-10-31):** `/dashboard/my-performance` (sidebar "Me" group, `roles: ["MANAGER"]`; any other role opening the URL sees "This page is for managers"). A manager's OWN numbers for a period (This month / Last month / Last 7 / Last 30 / custom up to 93 days): productivity %, productive time vs expected, days present (leave, absent, not tracked), tasks done/pending/completion, late arrivals, breaks, in-person meeting record, and a day-by-day table. NO backend: it reads `GET /reports?userId=<self>` (so it matches Reports; the manager's own row is shown even though managers are left out of TEAM productivity totals), `GET /tracker/history`, and `GET /meetings/participation/history`. Cross-checked against Ankit's real data: the summary cards equal the day table (productive, break, days present, late). Display lives in `my-performance/_View.tsx` (data-free, so it can be checked with sample data). Own data only; nothing new is exposed.
- **Manager self-managed tasks (built 2026-10-31; migration `20261101140000_manager_self_tasks`, NOT applied to any database; staging test written, NOT yet run):** a MANAGER's own tasks never go to "Waiting for Approval": they are created APPROVED under his own name, marked by a row in the NEW table `task_self_details` (startedAt, optional deadline) with history in `task_events` (append-only, DB trigger refuses updates). No column was added to `daily_tasks`. Employees, HR and Admin keep the existing approval workflow untouched; approving/grading a self-managed task via the review routes answers 409 `SELF_MANAGED`, and they never appear in review queues or notifications. Manager screen (`tasks/_self/SelfManagedTasks.tsx`, shown when `GET /tasks` returns `selfManagedMode`): Not started / In progress / Completed / Overdue, Start, Mark as completed, Reopen, edit description/priority/deadline after approval, work updates, history (`GET /tasks/:id/history`, owner only), remove only while not started. Overdue = deadline day fully passed in the company timezone and not completed/cancelled; no deadline = never overdue. Time spent = start to completion, only while clocked in and not on break (`taskWorkSeconds`), null when a timestamp is missing; it is part of productive time, never extra. `GET /tasks/self/summary` (MANAGER only, up to 93 days) feeds My performance (own tasks, trends). His self tasks are EXCLUDED from team productivity (`productivity.service.ts`, `selfManagedTaskIdsOf`) but count in his own Reports row. Before the migration: nothing changes (`selfTasksReady()` false). Ankit's pending tasks are converted at deployment with `scripts/make-tasks-self-managed.ts AI-01` (dry run first, backup, one transaction, `--apply` only with the owner's go-ahead). Tests: `tests/self-task-logic.test.mts` (18, no DB), `tests/manager-self-tasks.test.cjs` (staging only).
- **Employee profile photos (built 2026-11-02, local only, NOT pushed; migration `20261102130000_user_photos` NOT applied anywhere; DB test `tests/photos.test.cjs` written, not run):** HR/Admin upload, replace and remove an employee's photo (Edit Employee drawer, My Profile for HR/Admin on their own record, and an optional picker in Add Employee that saves right after the person is created via the new `userId` in the commit response). Employees cannot change photos (decision: no HR-policy setting exists, so HR/Admin only). Storage reuses the private file store (`stored_blobs`, folder `photos`); the NEW table `user_photos` (one row per person; no column added to `users`, the old `users.avatarUrl` is NOT used because it would be a public link). Server rules (`modules/photos/`): real format from the file's first bytes (JPEG/PNG/WebP only; SVG/HTML/PDF refused whatever the name or claimed type), 2 MB limit (the browser shrinks to 512 px first), change = HR/Admin of a company they may act on (404 otherwise), view = the person, anyone in the same company, or HR/Admin with access to that company; served only through `GET /api/photos/:userId` with the sign-in token (`private` cache, nosniff), never a public address; replace/remove delete the old file; every change is audited. `photoAt` (last-set time, null = no photo) rides on `/auth/me`, `/auth/me/profile`, the Employees list, the Edit model and the live Attendance board; the shared `<Avatar>` (`dashboard/_photos/Avatar.tsx`) shows the photo or falls back to initials with no request. Shown in: sidebar profile, My Profile banner, Today header, HR dashboard header, Employees list, Edit drawer, live Attendance board. NOT yet using it: HR dashboard activity/performance lists, Huddle list, meeting lists (their data does not carry `photoAt` yet).
- **My Profile sections + sidebar (built 2026-11-02, local only, not pushed):** My Profile is a link in the sidebar header under the "Avatar HRMS" name, where the company name used to be (every role; it is NOT in the menu list). Documents is no longer a sidebar entry for employees/managers: a person's own document checklist is the **My Documents** tab of My Profile (`/dashboard/profile?tab=My Documents`), and `/dashboard/documents` still works by URL. HR/Admin keep ONE entry, **Employee Documents** (same page: tracking, review, requirements). The checklist (upload, replace, preview, verification status, HR remarks, rejection reasons) lives in ONE shared component `_documents/MyDocuments.tsx` used by both pages; the APIs (`/documents/mine`, `/onboarding/me/documents`) and permissions are unchanged. Profile tabs: Personal, Contact, Address, Emergency contact, Employment, My Documents; the first two and Employment read the new read-only `GET /api/auth/me/profile` (own record only, id from the token); Address and Emergency contact are NOT stored anywhere, so they say so (nothing invented; adding them needs a new table and your decision). The phone header shows only 'Avatar HRMS' (the company stays under the logo in the sidebar).
- **Complaint Centre / Query Desk (built 2026-11-02; migration `20261102120000_query_desk` NOT applied to any database; DB tests not run):** ONE sidebar entry per role replaces 'Corrections' and 'Tickets': employees see **Complaint Centre**, MANAGER/HR/ADMIN see **Query Desk** (`/dashboard/queries`; `/dashboard/tickets` redirects there; the corrections page stays reachable only for the approval screen and old notification links). Backend `modules/queries/` (`/api/queries`, `/categories`, `/counts`, `/:id`, `/:id/messages|request-info|status|assign`, attachment download) sits ON TOP of the existing tickets tables and the existing attendance-corrections module and copies nothing. Attendance corrections are only LISTED (kind CORRECTION, opened on their own screen, manager step then HR; never turned into tickets; raising one through `/api/queries` answers 400 `USE_CORRECTION_FORM`). New categories (Leave, Payroll, Profile and documents, Technical, General HR, Other) are stored under the old ticket category (so routing and due times keep working) plus a row in the NEW table `query_meta`; tickets with no row read their old category (HR_QUERY=General HR, TECH_ISSUE=Technical, ADMIN_REQUEST=Other). `query_events` = append-only history (created, assigned, status, information requested/received; DB trigger forbids edits). 'Waiting for employee' = information requested and not yet answered. Visibility (pure rules in `queries.logic.ts`, 31 tests): the person who raised it; HR/Admin for the companies they may act on (Phase 1 access); a MANAGER only for queries raised by his reporting team (not Payroll) or assigned to him; payroll is never assignable to a manager; an employee only their own; unseen = 404. Handling (reply, internal remark hidden from the employee, request information, status, assign) needs staff who did not raise it; resolving or rejecting needs a written reason; attachments use private storage folder `queries`. Notifications: raised -> assignee or HR/Admin; reply -> the other side; information requested / status / assigned -> the person. Sidebar badge = items waiting for you. Not covered: legacy ATTENDANCE_CORRECTION tickets can no longer be applied from the screen (their API route still exists); no DB test yet.
- **Team Huddle invitations + Attendance meeting time (built 2026-11-02; migration `20261102110000_meeting_responses` NOT applied to any database; scenario test `tests/huddle-attendance.test.cjs` RAN on 2026-11-02 against a throwaway LOCAL PostgreSQL (embedded-postgres kept OUTSIDE the repo, migrations applied with `migrate deploy`, set to UTC like Neon; `users.monthlySalary` had to be added by hand because no migration creates it): 70/70 pass; nothing deployed):** ROOT CAUSES found: (1) an invitation was only a `meeting_attendees` row with no answer field, so Accept/Decline could not exist, and Join/End buttons exist only for meetings that have a `meeting_tracking` row (created only when the manager ticked 'In-person'; 4 of Ankit's last 5 meetings were made without it, so nobody had buttons); (2) the Attendance board's Meetings column and the shift-breakdown 'huddle' figure read `work_sessions.meetingSeconds` = the OLDER live `tracker_meetings` feature, while Team Huddle participation (`meeting_participation`) is stored separately and was never read by those screens. FIXES: new table `meeting_responses` (one ACCEPTED/DECLINED row per invitee per meeting, changeable until they join or the meeting ends; `POST /api/meetings/:id/respond`, invitees only, audited, organizer notified `MEETING_RESPONDED`). Joining a huddle needs ACCEPTED (409 `NOT_ACCEPTED`; the organizer needs none; before the table exists nothing is gated). Status shown to the person and the organizer = pure `inviteStatus` (Invited/Accepted/Declined/Joined/Completed/Not joined; 'Not joined' only for tracked meetings once over). A MANAGER who invites only people who report to them with no meeting link and no explicit `inPerson` now gets tracking ON automatically (a link means an online meeting; `inPerson:false` is respected). Organizer list rows carry `response`/`status` and the summary has accepted/declined/awaiting counts; HR/Admin `participation-counts` returns aggregate invited/accepted/declined/joined only. Attendance: `huddleBreakdowns()` (participation.service) merges live approved meetings and actual huddle participation with the shared `computeBreakdown` (clocked-in only, outside breaks, overlaps once, not joined = 0); the live board and shift breakdown show it to every viewer for the people they may already see (managers, HR, Admin; changed 2026-11-02 by the owner: DURATIONS only, never join/leave clock times; the HR oversight list and `participation-counts` still give counts only); people with no huddle record keep exactly their previous figure; productive time is unchanged (huddle stays a breakdown of it). Screens: Accept/Decline + status chip on the dashboard card (`_standup/StandupCard.tsx`) and the Meetings page (`InviteResponse` in `meetings/_participation/Participation.tsx`), 'Accept first' message instead of Join. Tests: `tests/invite-status-logic.test.mts` (15, no DB); the older staging tests `meeting-participation`/`huddle-productive` now accept the invitation before joining.
- **Manager step for leave + attendance corrections (built 2026-10-31; migration `20261101150000_request_manager_approvals`, NOT applied to any database; staging test written, NOT yet run):** a request goes to the person's MANAGER first, HR/Admin decide afterwards. Who is asked: the person they report to plus the managers of their active teams, but only active people with the MANAGER role in the SAME company, never the requester (`approvals/managerStep.logic.ts` `pickApprovers`); none (or an HR/Admin boss) = no manager step, straight to HR exactly as before. One row per request in the NEW table `request_approvals` (kind LEAVE|CORRECTION, status PENDING|APPROVED|REJECTED|SKIPPED, approverIds, decidedBy, note; no column added to leave_requests/attendance_correction_requests; requests made before the migration have no row and go straight to HR). Rules enforced on the server: HR cannot approve while the step is PENDING (409 `WAITING_FOR_MANAGER`) unless it decides WITHOUT the manager with a written reason of 5+ characters (step becomes SKIPPED, reason kept and added to the audit line); a manager's REJECTION is FINAL (it closes the request as REJECTED with the manager as decider and a reason of 3+ characters; HR can never approve it, `MANAGER_REJECTED`); a manager approval only moves it to 'waiting for HR' (nothing in leave days or attendance changes until HR approves, same transactions as before); only one manager answer is ever recorded (`STEP_ALREADY_DECIDED`); a manager may answer only requests of the people who ask him (`NOT_FOUND` otherwise) and never his own. Notifications: submit -> the manager(s) (HR only when there is no step); manager approves -> HR/Admin (+ owner HR) and the employee; manager rejects -> the employee. Routes: `GET /api/leave/manager/requests`, `POST /api/leave/manager/requests/:id/decide`, `GET /api/attendance-corrections/manager/requests`, `POST /api/attendance-corrections/manager/requests/:id/decide` (MANAGER only; a manager can also open the evidence file of a request waiting for him). Screens: a 'Team requests' tab for managers on Leave and Corrections, a step line + badge ('Waiting for manager' / 'Manager approved · waiting for HR') for employees and HR, and 'Decide without the manager' for HR (`dashboard/_approvals/ManagerStep.tsx`). Before the migration every function degrades to no step (`approvalsReady()`), verified read-only against production. Tests: `tests/manager-step-logic.test.mts` (16, no DB), `tests/manager-step.test.cjs` (staging only).
- **Team Huddle in productive time (built 2026-10-31, NO migration; DB tests written, not run):** "Team Huddle" = the in-person stand-up feature (buttons are now "Join Huddle" / "End Participation"). KEY RULE: huddle time is a BREAKDOWN of productive time, never an addition, because productive time is already clocked-in time minus breaks (`recompute`), so a huddle inside clock-in is already counted. One shared pure function `tracker/productiveBreakdown.ts` (`computeBreakdown`) splits a day into Regular / Team Huddle / Other eligible (approved live meetings); regular+huddle+other always equals productive. Rules: count from the recorded join to the recorded leave; only inside the clocked-in period, outside breaks; overlapping huddles/meetings merged (never counted twice, the huddle gets the overlap); not joined = 0; never ended = counted only up to the huddle's expected end and flagged `openPastEnd` (the record stays open for a manager correction; nothing is auto-closed); the split is computed on read, never stored, so corrections update every screen. FINISHED days reconcile to the STORED productive total (older days, before 28 Oct, were saved with the earlier meetings-subtracted formula and are not recomputed; today's running day uses a fresh figure), with Regular as the remainder. Surfaces: `/tracker/timesheet` days carry `session.breakdown` and `totals.{regular,huddle,otherEligible}Seconds`; employee card `_timesheet/Breakdown.tsx` on the Today page (Regular, Team Huddle, Other eligible, Total, plus each huddle with scheduled/joined/left/counted and "not counted" reasons); manager: `GET /meetings/huddles/overview` (MANAGER only, his reporting employees only = `visibleUserIds`: teams he runs + direct reports, same company), page `/dashboard/huddles` + a compact section on his dashboard (`_standup/HuddleOverview.tsx`), sidebar "Team Huddle". A manager can only invite his OWN reporting employees to a huddle (`NOT_YOUR_REPORT` 403); HR/Admin unchanged (counts only, no names/times). Tests: `tests/productive-breakdown.test.mts` (39, no DB), `tests/huddle-productive.test.cjs` (26, staging only). Verified read-only against production: on 42 real sessions the parts always add up; 13 differ from the stored column, all explained (7 still running today, 6 saved with the old formula).
- **Team Huddle in Reports and My History (2026-10-31, owner's decision "show it as its own line"):** `/reports` now returns `huddleSeconds` per person, per period bucket and in totals (managers left out of bucket/total figures like the rest of productivity), and `/tracker/history` rows carry `huddleSeconds`; the Reports page has a "Team Huddle" column, a tile caption and a CSV column, My History shows "incl. Xm Team Huddle" under Productive. It is a LABEL inside productive time: no total changed. Shared batched calculation `huddleSecondsByDay` in `meetings/participation.service.ts` (same `computeBreakdown` rules). First real production use on 2026-10-31 08:02Z: Ankit's huddle "hh", Ritika joined and ended (31 s), Ankit joined (38 s, excluded from team totals as a manager); the report's 31 s matched.
- **Attendance Calendar (built 2026-11-02, NO migration, local only, not pushed):** `/dashboard/attendance-calendar` (its OWN sidebar entry: "Calendar" for MANAGER/HR/ADMIN right under Attendance, "My Calendar" for employees under Today; since 2026-11-03 it is no longer a tab inside the Attendance page, which is the live board only), API `GET /api/attendance-calendar?from&to&userId&teamId` (any signed-in role; default = current month, max 93 days), code `team-attendance/{arrival.logic,attendanceCalendar.service,attendanceCalendar.routes}.ts`. One row per person per day, built on the existing month engine (`getMonthAttendanceBulk`, the one Reports/payroll read), so working and break time and the clock-in/clock-out stamps are the stored values, untouched. STATUS shown = the owner's arrival rule on the FIRST check-in (`work_sessions.loginAt`, i.e. clock-in incl. HR-approved corrections; **no biometric data exists**: `biometric_punches` has no importer yet, switch the source there when it does) in the COMPANY timezone, to the minute: <= 09:45 Present, <= 10:15 Late, after = Half day (9:45 P, 9:46 L, 10:15 L, 10:16 H). Limits live in ONE constant `ARRIVAL_RULES` (a per-team setting would need a schema change; not approved). Days with no check-in come from the engine unchanged (Leave, Holiday, Week-off, Not tracked, Absent only when the company was tracking and the day is over); today with no check-in is "Not in yet", never Absent. **The calendar is a VIEW only: Reports, payroll and alerts keep their own late (shift + grace) and half-day (hours worked) rules, so the two can disagree for the same day; the day panel shows the payroll label too.** Who is listed: employee = self; manager = reporting people + self; HR/Admin = companies in `accessFor` (assigned companies included, unlike the live board which still uses `visibleUserIds`). Today's running session shows working time as last stored (labelled "so far"). **Employees** have the sidebar entry "My Calendar" (Main group, right under Today, EMPLOYEE role only; managers/HR/Admin use Attendance > Calendar). The day panel carries the remarks (added 2026-11-02, still no migration, batched reads that degrade to nothing if a table is missing): the late reason the employee typed (`late_remarks`), "Corrected by <HR>" (`attendance_correction_audits`), auto clock-out, leave type + half day + the reason written on the leave request, holiday name, tasks done of planned, and a plain `remarks` line; a colleague never sees another employee's late reason (employee = own rows only). Not shown: early departure (needs the shift end per person). Tests: `tests/arrival-logic.test.mts` (29, no DB), `tests/attendance-calendar.test.cjs` (43, run on a throwaway local PostgreSQL).
- **Team Huddle wording + four totals (2026-11-02, no migration, local only):** owner asked for a self-started "Team Huddle" button; DECIDED (owner) to keep huddles scheduled by a manager only, because a free self-start would let someone press Huddle instead of a break and dodge the break allowance, and it conflicts with "meetings are manager-initiated". Not built: a self-start button, a `TEAM_HUDDLE` break type (every `tracker_breaks` row is summed into break time, so a huddle there WOULD count as a break). Done instead: the active state reads "Team Huddle in Progress" and the button "End Huddle" (was "End Participation", also in the notification text); the employee breakdown card (`_timesheet/Breakdown.tsx`) opens with four tiles that add up: Productive work + Team Huddle + Break = Total time on site (productive work = regular + other eligible; on site = productive + break, the same definition TodayView uses); the manager Huddles table gained Break and Time on site columns (`onSiteSeconds`/`breakSeconds` on `/meetings/huddles/overview`, derived from clock-in to clock-out and the existing productive figure, nothing stored). Break calculations, recompute and payroll untouched. **Live huddle counter (same day):** `GET /tracker/session` also returns `huddle: { todaySeconds, activeSince, openPastEnd }` (`liveHuddle` in `participation.service.ts`, same `computeBreakdown` rules; a failure there returns null and never blocks the timer). The Clock-in card (TodayView) shows "Team Huddle in Progress" with a live counter while `activeSince` is set (plus "Team Huddle today" with the total afterwards) and the browser tab title shows it; TodayView reloads on the `meetings:changed` signal so joining/ending is instant. DISPLAY ONLY: the main timer is still productive + break and already runs through a huddle (a huddle is not a break), so no total changed. A huddle left open past its expected end is not shown as running. `tests/huddle-attendance.test.cjs` is now 84 checks (7b = the four totals, 4b = the live counter).
- **Every report screen opens on Today (2026-11-02, no migration, frontend only; nothing is restored from localStorage/sessionStorage/URL, a refresh resets it):** the Today dashboard (`TodayView`, all roles), Reports > Attendance report (already did), Reports > Task productivity (was Last 7 days; the server answers for today in the company timezone when no dates are sent), and the employee Reports page `SummaryReport` (was this month; gained Today and Yesterday buttons next to Last 7 days / This month / quarter / year, and still has From/To). Presets for the team reports (`reports/_team/ranges.ts`): Today, Yesterday, Day before, Last 5/7/30, This week (Monday to today), Last week, This month, Last month, Single date, Custom (max 93 days). The Today dashboard (`TodayView`) has NO "This week" preset (Today/Yesterday/Last 7/Last 30/This month/Custom). Shift breakdown opens on today's date already. No calculation changed.
- **ACTIVITY POLICY: Washroom is not a break (built 2026-11-03, NO migration, local only, not pushed; supersedes every earlier "break" total that included Washroom):** ONE classifier, `tracker/activityClass.ts`: TEA, LUNCH, PERSONAL, OTHER = BREAK (counted in break time, use the daily break allowance, break reports, BREAK_OVERAGE); WASHROOM = NEUTRAL (own figure, NOT break, NOT productive, never touches the allowance); Team Huddle/meetings = productive (not break rows). Washroom rows stay in `tracker_breaks` with `breakType = WASHROOM` (no new enum/table/column). Because every away row still comes out of the day, **productive time and time on site did not change for any day**: productive = clocked in - break - washroom (= clocked in - every away row); time on site = productive + break + washroom. What changed: `recompute()` writes `breakSeconds` = counted breaks only; every reader (timesheet, My History, Reports day/week/month/year + CSV, Attendance report/Excel, calendar, live board, shift breakdown, live team, HR/manager huddle overview, alert job) derives Break and Washroom from the break ROWS via `awayOf()`/`splitAwayTime()` (washroom has no stored column; fallback to the stored column only when a session has no rows). **Decision (owner): history is NOT rewritten**: older sessions still have the old `breakSeconds` (washroom inside break) stored, and are displayed correctly only because readers recompute from rows; anything reading the raw column directly (correction snapshots, SQL) still sees the old figure for old days. A repeatable dry-run-first script to fix stored columns was offered and declined; write it if raw readers ever matter. New API fields: `washroomSeconds` (session, timesheet day + totals, history row + `washroomCount` + `summary.totalWashroom`, reports person/bucket/totals, live board, shift breakdown, huddle overview), `washroomHours` (Attendance report/engine). The five on-site sums (timesheet elapsed, scheduler overtime, TodayView, Breakdown, huddle overview) now add washroom: NEVER compute on-site as productive + break again. UI: activity menu heading "Select activity" (Tea, Washroom, Lunch, Personal, Meeting / Team Huddle); the Meeting / Team Huddle item was first a shortcut to JOIN a scheduled huddle; **SUPERSEDED 2026-11-03**, it now starts an unscheduled DISCUSSION (see the next note); scheduled huddles are joined only from the huddle card / Meetings page; Washroom shows its own counter, tile, column, timeline colour and legend, and the productive clock pauses during it; the live Attendance board has a Washroom tile/column and its "On break" count excludes washroom. Tests: `tests/activity-class.test.mts` (18, no DB), `tests/activity-policy.test.cjs` (42, local DB: break/washroom per type, remaining allowance, old-style stored day read correctly and not rewritten, live start/end + refresh + re-login, all screens equal); `huddle-attendance` (84) and `attendance-calendar` (43) still pass.
- **"Meeting / Team Huddle" in the activity menu = an unscheduled DISCUSSION (built 2026-11-03, NO migration, local only, not pushed):** for a talk with a manager or colleague that is not a scheduled huddle. Picking it opens a popup "Why are you taking this time?" (remark required, 3-300 characters, trimmed; nothing starts until Confirm). Server: `POST /tracker/break/start { breakType: "DISCUSSION", remark }` (`REMARK_REQUIRED`/`REMARK_TOO_LONG` in the route MESSAGES) saves ONE row in the existing `tracker_breaks` table: type `OTHER`, `reason = "Discussion: <remark>"`, start stamped by the server, end and duration from the existing end route. No enum change, no new table, and NO meeting, participation record, invitation or notification is created (tested). Classified in `tracker/activityClass.ts` as PRODUCTIVE (owner's choice): NOT break time, does not use the break allowance, and nothing subtracts it: `recompute` still does productive = elapsed - break - washroom, so a discussion stays inside productive time (time on site = productive + break + washroom, discussion never added again). Everything that passes rows into `computeBreakdown` or treats an open row as time away uses `awayRows()` so a discussion is never subtracted (timesheet, huddle code, Attendance board). The system's own OTHER row ("Away between clock-out and clock-in") is still a break because only the `Discussion: ` prefix marks a discussion. Visible: employee Today card ("Discussion in progress/today" counter, state "IN A DISCUSSION", button "End discussion", tab title), entry log (remark + time), timesheet/history figures (`discussionSeconds`, `discussionCount`); manager and HR see remark + duration in the Attendance Calendar day panel (same people they already see; a colleague and another company see nothing); the live board shows the person as "In meeting". Not shown as its own Reports column (it sits inside productive). Menu item is disabled only while the person is in a scheduled huddle. Because it is productive time chosen by the employee, a manager can read the remark but nothing limits how long it runs; if abuse appears, add a length flag, not a deduction. Tests: `tests/activity-class.test.mts` (32), `tests/activity-policy.test.cjs` (81, section 5/5b = this feature).
- **Task Revert / Reopen (built 2026-11-03, NO migration, local, NOT committed or pushed on the owner's instruction):** a manager (or higher) sends a Completed task back to In Progress with a REQUIRED remark (3-300 characters) and one of two kinds: `PARTIAL` = "Partially completed", `NOT_FULL` = "Not fully completed" (both behave the same: back to In Progress, the kind and remark are shown to the employee). `POST /api/tasks/review/:id/revert { type, remark }` -> `tasks.service.ts revertTask`, pure rules in `tasks.revert.logic.ts`. Who: `assertCanReviewTask` (the task's team manager/co-manager, the person it reports to, HR/Admin of the company), never the task's own owner (403), employees get 403, another company 403/404; a manager's own self-managed task is excluded (`SELF_MANAGED`). Only an approved task whose status is COMPLETED (graded Completed, Partial, Failed or not yet graded); one transaction with the state check inside the write (`updateMany where status COMPLETED`), so a second or simultaneous press answers 409 `ALREADY_REOPENED`; after the employee finishes it again it can be reverted again (a new cycle). What changes on `daily_tasks`: status IN_PROGRESS, `outcome` and `completedAt` cleared (so it stops counting as completed in Reports, the Excel export, productivity and the hourly chart, and counts as pending), `reviewedById/At` = the reverter, `reviewNote` = a marker + the remark (`[Reopened: partial] ...` / `[Reopened: not full] ...`, decoded by `decodeRevertNote`, so the latest revert shows on the task with no new column). Kept untouched: title, weight, approval, assigner, the employee's submission note (`completionNote`), the row itself (never deleted). **Permanent history = the immutable audit log** (module Tasks, summary `Reopened task ...`, before = previous status, original grade and who graded it, original `completedAt`, grade note and submission note; after = new status, kind, remark, manager id and name, employee id, time); the earlier grade note on the task row is overwritten by the marker but survives in that audit row. Only HR/Admin can browse the audit log: managers and employees get NO step-by-step timeline screen (a `task_status_history` table would need a migration; not approved). Employee: notification `TASK_REVERTED` (a popup type) with the kind and remark; the task is back in Today's work with a violet banner "Reopened by your manager - <kind>: <remark>"; a reopened task from an EARLIER day (the employee list is otherwise today-only, the schema's carry-forward is not implemented) is added to the working list flagged `fromEarlierDay` and does NOT change today's score or task list. Manager: `Revert / Reopen` button on every row of the Completed tab on Approvals (popup `RevertModal`: two options + remark + Cancel/Confirm), reopened tasks show their remark in the In progress tab, a task the employee finished again shows `resubmitted` in the Completed tab. Employees' own existing "Reopen" (before grading) is unchanged. Reports/Excel needed no change (they read status/outcome). Tests: `tests/task-revert-logic.test.mts` (20, no DB), `tests/task-revert.test.cjs` (60, local DB).
- **Fix: End Huddle vanished after the scheduled end (2026-11-03, NO migration, local, NOT committed or pushed):** found on 2026-11-03 when a manager's table showed one person "In the huddle / not ended" with 32 min counted (the cap at the expected end) after the huddle was over. Cause: `GET /meetings?scope=upcoming` returned only `endsAt >= now`, the dashboard card hid `state === "past"`, and the Meetings page showed the read-only `PastRecord` for past meetings, so once the scheduled end passed a person still marked in had NO End button at all. Fix: `listMine` also returns, in `upcoming`, the meetings the person joined and has not ended (`openMeetingIds`, recent 2 days); the StandupCard and the Meetings page show `JoinControl` (End Huddle) for a past meeting while `me.state === IN_MEETING`; `GET /tracker/session` huddle block gained `openMeetingId` (even past the end) and the Clock-in card shows an "End Huddle" button for it (with a note when past the scheduled end). `leave()` itself never had a time window, so a late End records the server time of the press. This only PREVENTS new cases: records already left open stay open until the ORGANIZER uses Team Huddle > the person's row > Correct (set the left time, reason 5+ characters; the correction is kept as history and the person is told). Still true by earlier decision: an open record is never auto-closed; it is counted only to the expected end and flagged. Test: `tests/huddle-attendance.test.cjs` section 8b (now 96 checks).
- **Late Remark in the Attendance report and My History (built 2026-11-03, NO migration, local, NOT committed or pushed):** a "Late Remark" column in Reports > Attendance report (table, CSV and Excel share one table; in Excel it sits right after "Early departure", width 46, wrapped; every other column, the Pending Tasks formula, header colours, filters and frozen header are unchanged) and a Late remark line/CSV column in My History. API: `GET /team-attendance/attendance` rows gained `arrival` (ON_TIME | LATE | HALF_DAY | ABSENT | LEAVE | HOLIDAY | WEEK_OFF | NOT_TRACKED | NOT_CLOCKED_IN), `arrivalMinutes` (minutes after 09:45), `arrivalLimit`, `lateReason`, `correctedBy`, and a new `arrival` query filter (`ON_TIME`, `LATE`, `HALF_DAY`, `LATE_OR_HALF`, `ABSENT`; bad value = 400 `BAD_ARRIVAL`) shown as the "Late remark" dropdown ("Late and Half Day" first). `/tracker/history` rows gained `arrival` and `arrivalMinutes`. The rule is the Attendance Calendar's (`arrival.logic.ts`: <= 09:45 on time, <= 10:15 Late, after = Half Day, company time, to the minute; Absent only after the day is over and only where the existing engine says absent, today = "Not in yet"); it is a VIEW ONLY: the existing Status and Late arrival columns keep their own (shift + grace, hours-worked) rules, so a row can show Status Present with Late Remark Late. First check-in = the clock-in time (`work_sessions.loginAt`; still no biometric data). Text built by `reports/_team/lateRemark.ts`: label, check-in, late duration, "Reason: <what the person typed at clock-in>" or exactly "No remark provided" (never invented), plus "Corrected by <HR>" when an HR correction was applied; HR remarks other than corrections are not stored anywhere. Permissions: managers = people who report to them; employees cannot open the team report (403) and read their own in My History / the Calendar; **HR/Admin now see every company they are authorized for in this report** (company-access rule `accessFor`; before, only their own company), which is the one behaviour change to the existing report. Tests: `tests/late-remark-report.test.cjs` (34, local DB), `tests/late-remark-text.test.mts` (10); the generated Excel was read back (14 checks).
- **Fix: remark textarea ignored typing (2026-11-03, frontend only, no database, local, NOT committed or pushed):** the "Why are you taking this time?" box (and every other `Modal`) showed a cursor but took no text. ROOT CAUSE (reproduced in a simulated browser: only 1 of 23 typed characters arrived): the shared `_employees/Modal.tsx` ran an effect that depended on `onClose` and called `panel.focus()`. The Today page re-renders every second (the live timer tick) and hands the modal a NEW inline `onCancel` each time, so once a second the effect re-ran and moved keyboard focus from the textarea to the dialog box. The same applied to the late-remark box at clock-in and the Revert / Reopen box, and to any `Modal` with an input opened from a page that re-renders on a timer (18 screens use `Modal`). The textarea, its state and its `onChange` were correct. Fix in `Modal.tsx`: the newest `onClose` is read from a ref, the effect depends only on `dismissible`, focus is moved into the dialog once on open and never stolen from a field that already has it (an `autoFocus` input was also being stolen from right after it opened). RULE: never put a callback prop in the dependency list of an effect that moves focus, locks scroll or adds listeners; keep it in a ref. Also: the three remark modals (`DiscussionModal`, `LateRemarkModal`, `RevertModal`) now show the typed length (not the trimmed length) in the counter, validation is unchanged (3-300 trimmed characters, `maxLength` 300), and the textarea wraps long words (`break-words`, `overflow-wrap:anywhere`, `resize-y`). Verified with jsdom harnesses kept outside the repo (jsdom is not a project dependency; adding a frontend test framework is a dependency decision for the owner): focus + typing under a ticking parent, then the real `DiscussionModal` (21 checks: typing, paste, delete, edit, 300 and 301 characters, spaces only, Confirm sends the trimmed text once, Escape still closes). Not tested in a real browser or on a phone; real-keyboard behaviour is the same focus rule.
- **Team Huddle and Meetings are ONE section named "Team Huddle" (built 2026-11-03, FRONTEND ONLY, no backend/schema change, no migration, local, NOT committed or pushed):** there never were two systems: a Team Huddle is a meeting with participation tracking (`meeting_tracking`), same tables, same `/api/meetings` routes, same `meetings:changed` signal. The sidebar now has ONE item, "Team Huddle" (`/dashboard/meetings`, every role: Employee, Manager, HR, Admin); the separate manager-only Team Huddle item and the "Meetings" label are gone. The page keeps the address `/dashboard/meetings` because notifications, emails and `?id=` links already point there. Tabs (`meetings/tabs.ts`, pure rules, tested): Upcoming (not started), Ongoing (happening now, PLUS a meeting past its scheduled end while you are still marked in it, so End stays reachable), Invitations (unanswered invites that are not yours and not over), History (Completed / Cancelled filter, past meetings show actual joined/left/duration), and by role Participation (MANAGER: the old Team Huddle overview `HuddleOverview`: today's huddles, who joined, productive-time split per person) or Company overview (HR/Admin, counts only, unchanged). Upcoming, Ongoing and Invitations are filters over the "upcoming" list the server already sends; counts are derived from it. `?tab=` opens a tab if the role has it (otherwise the default). `/dashboard/huddles` is now a redirect to `/dashboard/meetings?tab=participation`; the dashboard link "Open Team Huddle" points there too. "New meeting" button renamed "Schedule meeting"; buttons keep "Join Huddle" / "End Huddle" wording (the owner had asked for those earlier; the latest request listed "Join Meeting" / "End Participation", not applied, ask if wanted). "Team Huddle" is also still the name of the TIME CATEGORY in Attendance, the breakdown card and Reports/Excel (renaming that would change report headers; not done). The two invitation messages now say "Team Huddle" instead of "Meetings". Attendance meeting time, productive-time maths and records are untouched. Tests: `tests/huddle-section.test.mts` (21: tab rules, who gets which tab, ?tab= handling, the redirect, the single sidebar entry); `huddle-attendance` (96), `attendance-calendar` (43) and `late-remark-report` (34) still pass. The page itself was not opened in a real browser. **Open huddle records (2026-11-03):** in a real case a person was left "In the huddle / not ended" (counted only to the scheduled end) and the Audit Log had NO "Declared leaving" line for them, i.e. the server never accepted an End from them (only successful requests are audited; the only writers of a participation record are join, leave and the organizer's Correct). The Participation tab could show "not ended" but offered no way to fix it, so unended rows now carry a "Correct" link to that meeting's details (`?tab=participation&id=<meeting>`), where the organizer's audited correction (left time, reason 5+ characters, kept as history, the person is told) already exists. HR/Admin cannot correct (organizer only, by design). Not built, because it needs a migration (the `endedBy` column is CHECK-limited to SELF/MANAGER and recording a system end honestly needs a new value): closing an open huddle automatically at clock-out. Until then an open record is flagged and counted only to the expected end.
- **Admin > People toolbar is one row (2026-11-03, frontend only, local, NOT committed or pushed):** search, Company and Role filters and the people count on the LEFT, Add / Import / Update Employees on the RIGHT, every control 40px tall (`h-10`), centred. Code: the toolbar in `admin/page.tsx` (left group `flex-[1_1_31rem]`, search `flex-1 min-w-[8rem] max-w-sm`, Company `w-40`, Role `w-28`, both truncate) and the shared `_employees/EmployeeActions.tsx` (also used on the HR dashboard: buttons now `h-10 px-3 whitespace-nowrap`). The actions are `ml-auto shrink-0`, so when the page is too narrow the buttons drop to a second row AS A GROUP, right-aligned, and the filters never wrap one by one. Measured in headless Chrome with the app's compiled CSS: toolbar width = viewport - 336px (256px sidebar + 16px shell padding + 64px page padding); ONE row from a toolbar width of about 1030px (a 1366px screen; the search shrinks to ~170px there and its placeholder is clipped), two clean rows below (a 1280px screen); no overlap, nothing outside the container, all heights 40px, buttons right-aligned, at 520/700/944/1030/1104/1200/1584px with and without the Company filter; dark theme rendered and checked. No filter, search, import or permission logic changed. Not seen in the real app (the measuring used a copy of the toolbar markup with the real stylesheet).
- **Team Tasks (Approvals page) employee picker (2026-11-03, frontend only, no backend/database change, local, NOT committed or pushed):** the row of employee name chips is replaced by ONE searchable dropdown, "Select Employee" (`approvals/EmployeePicker.tsx`, rules in the pure `approvals/people.ts`). Default "Everyone" (counts every task in view). It lists the WHOLE reporting team from the server roster (`queue.members`: a manager's reporting people; HR/Admin their company), alphabetically, each with name, employee code and task count in the dates being viewed (0 when none; the old chips hid people without tasks); two people with the same name are told apart by code and are never merged (selection is by id). Search by name or code; no match shows a message. Choosing a person filters the five status tabs and their counts and the list exactly as the chips did (same `person` state, same `bucketOf`, same rows, so the tabs add up to the person's count); Everyone shows the whole team; the dates filter, the live Socket.IO refresh and every task action (grade, approve, Revert / Reopen) are unchanged and keep the selected person. Accessibility: standard combobox + listbox roles (`aria-expanded`, `aria-activedescendant`, `aria-selected`, a polite match count); Enter / Space / Down opens, type to search, Up / Down / Home / End move, Enter chooses, Esc closes and returns focus to the button, Tab closes; click outside closes. Responsive: full width on a phone, 20rem (list 22rem) from `sm`. No change to task assignment, approval, completion or productivity logic or to permissions. Tests: `tests/team-tasks-picker.test.mts` (21, rules); 13 checks in real Chrome against an isolated local stack (chips gone, default Everyone, tabs add up, whole team listed incl. 0-task people, search by name and code, duplicate names, keyboard path, aria, mouse + date filter keeps the person, Revert button still there, live update 3->4 without reload, no JS errors, phone width) plus HR/Admin/employee role checks and light/dark/phone screenshots.
- **Application name is "Avatar HRMS"** (login, sidebar, mobile header, browser tab `<Page> | Avatar HRMS`, metadata, outgoing emails). The HR/Admin menu item "Productivity Tracker" is a feature name (their own clock) and stays.
- **Deployment pin (`HRMS_COMPANY_SLUG` in `backend/.env`, e.g. `avatar-india`; `src/lib/deployment.ts`)**: unset = every company works as before. Set = this deployment serves ONE company: only its people can sign in (others get the normal wrong-password error) and use the API (403 `NOT_PART_OF_THIS_HRMS` even with a valid token, checked in `requireAuth`); owner (`OWNER_CODES`) and Admin cross-company reach is off (`viewerIsOwner`, projects `visiblePlatformIds`, holidays `allowedPlatforms`); manager visibility (`visibleUserIds`, reports scope, task queue, `assertCanReviewTask/assertCanManage`, projects) drops other-company people even where a reporting line or team crosses companies; admin manager dropdowns, onboarding SLA/reminder emails and the stale-session/alert schedulers touch only that company. A slug that matches nothing returns 503 for everyone (fail closed). Other companies and their rows stay in the database. **Before setting it, make sure the pinned company has an HR or Admin user** (Avatar India had none on 2026-10-28; the AVT-0001 Admin belongs to the `avatar` company and would be locked out). Edit `.env` in the editor only.
- **Companies (HR/Admin only)** (`/dashboard/companies`, `modules/companies/`, routes `GET/POST /api/companies`, `POST /api/companies/:id`; no migration): list every company (names/status), create (HR and Admin), edit (HR: own company only; Admin: any). Employee counts shown for the viewer's own company only. Nobody can deactivate their own or the pinned company. A deactivated company is closed to its people (`COMPANY_INACTIVE`, checked in `requireAuth`). The slug never changes (it is how a deployment is pinned). Managers/Employees get 403 from the API and no menu item.
- **Role hierarchy is enforced in `admin.service.ts` (found by the 2026-10-29 audit):** only an ADMIN may create, promote, suspend, or reset the password of an HR or Admin account; HR cannot change their own role/status; HR is only offered the EMPLOYEE/MANAGER roles. **Every `:id`/`:userId`/`:teamId` route parameter must be a UUID** (`guardUuidParams(router)` in `src/lib/params.ts`, called right after `Router()`), otherwise the answer is 404, not a database 500. Router `fail()` helpers map Prisma/runtime input errors to 400 (`isBadInput`) and never echo raw error text to the browser. A global `textFields` middleware rejects non-text values in known text fields with a 400. Client dates always go through `parseIsoDay`; client clock times through a validated parse. **Backend tests live in `backend/tests/` (see its README): they write data, run them ONLY on a disposable Neon branch (`_guard.cjs` refuses the `.env` database).**
- **Attendance corrections + notification inbox (built 2026-10-29; migration `20261029100000_attendance_corrections`, NOT yet applied to the live DB — apply after a backup, then record it in `_prisma_migrations`):** tables `attendance_correction_requests` (one PENDING per person per day, enforced by a partial unique index), `attendance_correction_audits` (before/after session snapshot, `actedById` RESTRICT so an HR user with audit history cannot be deleted), `notifications`. Employee asks (`POST /api/attendance-corrections`, times typed as HH:MM in the COMPANY timezone, last 30 days, reason required, optional evidence file, own requests only); HR/Admin of the same company decides (`/decide`; never their own; managers cannot; reject needs a comment). Nothing changes on submit. Approval runs in ONE transaction: claim the request (409 if already decided) -> lock the day's session -> re-validate against the session as it is now -> update/create the session -> `recompute` (same function as clock-out) + `computeLate` (same formula as clock-in, shift start snapshot + current TeamConfig grace; NOTHING is waived, a corrected time still past the grace stays late) -> delete stale NO_LOGIN/LATE_LOGIN alerts -> audit row. A correction that no longer fits the recorded breaks, or closes today's running session, is refused. Reports, History, Timesheet and CSV exports read the recomputed columns, so they follow automatically; the Timesheet shows a "Corrected by HR" chip (`/tracker/timesheet` reads the audit table inside a guard so the page still loads if the table is missing). Notifications: HR/Admin get an in-app notification (+ best-effort email through the existing mailer) on submit, the employee on the decision; bell in the sidebar (`_notifications/Bell.tsx`, polls `/api/notifications` every 30 s), pending-count badge on the HR "Corrections" link and a row in the HR dashboard's Pending HR Approvals. Pages: `/dashboard/corrections` (everyone: My requests; HR/Admin: Pending/Approved/Rejected with search, date and team filters, side-by-side details, confirmations, audit history). The old ATTENDANCE_CORRECTION ticket path is kept unchanged by decision (manager-assigned, no audit snapshot, no late recompute). Half/full-day status is NOT implemented (no such rule exists in the repo; the live DB has unexplained `halfDayAfterHours`/`fullDayAfterHours` columns). Tests: `backend/tests/attendance-corrections.test.cjs` (93 checks).
- **Audit log screen, document requirements, meeting links (built 2026-10-29). Migrations NOT yet applied to the live DB** (order: `20261029110000_audit_log_structured` → `scripts/backfill-audit.ts --apply` (dry-run first) → `20261029111000_audit_log_immutable`; `20261029120000_employment_type_and_requirement_scope`; `20261029130000_meetings`; plus `20261029100000_attendance_corrections`). Every migration has a `down.sql`. **RULE LEARNED THE HARD WAY: the live backend runs `tsx watch`, so new code is live the moment it is saved. Never add a column to an EXISTING table in the Prisma schema before the live DB has it (every query on that table reads all columns and fails). New features use NEW tables only, and code that reads them must tolerate "table does not exist" until the migration is applied** (see `scopeMap`/`typesOf` in `onboarding.service.ts`, the audit fallback in `middlewares/audit.ts`, the guarded corrections read in `timesheet.service.ts`).
  - **Audit log** (`/dashboard/audit-logs`, HR/Admin of their own company, read-only `GET /api/audit-logs[/filters|/:id]`; no write route exists): built on the existing `audit_logs` table + middleware. New columns (module, action, recordType, recordId, targetUserId, summary, before, after) are filled automatically from the route catalogue `lib/auditCatalog.ts` (add a line there for every new write route) and enriched by services via `auditNote()/changedFields()` (`lib/auditContext.ts`, AsyncLocalStorage; one row per request).
