Before/after captured for employee changes (role/status/access = action PERMISSION), password resets (value never recorded), sign-ins, leave decisions, attendance corrections, document review and requirements, company edits, meetings. Secrets are scrubbed on write AND on read. The IP address is shown to Admin only. A database trigger refuses UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE of audit rows (only the FK `SET NULL` clearing of a deleted user/company is allowed). Search finds what a person did AND what was done to them. Notification "mark read" and import previews are not logged.
  - **Document requirements** (`/dashboard/documents`: everyone = My documents; HR/Admin = Employee tracking + Requirements): reuses `document_requirements`/`employee_documents` and the existing upload/review routes (`/onboarding/me/documents`, `/onboarding/documents/:id/review`, `/onboarding/requirements`). Statuses shown as Not submitted / Submitted / Verified / Rejected (maps REQUESTED/SUBMITTED/APPROVED/REJECTED). Optional **employment type** per employee (`employee_profiles`) and per-requirement scope (`document_requirement_scopes`): no row = everyone / unspecified = sees every requirement. Scoping is applied in `recomputeOnboarding` and the joiner checklists too. Deactivating a requirement never deletes files (listed as "no longer required"). The starter list that `ensureRequirements` seeds for a company with none is kept (editable). Employment type is set from the HR tracking dialog only (not in Add/Import/Update Employees yet).
  - **Meeting links** (`/dashboard/meetings`, `/api/meetings`): tables `meetings`, `meeting_attendees`; separate from `tracker_meetings` (live worked-time meetings). Manager/HR/Admin create; the organizer edits; the organizer or HR/Admin cancel. Details (description, link, invitees) are visible to organizer + invitees ONLY, even HR/Admin; HR/Admin get `/api/meetings/oversight` (title, organizer, time, head-count). Pasted link must be https, no credentials, no localhost/IP/single-word host; provider recognised (Google Meet / Teams / Zoom / Other); NO provider integration, nothing is auto-created. Invitations/changes/cancellations go to the notification inbox (+ best-effort email). Times are typed in the company timezone and stored as UTC.
  - **Idle-time tracking is NOT built** (on hold pending company policy and an employee notice). Agreed design when it is: opt-in per team, employee acceptance first, per-minute yes/no "interaction inside an HRMS tab" only, idle = no interaction for at least a threshold while clocked in and not on break/meeting, untracked minutes are never idle, never subtracted from worked hours, HR sees summaries only.
- `GET /reports` accepts an optional `userId` that can only narrow the viewer's already-permitted people.
- Existing pages (Live Team, Reports, History) still show late-login information; only the new HR dashboard omits it.

## Auth

- bcrypt cost 10 (via `bcryptjs`).
- JWT HS256 carrying `{ userId, platformId, role }`. Bearer header, stateless.
- `requireAuth` and `requireRole` middleware (`src/middlewares/auth.ts`). `requireAuth` also reads the live user row on every request: SUSPENDED/EXITED users get 401 immediately, and `role`/`platformId` come from the database, not the token (a demotion applies on the next request).
- Login accepts an email or an employee code. The same email can exist on several platforms, so email login tries every account with that email and signs in the one whose password matches.
- Login is rate-limited (`loginLimiter`, 10 attempts / 15 min per IP + identifier, cleared on success). The key reads the first `X-Forwarded-For` value, so it is only trustworthy behind a proxy that overwrites that header.
- Login returns the same error for unknown email and wrong password (prevents email enumeration).
- `publicUser()` never returns `passwordHash`.

## Built and working

Login with roles · dashboard shell with role-filtered sidebar · clock in/out · tagged breaks · live three-counter timer · shift-based late detection · manager Live Team view with alerts panel · manager→employee meeting invites with accept/decline · weighted Daily Tasks with carry-forward · My History with CSV export · stale-session scheduler every 15 min · alert generation (`LATE_LOGIN`, `BREAK_OVERAGE`, `OVERTIME`, `LOW_PRODUCTIVITY`, `NO_LOGIN`) via idempotent upsert · Reports (person/day/week/month/year with CSV export).

Also present in the backend (modules exist): **Admin** (users, teams, shifts, TeamConfig, leave — API-tested), **tickets** with internal notes (API-tested), **holidays** (API-tested), and **onboarding, offers, payroll, projects** (only their role guards were tested).

## Planned phases (owner's scope, 2026-11-02) — documented, NOT built

- **Phase 1, HR & Company Management (BUILT 2026-11-02; migration `20261102100000_company_access_and_codes` NOT applied to any database; staging test `tests/phase1-company-hr.test.cjs` written, NOT yet run; nothing deployed):** (1) **Company access** = ONE rule in `lib/companyAccess.ts` (`accessFor`, pure rules in `companyAccess.logic.ts`): only HR/Admin ever reach beyond their own company; `OWNER_CODES` or an all-companies row in the NEW table `company_access` = all companies; HR/Admin with rows = own + assigned; a pinned deployment = that company only; read from the database on every request. `viewerIsOwner` now means `access.all`. Used by Admin employees (list with `companyId`, edit, create, password reset, team members, options), employee import/update, Companies, Leave, Corrections and Policies (`deciderIdsForCompany` decides who is notified). Payroll/onboarding still only distinguish 'all companies' (per-company grants are NOT applied there). Top management (access.all) assigns companies in Companies > Company access (`GET /admin/company-access`, `POST|PUT /admin/company-access/:userId`; nobody changes their own; only HR/Admin accounts). (2) **Employee IDs**: generated by `admin/employeeCode.ts` from the company prefix + a locked per-company counter (`employee_code_sequences`, advisory lock, allocated INSIDE the creating transaction so a failed create gives the number back, skips any code already in use, seeded above the highest existing number of that prefix, 4-digit padding); before the migration the old highest+1 rule is used. IDs are NEVER edited (`EMPLOYEE_CODE_LOCKED`); only an Admin may type one when creating; the Add Employee form leaves the ID blank by default (import option `blankIdIsGenerated`, shown as a tick for spreadsheets; email placeholders use the real generated ID). Prefix: only Admin/top management, 2-8 letters/digits, unique whatever the capitals; changing it never touches old IDs and the company's counter restarts above the highest number of the new prefix. (3) **Edit Employee** (`admin/employeeEdit.service.ts`, drawer in `admin/page.tsx`, `GET /admin/employees/:id/edit`): company and ID shown but locked (`COMPANY_TRANSFER_NOT_SUPPORTED`: a move would orphan history); designation must come from the company's list once it has one and replacing an existing one needs a confirmation; salary editable by Admin/top management only, replacing needs a confirmation (`monthlySalary`; payroll reads it, null = 0); reporting manager = active MANAGER/ADMIN of the SAME company, never self, no loops; employee type via `employee_profiles`; email editable (unique per company); every change is audited and salary amounts are shown to Admins only (`audit.service.ts` `hideSalary`). (4) **Dropdowns** (DB-backed): `GET /admin/employees/dropdowns?companyId=` (departments = Teams, designations, managers, shifts, statuses, employee types), `designations` table with Companies > Designations to add/rename/hide (never rewrites what a person has; `scripts/seed-designations.ts` copies existing titles verbatim, dry run by default). (5) **Duplicates**: case-insensitive unique indexes on company names, company prefixes and team names per company (migration), designations per company, plus a same-name person warning when creating (`DUPLICATE_PERSON`, override with `confirmNotDuplicate`). Existing data is never rewritten; the migration adds new tables and three indexes only (check the three duplicate queries in its comment before applying). Tests: `tests/company-access-logic.test.mts` (22, no DB), `tests/phase1-company-hr.test.cjs` (staging only).
- **Future phases — do NOT implement until asked:** (1) Unified Query Desk. (2) Bulk salary release after external payment completion. (3) Automatic salary slip generation. (4) Removal of PF/ESIC display.

## Known tech debt

- (none open — `platformToday()` now lives in `src/lib/dates.ts`; `server.ts` loads dotenv via `import "dotenv/config"` on line 1.)

## Open issues (as of 2026-10-28)

- None known in the API paths covered by the test run below.
- Sign-out, Reports padding, Reports stat-card captions and the `master`/`main` mismatch from the 2026-10-15 list are resolved (login form is at `/login`; Reports uses `p-8`; "Late days" tile now reads "of N days present").
- **Not yet deployed:** the 2026-10-28 backend fixes (login lockout, live status check, cross-company invite guard, error-code mapping, strict dates, meeting/productive formula) exist only in the working tree until the live server is redeployed.
- **Not yet covered by tests:** browser UI, onboarding/offers/payroll/projects flows beyond role guards, the 15-minute scheduler and alert generation, email sending, timezone behaviour on non-IST platforms.

## API test run (2026-10-28)

No automated test suite exists in the repo. A scripted API run was done against a **Neon branch copy** of the database (never the original): 272 checks, all passing after fixes. It covered login/token attacks, a role × endpoint access matrix (ADMIN, HR, MANAGER, EMPLOYEE), admin CRUD, clock-in/breaks/meetings, dashboard/report numbers vs raw rows, tasks and score weighting, tickets, holidays, and cross-company isolation. Behaviours worth knowing, confirmed by that run:

- Clocking in again on the same day **resumes** the session and logs the gap as an `OTHER` break ("Away between clock-out and clock-in").
- Removing a team member is a **soft** removal (`isActive=false`, `leftAt` set).
- A manager may re-grade an already-graded task; an employee may delete an approved task only while it is still `PENDING`.
- Users created in Admin must change their password before the tracker unlocks (`mustChangePassword` gate).
- Always test against a Neon branch, not the live database: the live one is in use.

## Verified in a browser (2026-10-15, as admin)

- Daily Tasks: add (HIGH, 3pt) → score 0/3, 0%; complete → 3/3, 100%; delete → 0/0. No console errors.
- Reports: Group by Person (4 rows), Day (2 rows), Week (1 row, 2026-W42) all load; totals reconcile (9h00 + 8h49 = 17h49 vs 16h target = 111%).
- **End meeting ends it for everyone (2026-11-03, NO migration, local, not committed):** `POST /api/meetings/:id/end` (organizer only) now also closes every participation still open (`meeting_participation.leftAt` null) at the SAME server time, `endedBy = 'MANAGER'` (already allowed by the table's CHECK), duration computed as usual, each claimed with `updateMany where leftAt null` so a person pressing End at the same moment is recorded once. People who never joined get nothing; people who already ended keep their own time. A meeting past its scheduled end can still be ended here while someone is left open (otherwise 409 `MEETING_OVER`). Audit line "Ended ... for everyone (N still in the meeting ended)"; every invitee's screen refreshes through `meetings:changed`. The confirm text on the Meetings page says it ends for everyone. Not built: a button for the past-but-open case (the organizer's per-person Correct still covers it). DB test not written/run.
- **Attendance Calendar: leave, holiday, week-off priority + half-day leave (2026-11-05, NO migration, local, not committed):** ROOT CAUSES found in code: (1) `calendarStatus` let ANY check-in override leave/holiday/week-off, and mapped the engine's hours-based `HALF_DAY` on a half-day-leave day with no check-in to **Absent**; (2) the month engine (`attendance.service.ts`) only read `leave_days` rows, so an APPROVED request whose day had no row (shift/holiday changed after approval) fell to Absent; (3) the engine put holiday/week-off ahead of leave. FIXES: the engine now reads APPROVED `leave_requests` as a read-only safety net, ONLY for a scheduled working day that is not a holiday and has no `leave_days` row (pending/rejected/cancelled are never read; nothing is written); full-day leave is checked first, then holiday, then week-off (half-day leave still sits after holiday/week-off, as before); each `AttendanceDay` carries `leaveHalfDay`. `calendarStatus` priority: Leave > Holiday > Week-off > half-day leave (NEW status `HALF_DAY_LEAVE`, "Half-day leave", counted on its own) > check-in (Present/Late/Half day) > Absent (finished tracked working day) > Not tracked; worked time is still shown on a leave/holiday/week-off day (note "Worked Xh on a leave day"). Reports, payroll and the Attendance report read the same engine, so they follow (payroll: a leave row on a holiday is now Leave instead of Holiday, both paid). Calendar page legend/colour for Half-day leave added; the report's Arrival column also shows it. Tests: `arrival-logic.test.mts` (39), `attendance-leave-priority.test.cjs` (36, local throwaway DB), `attendance-calendar` (43) and `activity-policy` (81) still pass. Not changed: UNPAID leave is still paid by the engine (existing rule), no history rewritten.
- **Organization hierarchy + approval workflows (built 2026-11-05, local only, NOT committed; migration `20261103100000_org_hierarchy_workflows` NOT applied to any real database, only to the throwaway local one):** NEW TABLES ONLY (`leave_request_projects`, `leave_approval_events` append-only with a trigger, `task_entry_details`); nothing hardcoded to names, the hierarchy is each person's `reportsToId`. **Reporting rules** (`admin/hierarchy.logic.ts`, enforced in create + Edit Employee, error `MANAGER_WRONG_ROLE`): Employee -> a Manager, Manager -> an Admin (same company, active, never self); HR/Admin need no line (a Manager/Admin is still accepted). Existing lines are not rewritten; the HR/Admin dashboard card lists people with no valid line ("map by hand"). **Leave** (`leave/leaveFlow.logic.ts` pure rules, `leaveFlow.ts` DB side, extends the existing leave module): Employee -> tagged Manager -> HR final; Manager -> mapped Admin -> HR final; HR -> tagged Admin (final, no HR step); Admin -> approved automatically (days written, visible on `GET /leave/away`). The tagged approver is stored in `request_approvals.approverIds` (no new column); ONLY that person can answer (role alone is not enough, 404 otherwise). One status everywhere (`stage`): Pending Manager / Admin / HR Approval, Approved, Rejected (+ Cancelled). HR sees every request at once but gets 409 `WAITING_FOR_MANAGER` until the first level approves; a first-level rejection is final; **the old "HR decides without the manager, with a reason" override is REMOVED for leave** (still exists for attendance corrections); an Admin only closes a legacy request that never had a first-level step. Form: leave type, from/to, total days (server `GET /leave/preview`), reason, projects (multi-select, only projects the person is on, validated), tagged approver (`GET /leave/form-options`; employee may change it to any active Manager of the company; manager's is fixed; HR must choose an Admin). History: `leave_approval_events` (who, role, action, time, comment) shown on each request. Notifications use dedupe keys (tagged approver + HR at submit, HR when the first level approves, the person on every decision). A person with no valid approver mapped still submits and it goes straight to HR (flagged). **Daily tasks** (`tasks/tasks.entry.logic.ts`, `tasks.entry.ts`): new form fields hours spent (0-24), progress Completed / In Progress / Blocked (stored as `daily_tasks.status` + a blocked flag; progress and approval are separate chips), tagged reporting manager (default = reporting manager, must be an active Manager of the company), project (one per entry, as before), the manager's optional approve comment. APPROVING/REJECTING: only the tagged manager (legacy tasks with no tag: project/team manager or reporting line); **HR and Admin can see every task but cannot approve or reject** (queue rows carry `canAct`, the UI says "View only"); grading and Revert/Reopen keep their earlier permissions (HR/Admin of the company still allowed). A manager's own self-managed tasks are untouched. Today's date is still the entry date (no back-dating). Before the migration every new reader degrades to "table missing" and leave/tasks behave as before (`flowReady()`, `entryReady()`). Dashboards: `_approvals/OrgOverview.tsx` card (`GET /leave/overview`) on the HR/Admin dashboard and the manager/employee Today page. Tests: `org-hierarchy-logic` (44), `task-entry-logic` (14), `org-hierarchy-workflows.test.cjs` (126, throwaway DB); `manager-step.test.cjs` leave expectations updated to the new rules, its 4 remaining failures are attendance-correction cases that need a recorded day (date-dependent, unrelated). Not built: task Date field is today only; browser UI not clicked through; no per-company HR approver scoping beyond `accessFor`; the old `leave.test.cjs` needs seed passwords and was not run.
- **Admin can read huddle participants (2026-11-05, NO migration, local, not committed; owner's decision, overrides the earlier "HR/Admin counts only" rule FOR ADMIN ONLY):** `GET /meetings/:id/participation` now also answers an ADMIN (for the companies they act on, `accessFor`): names, join/leave times, durations, late flags, `readOnly: true`; the Admin cannot correct records (`PATCH` stays organizer-only, 404). `GET /meetings/participation/history?userId=` also lets an Admin read one person's history across their companies. HR is unchanged (counts only via `/participation-counts`), managers still see only meetings they organized, another company's Admin gets 404. UI: on Team Huddle > Company overview an Admin's "x of y" is a link that opens the participant list (read-only). Test: `tests/admin-meeting-participation.test.cjs` (23, throwaway DB); `huddle-attendance` (96) still passes; the older `meeting-participation.test.cjs` fails at its first step (it invites people who do not report to the manager, which the "own reporting employees only" rule refuses), a pre-existing test problem.
- **Admin dashboard / control centre (phase 1 of the Admin Panel enhancement, built 2026-11-05, NO migration, local, not committed):** an ADMIN now lands on `AdminDashboard` (`dashboard/_admin/AdminDashboard.tsx`, chosen in `dashboard/page.tsx`); HR keeps `HrDashboard` unchanged. Sections: overview cards, who reports to whom (employees per manager, role and company split, people with no valid reporting line), attendance and leave (7-day stacked bars + today's late/half day/absent), productivity and tasks (7-day average productive hours, today's task counts, hours recorded), pending approvals, recent activity (audit log), quick actions (Add/Import/Update Employee via the existing `EmployeeActions`, Add Project, Manage Employees, View Reports). Data: new READ-ONLY `GET /api/admin-console/overview` (module `admin-console/`, ADMIN only, limited to the companies the Admin acts on via `accessFor`) built from the same engines the detail pages use (`getAttendanceCalendar`, `leave.overview`, `listLogs`, stored `work_sessions.productiveSeconds`, `daily_tasks`, `task_entry_details`); pending-item counts reuse the HR dashboard's endpoints. Cards open the detail page with the filter in the address where supported: Admin > People `?role=` and `?q=`, Leave `?tab=review|team`, Attendance Calendar `?from=&to=&status=` (the legend chips there now also filter people by status). Not yet built from the plan: employee full-profile page, create wizard, organization tree page. Tests: `tests/admin-console.test.cjs` (20, throwaway DB). UI type-checked only, not clicked through in a browser.
- **Role shown as "Admin" for HR (investigated and fixed 2026-11-05, frontend only, no migration, local, not committed):** ROOT CAUSE is presentation, not identity. Verified end to end that Sonali Gouda (CP-03, designation "HR Generalist") is stored as HR and that the sign-in response, the token, `/auth/me` and `requireAuth` all carry that stored role (the role is re-read from the database on every request; `tests/role-identity.test.cjs`, 18 checks, all four roles + a demotion). The "Admin" text a signed-in HR person saw came from the frontend: the Employees page (`/dashboard/admin`, shared by HR and Admin) had a fixed heading "Admin", the sidebar chip printed HR as "Hr" (`role.charAt(0) + role.slice(1).toLowerCase()`, also in `TodayView`), the HR dashboard had no title, and roles were formatted by several private copies. FIX: ONE shared `frontend/lib/roles.ts` (`roleLabel`, `ROLE_LABEL`, `dashboardTitle`): HR -> "HR", an unknown or missing role is never replaced by another role, only ADMIN produces "Admin". Used by the sidebar chip, profile, Today header, Add Employee, Admin dashboard tiles, the people lists, audit and query screens. Employees page heading is "Admin" for Admins and "People & settings" for HR; the browser tab and a small heading say "HR Dashboard" / "Admin Dashboard". Permissions were not touched. Test: `tests/role-labels.test.mts` (11). NOT reproduced in a browser (type-checked and API-tested only). Note: CP-03 may also be in `OWNER_CODES` (company-wide reach, an access setting, not her role); that was not checked.
- **HR no longer gets the Admin screen as-is (2026-11-05, frontend + 3 route guards, NO migration, local, not committed; follow-up to the "Admin shown for HR" fix):** ROOT CAUSE: HR's sidebar "Employees" (and "Teams, shifts & rules") opened `/dashboard/admin`, ONE page built for Admin (heading "Admin", tabs People, Teams, Shifts, Thresholds, Leave) with no role-based rendering; HR was only ever sent to the right DASHBOARD (`HrDashboard`), never to a right-sized Employees page. The sidebar role chip was already correct. FIX: `dashboard/admin/tabs.ts` (`tabsFor`, `tabFor`): Admin keeps all five tabs and the heading "Admin"; HR gets heading "Employees" and ONLY People, Teams and Leave (asking for `?tab=Shifts|Thresholds` lands on People); HR's sidebar link reads "Teams". ENFORCED ON THE SERVER, not just hidden: `POST /admin/shifts`, `PATCH /admin/shifts/:id`, `PATCH /admin/config/:teamId` are now `requireRole("ADMIN")` (HR can still READ shifts and teams to assign people; creating HR/Admin accounts, salary and the Admin console were already Admin-only). **This removes HR's ability to edit shifts and alert thresholds (a behaviour change on purpose; revert the three guards and the tab list to restore).** Tests: `tests/hr-admin-boundary.test.cjs` (25: 4 Admins, HR, 3 Managers, Employee), `tests/admin-tabs.test.mts` (8). Verified against the throwaway DB only; not clicked through in a browser.
- **Each role has its own home address (2026-11-05, frontend only, NO migration, local, not committed):** ROOT CAUSE of "HR opens /dashboard/admin": there were no per-role addresses. Sign-in sent every role to `/dashboard` and the role only picked a screen at that same address; `/dashboard/admin` was the shared Employees page (HR's sidebar "Employees" opened it), so HR kept landing on a URL that said "admin". Nothing in the code redirected HR to it after sign-in. FIX (`lib/roleHome.ts`, `dashboard/_routing/useRoleGate.ts`): Admin -> `/dashboard/admin` (the Admin control centre), HR -> `/dashboard/hr` (`HrDashboard`), Manager -> `/dashboard/manager` and Employee -> `/dashboard/employee` (both `TodayView`). Sign-in reads the role from `/auth/me` and goes straight to that home; `/dashboard` itself only redirects to the person's home; each role page is guarded (anyone else is sent to THEIR OWN home, never to another role's; unknown role = employee home, never Admin). The shared Employees management page MOVED to `/dashboard/employees` (HR and Admin, each with their own tabs, see the HR note above); an old `/dashboard/admin?tab=|role=|q=|status=` link is forwarded to `/dashboard/employees` with the same filter. Sidebar links follow the role. The server was not changed: every API still enforces the role. Tests: `tests/role-home.test.mts` (11) and a REAL Chrome run on an isolated stack (copied frontend on 3100 + throwaway DB on 5012, one user per role: 4 Admins, HR, 3 Managers, Employee): 75 checks, 0 failed (address after sign-in, refresh, `/dashboard` redirect, 10 cross-role direct URLs, Employees tabs per role, old links, sidebar links, 5 logout/login switches). Test users and the copy were removed afterwards.
- **HR filter system, Group 1: Attendance, Calendar, Employees (built 2026-11-05, NO migration, local, not committed; Group 2 and 3 not started):** ONE shared filter bar (`dashboard/_filters/FilterBar.tsx` + `useHrFilters.ts`): Search (name or employee ID), Company, Project (several can be ticked), Reporting Manager, plus Designation / Employment status where a page uses them and a slot for the page's own filters. It shows active filters as removable chips, "Clear all filters", and "Showing x of y employees" (filtered next to the organization total, with a "Filtered" tag). The selection lives in the address (`company`, `project`, `manager`, `q`, `designation`, `status`), is read once on arrival (survives refresh) and company/project/manager are carried by the sidebar between the Attendance, Calendar and Employees links. Choices come from the real records via `GET /api/hr-filters/options` (companies, projects, managers, designations, narrowed company -> projects -> managers -> designations; stale selections are dropped from the bar). BACKEND: `modules/hr-filters/` = pure rules (`parseFilters`, `matches`, accepts the old `teamId` as project), `resolvePeople()` (the unique, authorised people who pass the filters + `orgTotal`/`filteredTotal`) and the options. FILTERING IS NEVER AUTHORIZATION: the viewer's people are worked out first (HR/Admin = `accessFor`, i.e. every company they act on; manager = their own people) and filters only narrow that; an id outside it matches nobody. A project filter is a condition on the person (active member of ANY chosen project), never a join, so nobody is counted twice. DECISIONS (owner): **no Department filter** (the system has no department field; teams stand in for departments, so none was invented; Project = the team); no schema change. **The live Attendance board now follows company access for HR and Admin** (it used the older "own company only" rule; Sonali CP-03, Jayesh JAY-01 and Sanjay SSB-01 already hold an all-companies grant in `company_access`, nothing was changed there); managers are unchanged. WHAT CHANGED: `/team-attendance/live` takes the filters and returns `scope`, each row gains `projects[]`, `reportingManager`, `attendanceStatus` (the Calendar's own rule via `calendarStatus`, nothing new); the Attendance page has the bar above the cards, an attendance-status filter that narrows the table only (the cards stay split by activity), and the columns Employee, Employee ID, Company, Project, Reporting manager, Shift, Current activity, Clock in/out, Break used, Washroom, Meeting duration, Working duration, Attendance status. `/attendance-calendar` takes the filters (company, project, manager, name/ID; the old per-department select is gone) and returns `scope`. `/admin/users` takes project, manager and designation, returns `scope`; the Employees People tab uses the bar (Role stays as its own select) and its Team column is now "Projects". Existing calculations, approvals and exports were not touched. Tests: `hr-filters-logic.test.mts` (19), `hr-filters.test.cjs` (44, throwaway DB), and a real Chrome run on an isolated stack (50 checks, 0 failed: all three pages, two-project uniqueness, cascade, search with dropdowns, clear all, URL persistence, carry to Calendar, HR without a grant, manager, Admin). Not in Group 1: Dashboard, Leave, Productivity, Tasks, Reports, Meetings, Documents, Payroll, Query Desk.
- **HR / Admin Team tasks showed only 7 people (fixed 2026-11-05, NO migration, local, not committed):** ROOT CAUSE in the BACKEND task query, not the dropdown: `manageableUserIds()` (`tasks.service.ts`, feeds the task queue, its roster, its rows, its project list and the live Socket.IO audience) gave HR and Admin "everyone in my OWN company" (`platformId: actor.platformId`), the same older rule the Attendance board had. Sonali (CP-03, Colab Platforms) therefore saw only that company. FIX: HR and Admin now get every company they act on through the shared company-access rule (`accessFor` / `scopeWhere`: own company, assigned companies, or all for top management; a deployment pin still limits it), and the queue's project list follows the same rule. Managers are unchanged (their own reporting team). `GET /tasks/review/queue` also takes the shared HR filters (company, project, reporting manager) and returns `scope` (filtered total next to the organization total); filters only narrow the authorised list. UI (`approvals/page.tsx`, `EmployeePicker.tsx`): for HR/Admin the heading reads "N employees across your companies", the Everyone row reads "All authorized employees", and the shared filter bar (Company, Project, Reporting Manager) sits above the picker; managers keep "N people in your teams", "The whole reporting team" and no bar. VIEWING IS NOT APPROVING: HR and Admin rows show "View only · <tagged manager> decides" and the API answers 403 to approve/reject from HR or Admin, from a manager the task is not tagged to, and for tasks with no tag; only the tagged manager approves (unchanged from the org-hierarchy work). Tests: `tests/hr-team-tasks.test.cjs` (37, throwaway DB: counts vs the real active people, both companies, no leak for HR without a grant, filters, managers unchanged, direct-API approve/reject refused for HR and Admin) and a real Chrome run (21 checks: HR, manager, Admin). `manager-self-tasks.test.cjs` has 2 failures that come from this machine's IST timezone shifting a DATE read by that test (pg parses a DATE as local midnight), not from this change.
- **HR visibility audit (2026-11-05, read-only, nothing changed yet):** `backend/tests/hr-visibility-audit.cjs` (throwaway DB) seeds two companies and prints, per module, whether HR with an all-companies grant, HR without a grant, an Admin with all-company access and a manager see company A's and company B's employees. FOUND on the live code: CONSISTENT (HR/Admin with access see both companies): Attendance live board, Attendance report, Calendar, Team tasks queue, Leave summary and requests, Employees list. STILL OWN-COMPANY ONLY for HR and Admin (older rules): Shift breakdown, Task productivity (and its hourly view), Reports & Analytics (`reports.service.ts` staff branch filters `platformId: viewer.platformId`), Live team and alerts (`tracker.service.ts`), Employee documents tracking, Meetings company overview (`oversight`), Payroll (own platform by design). Two shared causes: `visibleUserIds()` (non-managers = own platform) used by shift breakdown, productivity, live team, alerts; and direct `platformId: viewer.platformId` filters in reports, documents, meetings oversight and payroll. Meeting INVITES stay inside the manager's company on purpose. Query Desk, Corrections and Policies use `accessFor` in code (not exercised by the script).
- **One profile in the sidebar, for every role (2026-11-05, frontend only, NO migration, local, not committed):** the sidebar is ONE shared component (`dashboard/layout.tsx`, same for Admin, HR, Manager, Employee; role differences are only which menu items show). It used to show the person twice (top: brand mark, name, My Profile, bell; bottom: avatar, name, role badge, employee code). NOW: TOP = the signed-in person's photo or initials (`<Avatar>` from `/auth/me`), full name, the My Profile link and the bell with its unread count (the avatar is a mouse shortcut to My Profile, hidden from screen readers and the tab order so the link is not announced twice); MIDDLE = the role's menu, scrolling on its own (`min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto`); BOTTOM = only the Theme switch and Logout, pinned (`shrink-0`), so no item hides behind it. The bottom profile block, the role badge and the employee code in the sidebar are gone (role and code still appear on My Profile); `ROLE_CHIP` was removed. The mobile drawer reuses the same sidebar. Menus, permissions, notifications, theme saving and logout are untouched. Verified in real Chrome as Admin, HR, Manager and Employee (72 checks: name appears once, correct person, role-specific menu, last item scrolls above the pinned block, My Profile and Notifications open, theme switches, Logout clears the session; plus the phone drawer for HR and Employee).
- **HR / Admin visibility made consistent (2026-11-05, backend only, NO migration, local, not committed; follows the audit above):** HR and Admin now see every company they act on (company access: own, assigned, or all for top management; a pinned deployment limits it) in EVERY people-based module. FIXED: `visibleUserIds()` (`tracker.service.ts`) now gives HR/Admin the company-access scope, which fixes Shift breakdown, Task productivity (and hourly), Live team and alerts in one place; Reports (`reports.service.ts`): the people come from company access AND the company-bound inputs are now read per company (each company's team targets, default working days and holidays are applied to ITS people; for a single-company view, a manager or an employee the numbers are identical, checked), the headline target stays the viewer's own company; Meetings overview and cancelling from it (`meetings.service.ts`) follow the same reach (still title, organizer, time and head-count only for HR/Admin). Managers, HR without a grant and Admin without a grant see no more than before. NOT changed, by decision: Payroll (salary data, own company; cross-company payroll is the owner's call), Employee documents tracking and verification (the checklist is built from each company's own document requirements, so showing several companies is a redesign, not a scoping fix), meeting invites (stay inside the manager's company on purpose). Tests: `hr-visibility.test.cjs` (61, throwaway DB: eleven modules x HR with grant / HR without / Admin with / Admin without / manager, report numbers identical for a single company, a company-B holiday counted for company B's people only, meeting cancel reach) plus the earlier suites (all still pass); `hr-visibility-audit.cjs` re-runs the table.
- **HR filter system, Group 2: Reports (all three tabs) and Leave (built 2026-11-05, NO migration, local, not committed; Groups 1 and 2 done, Group 3 not started):** the SAME shared bar (`_filters/`) now narrows these pages; the choice is read from and written to the address and carried by the sidebar between the filtered pages (company, project, manager: Attendance, Calendar, Employees, Reports, Leave, Team tasks). BACKEND: `narrowIds()` (`hr-filters/hrFilters.service.ts`) narrows a module's OWN already-authorised list and returns `scope {orgTotal, filteredTotal}`; it can only remove people. A person's figures are IDENTICAL filtered or not (tested), a person on several chosen projects counts once, and a filter naming a company/project outside the viewer's access matches nobody. "Productivity Tracker" in the sidebar is each person's OWN clock page and has no filter on purpose; task productivity lives under Reports. Tests: `hr-filters-reports.test.cjs` (35, throwaway DB) and a real Chrome run (27 checks: all three Reports tabs, the CSV download holding exactly the filtered people, Leave type / stage / dates / project, carry between pages); all earlier suites still pass.
- **Four individual Admin accounts (built 2026-11-05, local only, NOT applied to the live database; the owner must approve first):** `backend/scripts/provision-admins.cjs` (dry run by default; `--apply` writes in ONE transaction; DB from `ADMIN_DB_URL`, never `.env`; the live database is refused without `--allow-live`). RENAME: the existing Admin records SSB-01 (Sanjay Singh Bhati, Aayush Wellness Limited) and JAY-01 (Jayesh, Colab Platforms Limited) keep their user id, company, role, history and all-company grant; only `employeeCode` becomes `sanjay.bhati` / `jayesh.ahire` (owner's decision; normally IDs are locked, so this is a one-off script), the placeholder email `<old code>@<slug>.local` moves to the new name (a real email would be left), the password is reset to `Sanjay` / `Jayesh` and `mustChangePassword` = true. The old IDs stop signing in (no alias kept). CREATE: `devang.master` / `aayush.master` as ADMIN with an all-company `company_access` row (platformId null), passwords `Devang` / `Aayush`, forced change at first sign-in; their HOME company is a parameter (`--company-devang=<slug> --company-aayush=<slug>`) and is still the owner's to choose (no employee record exists for them). Refuses (writes nothing) if the target is missing/not ADMIN, a username is taken, an alias exists, or a same-named person exists; a second run is refused. One audit line per account (module Employees, no password recorded). Existing JWTs of SSB-01/JAY-01 stay valid until they expire (stateless tokens). **Behaviour change found by the test: the Audit log (`audit.service.ts`) now follows the shared company-access rule** (HR/Admin read every company they act on; before: own company only), so an Admin with the all-company grant reads the whole organisation's log; HR without a grant is unchanged. Test: `tests/admin-accounts.test.cjs` (73 checks, throwaway DB: dry run, apply, old IDs rejected, bcrypt, grants, history kept, sign-in per account, forced change gate, full Admin access, separate audit actors, trigger blocks edit/delete, suspension isolated); `hr-admin-boundary` (25), `hr-visibility` (61) and `role-identity` (18) still pass. The new-password minimum is the existing 8 characters.
- **Daily Tasks form: "Select Project" + "Tag Reporting Manager" (2026-11-06, NO migration, local, not committed):** builds on the task-entry work (tagged manager lives in `task_entry_details`, project in `daily_tasks.teamId`; notification and approval already went only to the tagged manager). CHANGES: (1) the manager list is now the person's AUTHORISED managers only (`authorizedManagers()` in `tasks.entry.ts`): their reporting manager (allowed even in another company, owner's decision 2026-11-06) + active Managers of the SAME company who manage (primary or co-manager) an active project the person is an active member of; before it offered every active Manager of the company. The reporting manager is preselected (or the only authorised one). The server refuses any other tag (`BAD_TAGGED_MANAGER`), including another company's manager or a non-manager. (2) "Required when available" (owner's choice, NOT strictly required): the server answers 400 `PROJECT_REQUIRED` when the person has projects and sent none, and `TAGGED_MANAGER_REQUIRED` when they have authorised managers but none is preselected and none was sent; a person with NO project / NO authorised manager is not blocked and the task keeps the earlier routing (project/team manager or reporting line). Read-only check on live 2026-11-06: of 61 active EMPLOYEE/HR/ADMIN people, 33 have no authorised manager and 5 no project (reporting lines not mapped yet), which is why strict was not used. Project is validated against the person's own memberships (`NOT_MY_PROJECT`); nothing is auto-assigned. (3) Form labels "Select Project *" / "Tag Reporting Manager *" with "Select a project" / "Select a manager" prompts, Send disabled until both are chosen when choices exist, an amber hint ("Ask HR to ...") when a list is empty, form resets after sending. Only the tagged manager can approve or reject (HR/Admin view only), unchanged. Tests: `tests/task-form-managers.test.cjs` (35, throwaway DB), `org-hierarchy-workflows` (expectation for the manager list updated; its remaining failure is leftover leave rows in the throwaway DB overlapping the picked dates, unrelated), `hr-team-tasks` (37), `task-revert` (60), `task-entry-logic` (14); real Chrome as Employee, tagged manager, another manager, HR and Admin (17 checks, light, dark and phone screenshots).
- **Daily Tasks: the approver is chosen by the PROJECT (2026-11-06, NO migration, local, not committed; SUPERSEDES the earlier "authorised managers" rule of the same day):** the form is Task description, Additional details, **Select Project**, **Tag Project Manager**, priority, Send for approval (hours/progress/date kept from before). The managers offered for a task are those of the project picked for THAT task, read from the existing assignments (`teams.managerId` + `team_managers`, `projectManagerChoices()` in `tasks.entry.ts`): an ACTIVE **Manager or Admin**, in any company (a project can be run from another company); the main manager is preselected, co-managers are offered too. The employee's reporting manager is a separate relationship and is offered ONLY when he also manages that project; it never changes. Server rules: tag must be one of the project's managers (400 `BAD_TAGGED_MANAGER`), a project E is not on is refused (403 `NOT_MY_PROJECT`), no project when the person has any = 400 `PROJECT_REQUIRED`, **a project with no eligible manager = 409 `PROJECT_HAS_NO_MANAGER`** with a clear message (the form shows it and disables Send), nothing sent = the project's main manager. A person with NO project is not blocked (no tag, old routing). The tag is saved in `task_entry_details.taggedManagerId`, so a later change of the project's manager never moves a submitted task (old approver keeps it, the new manager only gets new tasks); moving a not-yet-approved task to another project through PATCH re-tags it to the new project's manager (same checks). Only the tagged person can approve/reject (an Admin is an approver only when tagged; HR and other Admins stay view-only); notification goes to the tagged manager only. `GET /tasks` `entryForm` is now `{ ready, byProject: { [projectId]: { options, defaultId } } }` (the old `options/defaultId` are gone). Read-only check on live 2026-11-06: **6 active projects with members have NO eligible manager and would block their members until one is assigned (19 member slots):** AH Logistics (none), AH Product & Tech (listed manager Komal Sonar is an EMPLOYEE role), Avatar/Core Sales (none), Avatar/Operations (manager Neha Sharma is SUSPENDED), TC Software - team (none), Vakratunda - team (none; Junaid's team). Live mappings seen: Avatar (Colab Platforms) -> Ankit Kale, Avatar AI/ML -> Sanjay Singh Bhati (Admin), Colab Delivery and Take-health -> Md Faizan Qureshi, aayush wellness limited -> Roshan Sharma. Tests: `tests/task-form-managers.test.cjs` (42, throwaway DB: two projects/two managers independently, Admin as project manager, reporting manager not offered, no-manager project blocked, manager change keeps history, co-manager, project move re-tags, reporting line unchanged), real Chrome as Employee/Pavan/Admin approver/reporting manager/HR (16 checks, light/dark/phone), `hr-team-tasks` (37), `task-revert` (60), `hr-admin-boundary` (25); `org-hierarchy-workflows` expectation updated (its one failure is leftover leave rows in the throwaway DB).
- **Daily Tasks: "tag any manager" for other-project work (2026-11-06, owner's choice, NO migration, local, not committed; amends the project-based rule above):** the Tag Project Manager box now has two groups: **Manager of this project** (preselected, from the existing assignments) and **Other managers (work for another project)** = every active Manager or Admin of the employee's OWN company (never the employee, never another company, never HR/Employee roles; `otherManagerChoices()` in `tasks.entry.ts`, returned as `entryForm.others`). A manager of another company who is not on the chosen project is still refused (400 `BAD_TAGGED_MANAGER`). A project with no manager is no longer a dead end: nothing sent = 400 `TAGGED_MANAGER_REQUIRED` ("choose who should approve"), the form shows a hint and the employee tags another manager; 409 `PROJECT_HAS_NO_MANAGER` now only when nobody at all is available. The project is still a required own project and is saved with the task; the employee is NOT added to anyone's project and the reporting line never changes. Only the tagged manager can approve and is notified. Tests: `tests/task-form-managers.test.cjs` (50), real Chrome (17), `hr-team-tasks` 37, `task-revert` 60, `hr-admin-boundary` 25 (`org-hierarchy-workflows` keeps its one unrelated leave-data failure).
- **Daily Tasks: TWO INDEPENDENT dropdowns (2026-11-06, owner's final rule, NO migration, local, not committed; SUPERSEDES the project-based and "other managers" notes above):** **Select Project** = every ACTIVE project of the employee's own company plus any project they are already assigned to elsewhere (`selectableProjects()` in `tasks.entry.ts`); the employee does NOT have to be on it, and picking one assigns nothing. **Tag Reporting Manager** = every active MANAGER of the employee's company, plus active ADMINS who carry project approval responsibility (main or co-manager of an active project of that company), plus the managers of projects the employee is already assigned to even when run from another company (`eligibleManagers()`); never the employee, HR, Employee-role people, suspended people, a plain Admin with no project, or an unrelated company. The two lists are independent: nothing is preselected, and choosing or changing the project never changes the manager. Server (`proposeTask`): project required (400 `PROJECT_REQUIRED`) and must be selectable (403 `NOT_MY_PROJECT`, message "not available to you"), manager required (400 `TAGGED_MANAGER_REQUIRED`) and must be eligible (400 `BAD_TAGGED_MANAGER`); only a company with NO eligible manager at all keeps the old routing. Saved with the task: employee, title/description, project (`daily_tasks.teamId`), tagged manager (`task_entry_details.taggedManagerId`), date, priority, Pending, createdAt. Editing the project of a waiting task never touches the tag. A later change of a project's manager or of the reporting line never moves a submitted task. `GET /tasks` `entryForm` = `{ ready, projects: [{id,name,company,ownCompany}], managers: [{id,name,code,role}] }`. **Review queue fix found by the test:** because the approver can be outside the employee's usual team, `listForApproval` now also returns the tasks TAGGED to the viewer (and only those; the employee's other tasks stay hidden, and not while HR/Admin filters are active), so a tagged manager actually sees them; the live (Socket.IO) refresh of that queue still follows the old audience, so such a manager gets the in-app notification and the page's normal refresh. Only the tagged manager approves/rejects (an Admin only when tagged; HR and other Admins view-only). Tests: `tests/task-form-managers.test.cjs` (34), real Chrome as Employee / Admin approver / manager outside the team / reporting manager / HR (15, light/dark/phone), `hr-team-tasks` 37, `task-revert` 60, `hr-admin-boundary` 25; `org-hierarchy-workflows` (task section adapted to the new rule; its one failure is the unrelated leftover-leave-row problem).
- **Daily Tasks dropdowns read from the existing RECORDS, across companies (2026-11-06, owner's latest rule, NO migration, local, not committed; SUPERSEDES the "own company" scope of the two notes above, the independence and server rules stay):** **Select Project** = every ACTIVE project in `teams` (any company, real ids, whether or not the employee is on it; a deployment pinned to one company keeps only that company). Projects that share a name in different companies are different records, so their label gets the company ("Colab Delivery · Colab Esports"); a unique name stays plain; nothing is merged or created. **Tag Reporting Manager** = every ACTIVE Manager or Admin who is someone's reporting manager (distinct `users.reportsToId` of active/invited people) OR the main/co manager of an active project, any company, each user id once (`eligibleManagers()` in `tasks.entry.ts`); never the employee, HR, Employee-role people (a project "manager" with the Employee role is not eligible), or suspended people. No pairing between project and manager, nothing preselected. **Decision (owner): this exposes project NAMES and manager NAMES/codes across companies to every employee that opens the form (nothing else about those companies).** Live read-only run 2026-11-06 (as AI-04): 16 projects (aayush wellness limited, AH Logistics, AH Product & Tech, Avance Sales, Avatar, Avatar AI/ML, AWL Creative, Colab Delivery x3 [Colab Esports, Colab Platforms Limited, Colab Sports And Infra], Core Sales, Operations, Take Product, Take-health, TC Software - team, Vakratunda - team) and 6 managers (Aman Sharma TL-01, Ankit Kale AI-01, Jayesh JAY-01 Admin, Md Faizan Qureshi CE-04, Roshan Sharma AWL-01, Sanjay Singh Bhati SSB-01 Admin). Not eligible although listed in the records: Neha Sharma (suspended, runs Operations/Avatar) and Komal Sonar (Employee role, listed manager of AH Product & Tech). "Avatar India" and "Colab Platform" are COMPANY names, not project names (the projects are Avatar AI/ML and Avatar / Take-health / Colab Delivery). Records with missing details (reported, never filled in): 15 active people with no reporting manager (AH-01..06, CP-05, TCS-04..08, Vak-01..03), 1 with no project (AVT-15). Tests: `tests/task-form-managers.test.cjs` (41: lists equal an independent SQL of the records, no duplicates, same-name projects kept apart, any project + any manager, refusals, routing, only the tagged manager decides), real Chrome (15), `hr-team-tasks` 37, `task-revert` 60, `hr-admin-boundary` 25, `org-hierarchy-workflows` 125 (+ its one unrelated leave-data failure). Note: the pulled "Talk to Management" module needs `npm run db:generate` (its type errors are the un-generated Prisma client, not this change).
- **Daily Tasks form layout (2026-11-06, frontend only, no backend/database change, local, not committed):** the squeezed four-column row (Progress was cut to "In Progr", long project/manager names unreadable) is replaced by a labelled, stacked form in the order Task description *, Additional details (optional), then **Select Project * | Tag Reporting Manager *** side by side (two wide columns, `min-w-0 truncate`, the full selected name in the tooltip), then Date | Hours spent | Progress | **Task priority** (now labelled; two columns on a phone, four from `lg`), then a right-aligned Send for approval (full width on a phone). No logic changed (same state, same validation, same payload); dark theme uses the existing palette only. Checked in real Chrome with deliberately long names at 1500, 1100, 820 and 390 px, light and dark: no sideways overflow, every field inside the card, Project and Manager boxes at least 280 px wide on tablets and up; full workflow test still 15/15. Pitfall: `\u00b7` typed inside JSX TEXT renders literally, use the real "·" character (inside a JS string it is fine).
- **Fix: "the theme icon opens Employees" (2026-11-06, frontend only, no backend/database change, local, not committed):** ROOT CAUSE was NOT event bubbling. The round icon at the top right of the HR dashboard header (`HrDashboard.tsx`) was never a theme button: it was the "Teams, shifts and rules" LINK to `/dashboard/employees?tab=Teams`, and its icon (`Ico.cog`) was drawn as a circle with eight rays, i.e. exactly a sun. People clicked the "sun" to change the theme and were navigated. The real theme controls only existed in the sidebar (`ThemeRow`) and the mobile top bar (`ThemeIconButton`), so the HR desktop header had none. FIX: (1) `ThemeIconButton` (`_theme/ThemeToggle.tsx`) takes an optional `className`, is `type="button"`, and stops the click from reaching any parent; (2) the HR header now has a real sun/moon toggle first (round white button, same look as its neighbours) using the shared component; (3) the settings link keeps its address but its icon is now a real gear (`Ico.cog`, with a tooltip), so the two can no longer be confused. Theme saving and persistence (`ThemeContext`, `user_preferences`, browser mirror) are untouched. Only the HR dashboard had the look-alike link (Admin, Manager and Employee homes have no such icon; their sidebar toggle was checked too). Test: real Chrome on an isolated stack (26 checks: HR sun -> dark and moon -> light with no navigation and no notification panel opening, refresh and page change keep the theme, the gear still opens Teams, the profile name/avatar still opens My Profile, sidebar row and phone top bar toggle without navigating, Employee/Manager/Admin sidebar toggle stays on their home and survives refresh, no page errors).
- **Fix: read notifications stayed in the bell (2026-11-07, NO migration, no schema change, local, not committed):** ROOT CAUSES: (1) `GET /notifications` (the bell) returned the latest 30 notifications of ANY status, so a read one only lost its blue dot and stayed in the dropdown; (2) marking read was not applied on screen until the server answered and the list was re-fetched, a failed save was swallowed silently, and a fetch already in flight when the person clicked could bring the item back; (3) the notification centre had only All / Unread (the server could not filter on read); (4) the bell rows had no per-item control (only click-to-open or "Mark all read"); (5) older bug found by the browser test: clicking the ALREADY selected tab on the notification centre set "loading" without changing the filter, so the list stayed on the grey placeholder forever. FIX: `notifications.service.ts` `listMine` now returns the latest 30 UNREAD plus the true unread count (same response shape), `page()` accepts `filter=all|unread|read`; `NotificationsContext` marks read optimistically (the item leaves the bell and the badge drops at once, a `seq` counter makes any older in-flight fetch discard its answer, the save runs in the background and the next fetch restores the truth if it failed); `Bell.tsx` rows are an "open" button plus a "Mark read" button, empty state "all caught up, read notifications are in View all"; the centre has All notifications / Unread / Read with the optimistic update, the footer counts total / unread / read, and the same-tab click is ignored. Nothing is ever deleted: read items stay in the database and under All and Read; reading is still scoped to the signed-in person (`userId` is part of the update filter). The HR dashboard header has a SEPARATE bell ("Needs attention", built from pending approvals and alerts, not from notifications, no read state), so its badge can differ from the sidebar bell's. Tests: `tests/notifications-read.test.cjs` (61, throwaway DB, Admin/HR/Manager/Employee: bell lists only unread, count, saved readAt, record kept, refresh does not bring it back, All/Unread/Read, another person cannot mark it, mark all, 33 unread = badge 33 with 30 listed, guards) and real Chrome (35: Sonali's HR account and the three other roles, immediate removal in ~50 ms, a deliberately late stale fetch does not resurrect the item, refresh, View all filters, mark all, nothing deleted). The pulled "task rating" / "Talk to Management" modules still need `npm run db:generate` (type errors in tasks.rating.ts, not from this change).
- **Export Excel on all three team reports (2026-11-07, NO migration, no schema change, local, not committed; SUPERSEDES the old `reports/_team/xlsx.ts`, now deleted):** Reports > Attendance report, Shift breakdown and Task productivity each show **Export CSV** (unchanged) and **Export Excel** side by side (`_team/ExportButtons.tsx`; Attendance's old Export dropdown became these two buttons, same handlers). ONE builder `_team/reportXlsx.ts` (`buildReportWorkbook` is pure, `downloadReportXlsx` adds the download; exceljs loaded only on click) makes a genuine .xlsx: data sheet(s) with header row 1 (bold, coloured, frozen, filters on, readable widths, no merged cells) and a last sheet "Report info" (report name, period, filters in words from `/hr-filters/options`, exported on in the company timezone, exported by, record count per sheet, report totals that match the screen). REAL cell types: dates = date cells (`dd mmm yyyy`), times = time cells (`h:mm AM/PM`, company timezone), durations = numbers shown `[h]:mm` (they add up, may pass 24 h), counts/decimals/percent numeric; blank stays blank (never 0); text such as "Working", "Target not configured", "No task data available" stays text; text is never run as a formula. The format is put on every cell of a typed column. Attendance workbook: the old columns plus Project(s), Reporting manager and Team Huddle (Excel only; the CSV is unchanged); Pending Tasks is still a live formula. Shift Breakdown workbook: name/ID, team, project(s), reporting manager, date, day status, login, logout, shift duration, tea, lunch, washroom, huddle, other, total break, break allowance, break over allowance, productive time, target. Task Productivity workbook: sheets Productivity (one row per person, same figures as the CSV + project(s) + reporting manager), Daily (person x day) and Tasks (every task behind the report: employee, project, reporting manager, tagged manager/approver, task, details, task date, priority, hours, progress, approval status, completed on/at, "Counted in productivity" Yes/No). BACKEND (read-only, additive): `team-attendance/peopleDetails.ts` (`projectsAndManagers`, two batched reads); the existing `/attendance` rows gained `projects`, `reportingManager`, `huddleHours` (from `huddleSecondsByDay`, null when none), `/shift-breakdown` rows gained `projects`, `reportingManager`, `/productivity` people gained `projects`, `reportingManager`; NEW `GET /team-attendance/productivity/tasks?from&to&<HR filters>` (`getProductivityTasks`: the SAME authorised people as the report (`scopedPeople`), a manager's own self-managed tasks excluded, one row per task dated in the range plus any task completed in the range, `counted` = the report's own rule, so the number of Yes rows equals the "Tasks completed" total). Permissions are the existing ones (manager = own people, HR/Admin = companies they act on, employees 403); filters only narrow. The export holds the COMPLETE filtered data, fetched from the same endpoints with the same filters, not the screen's scroll position. Not exported because it is not in the report data: a tea/lunch split on the Attendance sheet (it is on the Shift Breakdown sheet). Tests: `backend/tests/report-xlsx.test.mts` (26, no DB: builds a workbook, reads it back: PK package, sheets, header style, freeze + filter, date/time/duration/percent cells and formats, blanks, text-not-formula, formula result, info sheet, 5,000 rows), `backend/tests/report-export-data.test.cjs` (35, throwaway DB: new fields, tasks endpoint, counted rule = report total, permissions, filters), real Chrome (43: HR and Manager, all three reports, files read back and compared with the API the screen uses, filters respected, dark and phone). NOT done: opening the files in desktop Microsoft Excel (no Excel on this machine; the files were validated by reading them back and checking the OOXML parts).
- **HR dashboard header: duplicate profile, settings gear and theme toggle removed (2026-11-07, frontend only, no backend/database change, local, not committed; supersedes the header part of the "theme icon opens Employees" fix):** the top-right row of `HrDashboard.tsx` now holds ONLY the "Needs attention" bell (pending approvals + alerts, with its count and panel, unchanged). Removed from it: the person's name + "View profile" + avatar (the sidebar already shows the person once, and still links to My Profile), the round gear link to Employees > Teams (the sidebar still has "Teams" for HR and "Teams, shifts & rules" for Admin, nothing in Admin settings was touched), and the sun/moon button added the day before (the sidebar "Light/Dark theme" switch is the theme control, and the phone top bar keeps its own icon + notification bell, which is the shared mobile header). The unused `Avatar`/`ThemeIconButton` imports and the gear icon were deleted; `ThemeIconButton` stays in `_theme/ThemeToggle.tsx` for the phone top bar. Inspected for the same duplicate: only the HR dashboard had this top-right row; Admin (`AdminDashboard`) has no such header; the Employee and Manager "Today" page has a LEFT-hand "Welcome, name" greeting with the avatar (a greeting, not a top-right profile; left as is). Notifications (sidebar bell: unread count, mark read, mark all, View all) are unchanged. Test: real Chrome on an isolated stack, one user per role (41 checks: no profile link or name block in the page body for HR/Admin/Manager/Employee, sidebar profile still opens My Profile, no gear and no sun/moon button on desktop, the sidebar theme switch changes the theme without navigating, the sidebar bell still marks read, HR's top-right bell still opens "Needs attention" without navigating and the top row holds exactly one control, phone has no overflow and no extra controls, no page errors).

- **Company owners are not regular employees for the document workflow (2026-11-07, NO migration, no schema change, local, not committed):** an "owner Admin" = a user whose ROLE is ADMIN and who holds the all-companies grant in `company_access` (or whose code is in `OWNER_CODES`), decided from the database by `ownerAdminIds()` / `isOwnerAdmin()` in `lib/owner.ts` (never by display name; an HR account with the same grant is NOT an owner admin, nor is a look-alike name without the grant). On live this is Sanjay, Jayesh, Devang, Aayush (plus the suspended `AVT-0001` System Admin, harmless). For them: `GET /documents/mine` answers `{exempt:true}` with an empty checklist (nothing "missing"); `/auth/me` and `/auth/me/profile` carry `documentsExempt`; the Profile page has no "My Documents" tab or Documents tile (a direct `?tab=My Documents` link falls back to Personal) and the Documents page shows only Employee tracking + Requirements; they are left out of document tracking (`tracking()` rows and totals), the joiners list, the onboarding reminder emails and the onboarding SLA alert. UNCHANGED: their Admin role and every other permission, the organisation-wide Employee Documents module (view, review, requirements) for Admin and HR, HR verification, and the employee's own workflow. No document rows were touched or deleted. Admin dashboard, name, photo and login are as before. Tests: `tests/owner-admin-documents.test.cjs` (37, throwaway DB, plus helper `tests/_owd-reminders.mts`) and a real Chrome run (45 checks: all four owners, HR, employee; script kept outside the repo).
- **Admin (owner) and HR are separate roles, menus and permissions (2026-11-07, NO migration, local, not committed; owner's decisions: HR keeps adding Employee/Manager accounts; payroll unchanged for both; "Organization Hierarchy" and "Approval Centre" are links to existing pages):** SERVER (not just hidden): ADMIN-ONLY now: creating/editing companies (`POST /companies`, `/companies/:id`; HR can still read the list), every project write (`/projects` POST routes: create, managers, co-managers, members), teams = projects (`POST/PATCH /admin/teams`, team members add/remove), the audit log (`/audit-logs*` and `audit.service`), company access grants (`companyAdmin.grantor`: even an HR with the all-companies grant cannot hand out access), changing a person's system role (`employeeEdit` and the bulk Update Employees), changing who an EXISTING person reports to (`HIERARCHY_ADMIN_ONLY`, also in the bulk update with `team` / `projects` / `reportsTo` rows rejected `ADMIN_ONLY_FIELD` for HR). Already Admin-only before: HR/Admin accounts, salary, shifts, alert thresholds, the owner overview. UNCHANGED for HR: employee details, onboarding/joiners, Employee/Manager account creation and import (incl. a team named in the sheet at creation), documents, attendance and corrections, leave (final approval), payroll, offers, policies, Query Desk, HR reports, holidays; task approval stays with the tagged manager only (HR and Admin view only). The Edit Employee drawer gets `permissions.canEditHierarchy` (HR sees department and reporting manager read-only, role read-only). SIDEBAR (`dashboard/layout.tsx` GROUPS): Admin = Owner Dashboard, User Management, Organization Hierarchy, Company Management, Project Management, Employee Overview, Attendance, Leave Oversight, Task Monitoring, Meetings, Productivity (`/reports?tab=productivity`, new tab link), Payroll Oversight, Employee Documents, Reports & Analytics, Approval Centre (`/leave?tab=review`), Audit Logs, System Settings (`/employees?tab=Teams`); HR = HR Dashboard, Employees, Employee Documents, Attendance, Calendar, Leave Management, Payroll, Recruitment / Joiners, Offer Letters, Policies, Reports & Analytics, Query Desk, Notifications; both keep a small "Me" group (own clock, Daily Tasks, My History, My Payslips, Change password, Management Inbox / Talk to Management) and Holidays. Admin's menu does not list Calendar, Query Desk, Policies, Joiners, Offers or Tasks & Performance (reachable by address and by API; Admin keeps the API rights). Pages `/dashboard/companies`, `/projects`, `/audit-logs` use `useRoleGate(["ADMIN"])` (HR is sent to the HR home). HR's Employees page has People and Leave tabs only (`employees/tabs.ts`); the "Create a project" quick link left the HR dashboard. Behaviour changes on purpose: HR can no longer edit companies/projects/teams, read the audit log, change roles/hierarchy or hand out company access. Designations still have no HR screen (they live under Companies). Tests: `tests/admin-hr-separation.test.cjs` (110, throwaway DB), `admin-tabs` (8), regression `hr-admin-boundary` 25, `role-identity` 18, `admin-console` 20, `hr-visibility` 61, `hr-team-tasks` 37, `owner-admin-documents` 37; real Chrome run (55 checks: four Admins, HR, Manager, Employee: sidebars, dashboards, direct URLs, read-only HR drawer). `phase1-company-hr.test.cjs` crashes in its own setup (never run before); `companies.test.cjs`/`audit-logs.test.cjs` (older, need seed users) were not run and expect HR to edit companies / read the audit log, so they need updating.
- **Green dashboard redesign (2026-11-07, FRONTEND ONLY, no migration, local, not committed; owner chose: recolour the whole app, an empty Birthdays card, HR and Admin homes only):** colour comes only from tokens in `globals.css` (light + `html.dark`): primary #0D9E76 / hover #087F61, page #F7F9F9, text #172522, secondary #72807D, border #E6ECE9; the Tailwind `indigo-*` scale now renders emerald and the old brand hexes in 29 files were swapped by one scripted pass (`bg-indigo-600` etc. are green). Reusable `_dashboard/widgets.tsx` (KpiCard, Panel, EmployeeStatusTable, EventsList, AnniversaryList) + pure `anniversaries.ts`; HR and Admin homes use them: KPI strip, Team Performance and Attendance charts (recharts), Employee Status table (first 8, from /admin/users + today's report/calendar), Events and Meetings (holidays + /meetings?scope=upcoming), Work Anniversaries (date of joining only), Pending Approvals, Recent Activity. Not verified: Add/Import/Update wizards end to end after the recolour, dark-mode contrast scan, tablet widths, realtime update live, `next build`.
- **Task approval visibility (2026-11-12, backend only, NO migration, local, not committed):** ROOT CAUSE: approving was already limited to the tagged manager, but the task QUEUE (`listForApproval`) returned every pending task of everyone the viewer could see, and `proposals` (Needs Approval, the HR/Admin dashboard counts) was "all pending", so HR, Admin and other managers saw tasks they could not decide. FIX: one pure rule `pendingTaskVisibleTo(role, canAct)` (`tasks.entry.logic.ts`): a task still PENDING is shown to HR never, to Admin as read-only monitoring (`canAct` false), and to a manager only when it is his to decide (tagged to him; untagged old tasks keep the earlier reporting-line rule). `proposals` = pending AND `canAct`. Same rule in the Excel/CSV task sheet (`getProductivityTasks`). Approving/rejecting by HR is refused for every task (403) even for an untagged task where HR is the reporting boss. Notifications for an untagged task no longer go to HR (`withoutHr`). Queue rows gained `decidedBy` / `decidedAt` (approval history for the Admin). After approval the task is visible to HR and the team as before (view-only). NOT changed: grading/ratings and Revert/Reopen keep their earlier permissions (HR/Admin of the company still allowed); the Attendance report's "Total Tasks Planned Today" still counts waiting tasks (a count, same for every viewer); old untagged tasks stay approvable by the project manager / reporting line. Tests: `tests/task-approval-visibility.test.cjs` (35, throwaway DB, four roles over HTTP); `hr-team-tasks` (38) and `task-form-managers` (41) expectations updated (HR no longer sees pending tasks); `task-revert` still has 7 failures that are the old `/outcome` grading route replaced by star ratings (not this change).
- **Warning letters (2026-11-12, NO migration, local, not committed; owner chose to REUSE `employee_documents`):** `modules/warning-letters/` (`/api/warning-letters`: `GET /mine`, `GET /person/:userId` HR/Admin, `POST /` issue, `GET /:id/file[?download=1]`, `POST /:id/acknowledge`). A letter is an `employee_documents` row: category OTHER, status APPROVED, no requirement, file in the private store (folder `warnings`). That table has no reason/name/date/acknowledgment columns, so the letter's name, reason and issue date are JSON inside `rejectionReason` after the marker `[WARNING_LETTER]`, the issuer is `uploadedById`, and the employee's acknowledgment is `reviewedAt` + `reviewedById` (written once, 409 afterwards). WORKAROUND TO REMEMBER: every reader of checklist documents must leave letters out: `notWarningLetter` is applied in `documents.service.ts` and `onboarding.service.ts` (checklists, joiner progress), and the old review/download routes answer 404 for a letter (`isLetterRow`). Any NEW query over `employee_documents` must add the same filter or letters will show as "documents". `onboarding.reminders.ts` counts only REQUESTED/REJECTED, so approved letters never trigger reminders. Rules: HR/Admin issue to people of companies they act on (`accessFor`; another company = 404; never to themselves); upload (PDF checked by its `%PDF` bytes, Word, image, 10 MB) OR type text (system builds a PDF with pdfkit), not both; name 3-120, reason 3-1000, issue date today or up to a year back; employee sees only their own (id from the sign-in); view/download only the employee or HR/Admin of that company; file served private, `no-store`, `nosniff`; employee notified once, issuer notified on acknowledgment; issue + acknowledge are audited. UI: `My Profile` card "Warning letter" under Offer letter and above Payslips (`_warnings/WarningLettersCard.tsx`, text "No warning letters issued."), HR/Admin panel + "Issue warning letter" modal inside the Edit Employee drawer (`_warnings/WarningLettersAdmin.tsx`). Tests: `warning-letters-logic.test.mts` (35, no DB), `warning-letters.test.cjs` (60, throwaway DB, five roles over HTTP). `documents.test.cjs` (1 fail: audit row) and `owner-admin-documents.test.cjs` (1 fail: people count 8 vs 9) fail identically WITHOUT these edits (checked by reverting them); `hiring-offers-payslips.test.cjs` needs seed passwords. Not done: opened in a real browser; no separate HR page listing all letters of a company.
- **HR dashboard shows every authorised company (2026-11-12, FRONTEND ONLY, no backend/database change, local, not committed):** ROOT CAUSE: `HrDashboard.tsx` cut every list down in the BROWSER to the signed-in person's own company (`inMyCompany`, written when `/reports` was own-company only), so HR Sonali (Colab Platforms, which has 6 people, all-companies grant) saw "6 employees" although the server already returned everyone she may see. The people list, live board, alerts and `/reports` were already on company access. FIX: that narrowing is gone; a Company selector (shown when more than one company is authorised, built from the people list) narrows the people, live feed, alerts, joiner counts and `/reports?company=`. Total Employees = everyone who has not left (active + invited + suspended); Active Employees = ACTIVE only; EXITED people are not counted (decision: former employees are not part of the headcount; say if they should be). The people list has no paging or manager filter (checked). Live check (read-only): 67 non-exited, 56 active across 12 companies. Test: `tests/hr-dashboard-scope.test.cjs` (18, throwaway DB: counts equal the database, every manager's people, company filter, HR without a grant unchanged, Attendance board counts). Not opened in a browser. There is no company called "Take Health" in the live database (the Take companies are "Take Limited"); Admin permissions and reporting lines untouched.
- **Additional reporting managers (built 2026-11-12, migration `20261112100000_reporting_manager_links` NOT applied to any real database; tested only on the throwaway DB; local, not committed):** owner chose real multiple managers ("option C"). DESIGN: the MAIN reporting manager stays `users.reportsToId` (one person; leave routing, hierarchy rules and the org overview still use only it). NEW table `reporting_manager_links` (userId, managerId, createdById; unique pair; CHECK userId <> managerId; no relations in Prisma, FKs only in the migration, like photos) holds EXTRA managers. An extra manager gets the same reach as a reporting manager: `visibleUserIds` (so Attendance board, Calendar, Reports, Productivity, Live team), task review reach (`actingUserIds`, `manageableUserIds`, `legacyMayReview`, `assertCanManage`), task notifications (`managersOf`), the task tag list (`eligibleManagers`), and the "Reporting manager" filter (`personWhere(f, linkedIds)`, `listUsers`). NOT changed: leave routing (still the main manager or the tagged approver), `reportsToId`, the org-hierarchy rules, who is "someone's manager" for the org-overview counts. Helpers in `lib/reportingLinks.ts` (`linksReady` re-checks a missing table every 30 s; every function answers "none" and never throws before the migration). Admin-only API `PUT /api/admin/employees/:id/additional-managers { managerIds }` (replaces the list; each must be an ACTIVE Manager or Admin, not the person, not their main manager; max 5; any company; audited with before/after; 503 `FEATURES_NOT_READY` before the migration; HR/Manager/Employee get 403); `GET .../edit` returns `additionalManagers` + `additionalManagersReady`. UI: Edit Employee > "Additional reporting managers" box (Admin only, saved straight away, `_employees/AdditionalManagers.tsx`). ALSO CHANGED (needed for the owner's "Faizan sees Colab" case): the Attendance Calendar no longer limits a MANAGER to people of his own company (it used `platformId = own`, unlike the live board and Reports); his list is his reporting people + teams + links, whatever company. Tests: `tests/reporting-links.test.cjs` (40, throwaway DB: runs BEFORE the migration (503, everything still loads), applies the migration SQL itself, then checks who sees/approves, validation, filters, removal, audit); regression `task-approval-visibility` 35, `hr-team-tasks` 38, `task-form-managers` 41, `hr-visibility` 61, `hr-filters` 44, `hr-filters-reports` 35, `hr-admin-boundary` 25, `hr-dashboard-scope` 18, `attendance-calendar` 43. NOT done: not opened in a browser; leave and attendance-correction manager steps ignore extra managers (the employee can tag any manager on leave); to use it live the migration must be applied to Neon after a backup (owner's go-ahead), then Saurabh Jadhav (CP-01) gets Jayesh and "DM sir" (identity still to be confirmed) added in Edit Employee.
- **Production migration status (checked and applied 2026-11-13):** `prisma migrate status` and a table-by-table check against Neon showed that EVERY migration in `backend/prisma/migrations` is applied and recorded in `_prisma_migrations` (42 of 42). The "NOT applied" notes in the entries above are OUT OF DATE (they were true when each feature was written). The last pending one, `20261112100000_reporting_manager_links`, was applied with `npx prisma migrate deploy` (never `migrate dev`, which wants to reset this drifted DB): row counts of all 73 tables were identical before and after (6,608 rows), only the new empty table and its `_prisma_migrations` row were added.
