- **Global layout + sidebar cleanup (2026-11-13, FRONTEND ONLY, no backend/database change, local, not committed):** ROOT CAUSE of the uneven alignment: every page set its OWN outer padding and width (`p-8`, `p-4 sm:p-8`, `p-3 sm:p-6`, `page-4xl/5xl/6xl/7xl/fluid`, `max-w-3xl`, `mx-auto`), so content started at 296 / 304 / 488 / 593 px and ended 1 to 432 px from the right edge depending on the page. FIX: ONE shared container in `globals.css` (`.app-container` + `.app-main`, tokens `--page-pad-x` = 16 / 24 / 32 px at phone / tablet / laptop and `--page-fluid` for the maximum width). The dashboard layout wraps `<main>` in it and the desktop header (`_shell/TopBar.tsx`, fixed 64 px) and the phone top bar use the SAME container, so title, breadcrumb and first card share one left edge. Pages must NOT add their own outer padding or max-width any more: 40 page wrappers were stripped of those classes (the `page-3xl..7xl` utilities remain in `globals.css` but nothing uses them as an outer wrapper; remove only after checking). Sidebar: the top brand block (green "H", "Avatar HRMS", company name) is gone on desktop AND from the phone top bar (which now shows the page title); the profile block (photo or initials, name, My Profile) stays at the top; every menu row is 40 px high; the menu scrolls on its own; new desktop collapse: a menu button at the left of the header toggles a 72 px icon rail (labels become tooltips, badges stay, My Profile and Logout stay reachable) and the choice is kept in this browser (`localStorage` key `hrms-sidebar`); the phone drawer is always expanded. Same shared layout for all four roles; menus and permissions untouched. Two phone-width overflows fixed (Employees tab row and Reports preset row now wrap). Measured in real Chrome on a throwaway local database, every page x Admin/HR/Manager/Employee (101 pages): at 1440 and 1920 px the sidebar is 256 px, the header 65 px, content starts at 304 px and ends 32 px from the right edge on every page (the earlier spread was 296 to 593 px); at 820 and 390 px no page scrolls sideways; collapse/expand/reload/navigate checked for all four roles (36 checks). Wide tables (Reports, Attendance) scroll inside their own box by design. Known, not changed: Chat keeps its own full-height layout; some pages still repeat the header title as a big heading. `tsc` clean, `next build` passes; eslint's `set-state-in-effect` errors in `layout.tsx` and `employees/page.tsx` pre-date this work. Not verified: real tablet/phone hardware, dark mode screenshots of every page.
- **Admin pages, titles and rating filter (2026-11-14, FRONTEND + one read-only backend field, NO migration, local, not committed):** (1) **Employee Overview** is its own page, `/dashboard/employee-overview` (`_overview/EmployeeOverview.tsx`, counting rules in the pure `_overview/stats.ts`, Admin only via `useRoleGate`); User Management stays at `/dashboard/employees` (before, both sidebar items opened it). It reads the same `/admin/users` list as User Management (so the numbers agree): tiles Total / Active / Inactive (suspended + exited) / Not started (invited + candidate) add up; bars by company, department (= teams; a person in two teams counts in both), designation, reporting manager (main line); Active / Inactive / All toggle; searchable directory, 20 per page; profile dialog with direct reports and a link to User Management. Shared filter bar narrows everything. Note: HR dashboard "Total employees" excludes exited people, this page's Total counts every record. (2) **Header, breadcrumb and tab title use ONE rule** (`_shell/pageTitle.ts`): own home, My Profile, the sidebar entry (the plain entry wins over a `?tab=` shortcut, so Admin's Reports page no longer says "Productivity"), then `EXTRA_TITLES` for pages with no menu entry (Corrections, Holidays, Office Network, Query Desk ... used to say "Dashboard"). The tab title is re-applied while a page is shown because Next put "Avatar HRMS" back after in-app navigation; the three Today pages (`/dashboard/today|employee|manager`) are exempt because they write their own live timer title. Admin menu "Meetings" renamed "Team Huddle"; the Admin Employees heading is "User Management". (3) **Admin profile** (`_profile/AdminProfile.tsx`; the staff profile is untouched, `profile/page.tsx` picks by role): tabs Personal (photo), Contact, Account details, Role & access, Security; no offer/warning letters, payslips, documents, performance or reporting manager. `/auth/me/profile` returns an `admin` block (company access names or ALL, created, last sign-in) for Admins only. The Edit Employee drawer shows "N/A" for an Admin's reporting manager and hides Additional managers and Warning letters for Admin accounts (nothing is cleared). Sidebar "My Payslips" for Admin is still there (payroll decision unchanged). (4) **Admin Leave Oversight** has no "My leave" tab and no Request leave button (For my approval / All requests / Leave taken; opens on For my approval, `?tab=mine` falls back); Employee, Manager and HR unchanged; the API was not changed, so an Admin could still call the request endpoint directly. (5) **Tasks & Performance > Completed** has a Rating status dropdown (All / Pending rating / Rated, by the saved rating, not by status). (6) **UI audit** (real Chrome, 4 roles, 1920/1600/1366/1280/960/820 px, light + dark, throwaway DB): the 13 Nov shared layout held (same content edges on every page, no sideways overflow); the findings above were the real defects. Left as is: control heights differ by 2 to 4 px across pages (selects 36/38/42, inputs 34-42, buttons 32-42) and some Admin menu labels differ from page headings (Leave Oversight / Leave, Company Management / Companies); a uniform control-height pass touches hundreds of class lists and was not done. Pitfall found: the account theme (`user_preferences`) overrides the browser mirror, so a test must PATCH `/preferences` to test dark. Tests: `employee-overview-logic` (27), `page-title` (12); browser scripts kept outside the repo (Overview 45 checks, Admin profile 68, Admin leave 43).

NOW: **Approval Centre** = `/dashboard/approval-centre` (Admin only via `useRoleGate`; `_approvals/LeaveApprovalQueue.tsx` + `TaskApprovalQueue.tsx`): leave tagged to this Admin (a Manager's leave: first stage, HR decides after; HR's own leave: final) with Approve/Reject through the existing `/leave/manager/requests` + `/decide`, and tasks employees tagged to this Admin (the queue's `proposals`) with Approve / Send back through the existing `/tasks/review/:id/approve`. Only the tagged Admin sees and can answer an item (another Admin sees none). Attendance corrections and the final leave step stay with HR and are NOT in the queue. **Leave Oversight** (`/dashboard/leave`) is monitoring only for Admin: tabs All requests and Leave taken (shared filters, leave type, stage, dates, per-person summary), no "For my approval" tab, a banner linking to the Approval Centre when something waits; an old `?tab=team` link redirects there (the redirect sits in a wrapper before the page mounts, because the shared filters rewrite the address and cancelled a redirect made inside the page). The Admin dashboard's "Leave sent to you for approval" row and the sidebar point to the new page. Manager's Team requests tab unchanged. No server, permission, leave record or history change. Tests (browser scripts outside the repo): approval-centre 37 checks, Admin leave 43.
- **Organization Hierarchy (Admin, 2026-11-14, NO migration, local, not committed):** found: the sidebar item was just `/dashboard/employees?tab=People` (User Management's People tab), no page of its own; the data already existed (`users.reportsToId`, `needsManualMapping`, the Edit Employee reporting-manager field with role rules and loop check), so no new table or API: `/dashboard/organization-hierarchy` (Admin only, `_hierarchy/OrgHierarchy.tsx`, pure rules in `_hierarchy/orgTree.ts`) reads `/admin/users` and saves ONLY a reporting manager via the existing audited `PATCH /admin/users/:id { reportsToId }`. Shows: the Admins side by side as top-level authority (no boss, "Top level" instead of a Change button), Managers under their Admin, employees under their Manager with direct and team counts, designations, expand/collapse (+ Expand all / Collapse all), search, company and project filters (a match keeps the chain above it), a "Needs attention" list (no manager, wrong kind of boss, suspended boss, boss outside your view) with Assign manager, HR listed apart, a two-step change (pick, then a confirmation of from/to) and a notice after saving. Suspended/exited people are not placed. **Owner decision: reporting lines may cross companies** (the live data already did: 2 managers under another company's Admin, 8 employees under another company's manager): `reportingError(..., {anyCompany:true})` is used only by Edit Employee / this page; creating an account keeps same-company. **An Admin can never be given a reporting manager** (`ADMIN_TOP_LEVEL`, 400, new). Because a cross-company line was otherwise ignored by leave, the leave flow now accepts a person's OWN reporting manager as first-stage approver in any company (`approverOk(..., ownLine)`; the default and the options list include them; a free choice of someone else stays inside the company) and `managerDecide` no longer compares the request's company with the manager's (the tag in `request_approvals` is what authorises, enforced by `claimStep`). Task tagging already used reporting managers across companies. Other roles: HR/Manager/Employee are sent to their own home and cannot change a line (HR 403 `HIERARCHY_ADMIN_ONLY`). Live data note (read-only, 2026-11-14): 13 active employees have no manager, 1 manager has none, 10 employees report directly to an Admin (flagged), so the Needs attention list will not be empty. Tests: `org-tree-logic` (26), `org-hierarchy-logic` (55, +11), real Chrome `hierarchy-e2e` (70: structure vs the database, expand/collapse, search, filters, confirmation, cancel saves nothing, audit row, effects on the new manager's live board, leave form and approval, server refusals, HR/Manager/Employee, dark, phone), approval-centre 37 and Admin leave 43 still pass.
- **Owner Dashboard bounced back to User Management (fixed 2026-11-14, FRONTEND ONLY, no migration, local, not committed):** ROOT CAUSE: `/dashboard/admin` forwards OLD links that carry a filter (`?tab=`, `?role=`, `?q=`, `?status=`) to `/dashboard/employees`, and it decided that from `window.location.search` when the page mounted. During a click inside the app the browser address is still the PREVIOUS page's, so clicking "Owner Dashboard" from User Management with a tab open (`?tab=Teams`) or from any page with `?q=`/`?role=` in its address was taken for an old link and sent straight back (it worked from plain addresses, so it looked random). Found by clicking the item from 17 starting pages as four Admins. FIX: `admin/page.tsx` reads the page's own `useSearchParams()` inside a `<Suspense>`; real old links (`/dashboard/admin?tab=Teams`) still redirect. RULE: never read `window.location` to decide a redirect on mount of a page you can reach by a client-side click. The sidebar entry, route guard, title ("Admin Dashboard"), breadcrumb and highlight were already right. Test (browser script outside the repo, four Admins): Owner Dashboard opens from 40 of 40 starting pages, 15 sections load from real data, no console or request errors, People card is a real count, old link still redirects: 77 checks. The dashboard already had the requested content (people and active/inactive, companies, managers and teams, attendance, leave and pending approvals, tasks and performance, activity, quick actions); the notifications/alerts it shows are the pending-approval counts and recent activity, the bell stays in the header.
- **Manager team reports, "Export reports" (2026-11-14, NO migration, local, NOT committed or pushed):** Reports & Analytics (Manager; HR/Admin see it too, for the people they already see) has an "Export reports" card (`reports/_team/TeamExport.tsx`) with presets Last 7 / **Last 15 (default)** / Last 30 / This month / Custom (max 93 days, end not in the future), the dates, the day count and "N employees included" (the SAME call the download uses; the shared filter bar narrows it). Downloads: **complete team workbook (.xlsx)**, one report (Excel sheet or CSV), or one employee's full record. ONE read-only server call, `GET /team-attendance/team-report?from&to&<HR filters>` (`teamReport.service.ts`; router guard = MANAGER/HR/ADMIN, employees 403): the people are decided once by `getAttendanceReport` (viewer's authorised people + filters; a manager gets only people who report to them), every other read is limited to those ids. It composes, without recalculating: attendance rows (login, logout, working/productive/break/washroom/Team Huddle hours, late remark, correction, leave/holiday), the Task productivity rows (now also `rating`, `ratingComment`, `completionNote`, `rejectionNote`), every `tracker_breaks` row (classified by `activityClass`: Break / Washroom (not a break) / Discussion), scheduled meetings and Team Huddle participation (one row per person per meeting, never presumed attended; open records counted only to the scheduled end and flagged) plus approved/unapproved live meetings, all leave requests (every status) and leave days taken, attendance corrections. Tables that may not exist degrade to "none". Workbook (`teamReportBook.ts`, builder `reportXlsx.ts`): Team Productivity Summary, Team Attendance, Team Task Performance, Meetings and Huddles, Break and Working Hours, Leave and Late Remarks, Break details, Attendance corrections, then one sheet per employee (name, ID, reporting manager, period and totals above the table, every day of the period including days with no record, tasks/breaks/meetings as wrapped text per day), then Report info. Summary = plain counts and sums of existing values: NO productivity percentage and NO new formula; Team Huddle time is a column inside productive time, meeting participation is the recorded duration, break time and washroom time stay separate. Hours are the attendance engine's daily figures (rounded to 0.01 h), so totals can differ from stored seconds by a few seconds per day (stated in a header note). `reportXlsx.ts` gained an optional `preamble` (lines above the table; sheets without it are unchanged); its stale brand-colour test expectation was updated to the green. Tests: `team-report.test.cjs` (43, throwaway DB, seed `_team-report-seed.cjs` which refuses anything but the local DB on port 54329; NOTE the pg driver sends JS Dates in the machine's local zone to `timestamp without time zone` columns, so tests insert ISO UTC strings), `team-report-book.test.mts` (25, no DB), `report-xlsx.test.mts` (30), real Chrome `team-export-e2e` (54: real downloads read back and compared with the database, other manager's employee absent, narrowed export, CSV, HR/Admin/Employee, phone). Not done: opened in desktop Excel; a manager's own tasks are excluded as in Task productivity.
- **Bulk temporary passwords script (2026-11-16, NO migration, local script, NOT committed; NOT yet run against live):** `backend/scripts/bulk-temp-passwords.cjs`, test `tests/bulk-temp-passwords.test.cjs` (42 checks, throwaway DB). PREVIEW by default; `--apply` also needs `--confirm "ISSUE <N> PASSWORDS"` with the N the preview prints; the database comes from `BULK_PW_DB_URL` (never `.env`); the live database is refused without `--allow-live` (checked before connecting). SELECTS (owner's rules): ACTIVE/INVITED Employee, Manager and HR accounts that have NEVER signed in (`lastLoginAt` null) or have no password; anyone who has signed in owns a password and is never touched; Admins only when named in `--include-admins=code,...`; suspended/exited/candidate never; **left out: any person whose company OR active project name contains "avatar"** (owner's choice: both Avatar companies and Avatar projects; change with `--exclude-match=`); people already issued by this script and not yet signed in are skipped unless `--reissue-unused`. EFFECT: a unique 12-character cryptographically random password (crypto.randomInt, no look-alike characters, never starts with a symbol), stored only as bcrypt cost 10 (same as the app), `mustChangePassword = true` (the login returns it and the app forces the change), one audit row per person (actor null, path `/scripts/bulk-temp-passwords`, "password never recorded"); employee IDs, emails, roles, companies, names untouched. Rerun safety comes from those audit rows plus the never-signed-in rule. One transaction; the credentials CSV is created (`wx`, never overwrites) and flushed BEFORE the commit and removed if the run fails (only files that run created); it is written OUTSIDE the repo (default `~/productivity-backups/temporary-passwords-<time>/`, repo paths refused) and locked to the current user (icacls on Windows, mode 600 elsewhere), with a second `issue-report.csv` that has every account and its result but no passwords. Nothing is emailed; passwords are never printed or logged. Read-only live preview on 2026-11-16: 34 of 66 accounts would be issued (Aayush Healthscience 6, Aayush Wellness 9, Avance 9, Colab Esports 2, TC Software 5, Vakratunda 2, one Manager among them); 15 signed-in people, 8 Avatar India, 4 Admins, 4 suspended, 1 exited left alone. Note: the Admin screen's own "Reset password" does NOT set `mustChangePassword`; this script does.
- **Quick Add (multiple tasks) + drafts (built 2026-11-16, local, NOT committed or pushed; migration `20261116100000_task_drafts` NOT applied to any real database, only the throwaway one):** Daily Tasks (employees) has two tabs: **Add Single Task** (the old form, unchanged) and **Quick Add (Multiple Tasks)** (`tasks/_quick/QuickAdd.tsx`, pure rules `quickRows.ts`). Each row = its own task (description, project, reporting manager, hours, priority, progress, optional details); buttons Add another / Duplicate / Remove / Save as draft / Submit all (up to 20 per send, rows kept up to 30 in a draft). New rows preselect the last project and manager (browser `localStorage` key `hrms-quickadd-last`, only if still on the lists); picking a project never changes the manager. **Submit all** = `POST /api/tasks/bulk { rows }` (`tasks.bulk.ts`): runs the SAME single-task creation (`createProposedTask`, split out of `proposeTask`, so every rule, notification and routing is identical) once per row in its own transaction; a bad row changes nothing for the others; the answer lists each row (`ok` / `error` in words). Rows that fail stay on screen with the reason; sent rows leave the editor and appear in a summary. **Duplicate guard (Quick Add only; the single form is unchanged):** a row with the same words (case/spacing ignored), project and manager as a task already sent TODAY (not rejected/cancelled) is refused `DUPLICATE_TASK`; checked inside the transaction under a per-person advisory lock, so identical requests at the same moment create ONE task; the screen also ignores clicks while a send is running. Only the tagged manager is notified/asked; HR and Admin stay view-only (unchanged). A MANAGER in self-managed mode gets 403 `BULK_NOT_AVAILABLE`. **Drafts:** NEW table `task_drafts` (one row per draft row, owner only; never read by reports, queues, notifications or productivity; unknown project/manager ids are dropped), `GET /api/tasks/drafts`, `PUT /api/tasks/drafts { rows }` (replaces the person's whole draft sheet; `[]` clears). Before the table exists drafts answer `{ready:false}` / 503 `FEATURES_NOT_READY` and the Save as draft button is hidden, Quick Add itself still works. After a send the draft is rewritten to hold only the unsent rows. Audit: "Sent several tasks for approval (Quick Add)". Tests: `backend/tests/quick-add-logic.test.mts` (17, no DB), `quick-add-tasks.test.cjs` (45, throwaway DB, applies the migration part-way to prove before/after), plus a real-Chrome run (23 checks, desktop/dark/phone, kept outside the repo); `task-approval-visibility` 35, `task-form-managers` 41, `hr-team-tasks` 38, `task-entry-logic` 14 still pass. Not done: autosave of drafts (manual button only), editing a task after it was sent through Quick Add (the existing Daily Tasks rules apply), a per-row "reporting manager" memory per project.
- **Add Previous Day's Tasks / late submissions (built 2026-11-16 on top of Quick Add, local, NOT committed or pushed; migration `20261116110000_task_late_submissions` NOT applied to any real database, only the throwaway one):** a third Daily Tasks tab for employees. The person picks a day BEFORE today (date picker, today and the future excluded), and the same Quick Add rows are saved on THAT day (`daily_tasks.date` = original task date; `createdAt` = real submission time; neither is ever changed afterwards; nothing touches attendance/work_sessions). Owner's decisions: window = **any past date, counted in calendar days** (no working-day check), **per-company setting** `allowedDays` (default 3, Admin only, 0-14), **hard limit 14 days** (`MAX_BACKDATE_DAYS`; older is never accepted even with a reason). Rules (`tasks.late.logic.ts`, mirrored on screen in `_quick/quickRows.ts` `lateState`): within the window it is accepted, flagged "Late submission", reason optional; older than the window up to 14 days needs a written reason (3-300 characters); today/future = `BACKDATE_NOT_PAST`, over 14 = `BACKDATE_TOO_OLD`, bad date = 400 `BAD_DATE`. `POST /api/tasks/bulk { rows, forDate?, lateReason? }` (one date and one reason per send; no `forDate` = today exactly as before) runs the SAME `createProposedTask` (new options `forDate`, `lateReason`), so tag-required, project rules, notifications and routing are identical; the duplicate guard compares against the SELECTED day. A late task always waits for a manager: if it would self-approve (no tag and no reporting manager) it is refused `NO_APPROVER_FOR_LATE`. Only the tagged manager approves/rejects (HR and Admin stay view-only), notification to that manager only, titled "Late task needs your approval" with the date. NEW tables only: `task_late_details` (one row per late task: lateByDays, outsideWindow, allowedDays snapshot, reason; cascades with the task) and `task_late_policies` (one row per company, no row = default 3). Before the migration a previous-day request is REFUSED per row (503-style "not switched on", never silently saved) and everything else works. `GET /tasks/late-policy` (the screen), `GET /tasks/late-policy/all` and `PUT /tasks/late-policy { companyId?, allowedDays }` (ADMIN only, only companies they act on, audited; card "Previous-day tasks" on Admin > Thresholds, `_late/LatePolicyCard.tsx`). Visible: amber **Late submission** label + "Task date X · Submitted <time> (N days late)" + reason on the manager's Approvals rows and on the employee's Daily tasks date view (`_late/Late.tsx`), and a Normal / Late filter on Approvals ("All / Normal / Late submissions (n)", narrows the tabs, counts and list together). `listForApproval` rows and `/tasks/range` tasks carry `late`. Drafts are NOT offered for previous days (the draft table has no date). Not done: a Late column in the Excel/CSV task exports, a late flag in Reports, and a working-day check (owner chose calendar days). Tests: `backend/tests/late-submission-logic.test.mts` (12, no DB), `late-submissions.test.cjs` (59, throwaway DB, applies the migration part-way; drop the two late tables and restart the backend before re-running), real Chrome run (28 checks: employee, tagged manager, Admin, dark, phone; kept outside the repo); `quick-add-tasks` 45, `task-approval-visibility` 35, `task-form-managers` 41, `hr-team-tasks` 38, `task-entry-logic` 14 still pass. Pitfall: a throwaway-DB test must read `timestamp without time zone` columns with `extract(epoch ...)` (the pg driver shifts them by the machine's local zone).
- **Designation "Content Creator" (2026-11-16, local, NOT committed; NO migration, NO database change):** the designation list the Add / Edit / Import / Update dropdowns use is the fixed list `DESIGNATIONS` in `backend/src/lib/designations.ts` (one list for every company, served by `GET /admin/employees/dropdowns` and `/admin/employees/lookups`; `canonicalDesignation` accepts any capitals/spacing and stores the list's own spelling; a near-spelling such as "Content Creater" is refused `DESIGNATION_NOT_IN_LIST`). Adding a designation = ONE line in that array, in alphabetical order (Content Creator sits between "Business Operations & Legal" and "Creative Head"), then a normal deploy: nothing is written to the database, so no live data changed and existing people keep exactly what they have. `users.designation` is plain text, so the people list, filters (`/hr-filters/options` counts it once someone holds it), profile, reports and exports show it with no further change. Finding: the `designations` table and the Companies > Designations screen (add/rename/hide) are NOT what feeds the dropdowns any more (the earlier per-company design was replaced by the fixed list; `ensureDesignations` does nothing), so adding a designation there does not make it selectable. The older note above that calls the list per-company/DB-backed is out of date. Test: `backend/tests/designation-content-creator.test.cjs` (28, throwaway DB: both dropdown endpoints, once and no duplicates, HR and Admin edit incl. overwrite confirmation, Add through the import pipeline, near-spelling refused, list/filter/profile display, nobody else's designation changed); `admin-hr-separation` 110 and `hr-filters` 44 still pass.
- **Delete task, soft delete by the tagged manager (built 2026-11-16, local, NOT committed or pushed; migration `20261116120000_task_deletions` NOT applied to any real database, only the throwaway one):** Approvals (Tasks & Performance) rows get a red "Delete task" action wherever the manager can act (`canAct`, i.e. exactly who may approve: the tagged manager; an old untagged task keeps its project/reporting-line reviewer; HR never, another manager never, an Admin only when tagged, nobody their own task, a manager's self-managed tasks excluded). Owner's decisions: only the tagged manager may delete; the Deleted list is for HR and Admin (companies they act on, `accessFor`) plus the manager who deleted each task. Dialog (`approvals/DeleteTaskModal.tsx`): employee, task, original date, project, a red warning (also says the rating is removed when rated), a REQUIRED reason from a list (Assigned to the wrong employee / Submitted under the wrong project / Approved or rated by mistake / Duplicate task / Other) and a note (required, 3-300 characters, only for Other). `POST /api/tasks/review/:id/delete { reasonCode, note }` (`deleteTaskAsManager`, rules in `tasks.delete.logic.ts`). **Soft delete:** NEW table `task_deletions` (one row per task: who id/name/role, when, reasonCode, reasonNote); the `daily_tasks` row, its rating (`task_ratings`), comments, entry details and approval history are NEVER changed or removed (tested byte for byte). DailyTask only gained a virtual relation `deletion` (no column). **ONE filter for every reader:** `activeTasks()` (`tasks.deleted.ts`) returns `{ deletion: { is: null } }` (empty until the table exists) and is spread into the `where` of every task query: employee list/day/range, reopened list, the manager queue (+ the tagged-outside query), duplicate check (a deleted task no longer blocks a corrected resubmission), self-managed summary, reports, productivity (3 queries + task export + hourly), attendance report/calendar task counts, tracker timesheet, Admin console, leave overview; single-task actions (edit, withdraw, approve, rate, revert, history) answer 404 for a deleted task. **Any NEW query over `daily_tasks` must add `...(await activeTasks())` or deleted tasks will count again.** Ratings are read through the task rows, so a deleted task's stars drop out of ratedCount/ratingSum/avg automatically. Deleted twice or at the same moment = one record (unique taskId, 409 `ALREADY_DELETED`). Employee gets notification `TASK_DELETED` (popup type) with the reason and nothing else changes for them. Audit log: one line per deletion with a full copy of the task (title, description, date, project, status, rating, comments, tagged manager) in `before`. `GET /api/tasks/deleted` (HR/Admin: their companies; MANAGER: only his own deletions; EMPLOYEE 403) feeds the "Deleted tasks" tab (`approvals/DeletedTasks.tsx`, read only, search, newest 300). Before the migration: Delete answers 503 `FEATURES_NOT_READY`, the list says not ready, everything else is exactly as before. No restore button (not requested; the data is all there if wanted). Tests: `backend/tests/task-delete-logic.test.mts` (6, no DB), `task-delete.test.cjs` (55, throwaway DB, applies the migration part-way; drop `task_deletions` and restart the backend before re-running), real Chrome run (24 checks: dialog rules, delete pending and approved+rated, Deleted tab, HR view-only, employee list + notification, dark, phone; kept outside the repo); `task-approval-visibility` 35, `task-form-managers` 41, `hr-team-tasks` 38, `attendance-calendar` 43, `hr-visibility` 61, `hr-filters-reports` 35, `hr-filters` 44, `quick-add-tasks` 45, `late-submissions` 59 still pass. Three suites each have one failing check that ALSO fails on the last committed code (checked against a clean copy of HEAD): `report-export-data` (expects a waiting task the 2026-11-12 visibility rule hides from HR), `admin-console` and `hr-dashboard-scope` (depend on how much data the throwaway DB has accumulated).
- **Default task settings in Quick Add (2026-11-16, FRONTEND ONLY: no backend, schema or database change; local, NOT committed):** `_quick/QuickAdd.tsx` has a "Default task settings" box above the rows (Default project, Default reporting manager, the same dropdown lists the server returns in `entryForm`, nothing hardcoded). Each row remembers per field (`projectCustom`, `managerCustom` on `QuickRow`) whether it follows the default or was picked by hand. Rules (pure, in `_quick/quickRows.ts`: `applyDefault`, `setRowChoice`, `resetRowChoice`, `inferDefaults`): choosing a default fills every row that FOLLOWS it; choosing a value on ONE row changes only that row and marks it "Custom" (unless it equals the default, then it follows); changing a default later never overwrites a Custom row (a custom row whose value equals the new default simply follows again); a new row, Remove-of-the-last-row and the rows left after a send start from the current defaults; Duplicate copies the flags; each field has its own "Reset to default" link; project and manager defaults are independent; description, hours, priority, progress and details are never touched by defaults. Row tags: green "Default" (only when a default is set and the row has a value) or amber "Custom" + "Reset to default". **The tag and reset button are OUTSIDE the `<label>`** (the label uses `htmlFor`): a button inside a label made the label belong to the button instead of the dropdown (found by the browser test). The data sent to the server is unchanged (`toPayload` still sends each row's own teamId and taggedManagerId; the flags never leave the screen): one record per task, routed only to its own manager. **Drafts: no schema change.** `task_drafts` stores each row's own project and manager, not which ones followed the default (adding a column would be a live migration), so on reopening `inferDefaults` works the default out again: the value most rows use (the first met wins a tie) becomes the default, rows with that value or none follow it, every other row is Custom; the rows keep exactly their saved values and changing the default afterwards still spares the Custom rows. The only difference from before saving: a row set by hand to the same value as the default reopens as "Default". The remembered last project/manager (browser `localStorage` `hrms-quickadd-last`) now only pre-fills the two default dropdowns (written when a DEFAULT is changed, no longer on every row change); it supersedes the earlier "last chosen in any row" rule. The same box also appears in Add Previous Day's Tasks (same component; no drafts there). Tests: `backend/tests/quick-add-logic.test.mts` is now 33 checks (16 new), real Chrome run (35 checks, kept outside the repo: 10 tasks with one project/manager, 8 mixed with overrides, default changed after rows and after reopening, new rows after a change, reset, duplicate, draft save/reopen, submit with per-manager notification counts, dark, phone); the earlier Quick Add browser run still passes except its one check for the replaced "last chosen" behaviour, previous-day browser run 28/28.
- **Progress "To Do" + required Blocked reason (built 2026-11-17, local, NOT committed; NO migration needed for the feature itself; one OPTIONAL migration for drafts, see below):** progress is now To Do / In Progress / Completed / Blocked everywhere (single form, Quick Add rows, the employee's list, the manager's Approvals page and Approval Centre, the employee's date view, Excel/CSV exports). **To Do is stored as the status the app always used for "not started": `daily_tasks.status = PENDING`** (`storeProgress("TODO")`; `progressOf("PENDING", false)` = `"TODO"`), so no column, table or status was added and NO existing task was converted: every task already stored as PENDING (e.g. assigned by a manager) simply READS "To Do" instead of "Not started" (the stored value is identical; tested). The API value `NOT_STARTED` is gone (responses say `TODO`; input `NOT_STARTED` is refused with 400); `frontend/app/dashboard/_progress/progress.ts` is the one place for labels/colours (`PROGRESS_LABEL`, `PROGRESS_CHIP_CLASS`, `PROGRESS_OPTIONS`, `normalProgress`). Defaults are unchanged (new tasks default to In Progress). Progress stays separate from approval (own chip, own field; changing progress never touches `approvalStatus`; the approval flow is untouched). A manager's OWN self-managed tasks (their own screen, "Not started") are unchanged. **Blocked needs a reason** (`checkBlockedReason`, 3 to 500 characters, trimmed): `POST /tasks` and every Quick Add row refuse Blocked without one (400 `BLOCKED_REASON_REQUIRED`, nothing saved, other Quick Add rows unaffected); `PATCH /tasks/:id` with `progress: BLOCKED` needs `blockedNote` (or the task's existing reason); a blocked task's reason can be reworded but never emptied; changing to any other progress clears the reason; a reason sent with another progress is ignored; editing only the hours of an OLD blocked task that has no reason is still allowed (no reason is forced onto old data), but choosing Blocked again asks for one. Screens: single form shows a required "Blocked reason" box; in the list, choosing Blocked opens the "Why is this task blocked?" dialog (`_progress/BlockedReasonModal.tsx`); each Quick Add row shows its own reason box. **Visible to the reporting manager:** the queue rows carry `progress` + `blockedNote`; Approvals shows an amber "Blocked: <reason>" callout on every row in every tab (an old blocked task shows "No reason was written") and a new **Progress filter** (All / To Do / In Progress / Completed / Blocked, with counts, narrows tabs, counts and list together like the Normal/Late filter). **History + audit:** every progress change (and a reworded reason) is written to `task_events` as kind UPDATE ("Progress: To Do -> Blocked. Blocked reason: ...") and to the audit log with before/after incl. the reason; a task starts with a CREATED event ("Submitted as Blocked. Blocked reason: ..."); the owner reads it at `GET /tasks/:id/history` (managers see the current reason on the row, HR/Admin the audit log; no manager-facing history screen). **Reports/exports:** `/team-attendance/productivity/tasks` rows have `progress` (TODO/IN_PROGRESS/COMPLETED/BLOCKED/CANCELLED) and `blockedReason` (only for Blocked); the Task productivity Excel "Tasks" sheet and the team workbook "Team Task Performance" sheet have a "Blocked reason" column right after Progress; the team summary sheet has a "To Do" count before In progress and Blocked; the per-employee sheets show the reason in the task line; `/tasks/range` (employee date view) carries progress and `blockedNote`. NOT done: To Do/Blocked counts in the Reports page columns, HR/Admin dashboard tiles and the Reports CSV (the Excel/CSV task sheets and the team workbook are covered). **Drafts:** `task_drafts` was created with a CHECK allowing only COMPLETED/IN_PROGRESS/BLOCKED and has no column for a blocked reason: until the OPTIONAL migration `20261117100000_task_drafts_allow_todo` (widens that one CHECK only: no column/table/row change, no Prisma model change, so the running code is unaffected before or after) is applied to the live database, saving a draft that holds a To Do task answers 409 `DRAFT_TODO_NOT_READY` with a clear message and the saved draft stays untouched; a draft never keeps the typed blocked reason (the screen says so when saving and when reopening: Blocked rows come back asking for it again). Tests: `backend/tests/task-entry-logic.test.mts` (23), `quick-add-logic.test.mts` (35), `task-progress.test.cjs` (47, throwaway DB: creation, required reason, edits, history, audit, manager view and privacy, Quick Add, old tasks untouched, exports, rated task protected, drafts before/after the migration and its down script), real Chrome run (40 checks: single form, list dialog, Quick Add, drafts, date view, manager filter and callout, real Excel download read back, dark, phone); existing tests that create Blocked tasks now send a reason (`quick-add-tasks` 45, `late-submissions` 59, `task-delete` 55, `task-approval-visibility` 35, `task-form-managers` 41, `hr-team-tasks` 38, `attendance-calendar` 43, `hr-visibility` 61 pass). `org-hierarchy-workflows` shows 108 passed / 18 failed with and without this change (checked against a clean copy of the committed code): its expectations predate the 12 and 14 Nov rules (HR cannot see waiting tasks, reporting lines may cross companies).
- **Automatic default project + reporting manager on the task forms (built 2026-11-17, NO migration, no schema change, local, NOT committed; SUPERSEDES the earlier rule "nothing is preselected" for the Daily Tasks forms):** Add Single Task and Quick Add now open with the project and the reporting manager already selected, worked out by the SERVER from the person's own records and sent with the form as `entryForm.defaults = { projectId, managerId, projectSource, managerSource }` (`GET /tasks`; `pickDefaults` in `tasks.entry.logic.ts`, `entryDefaults` in `tasks.entry.ts`; nothing hardcoded). Rules, decided field by field, never guessed: **project** = the person's PRIMARY active team (`team_members.isPrimary`, active membership, active team); with no primary team, their ONLY team; two teams and none primary, or two primaries, is ambiguous so the field stays empty; a team the form does not offer is never used. **manager** = the person's reporting manager (`users.reportsToId`) if the form offers that person (an active Manager or Admin who is someone's reporting manager or runs a project: an HR, Employee or suspended reporting manager is NOT offered, so no default); with no usable reporting manager, an additional reporting manager (`reporting_manager_links`) if exactly one is offered. A manager's own self-managed screen and a form that is not ready get all-null defaults. **The server still saves exactly what the client sends and still refuses a task with no project or no manager chosen** (`PROJECT_REQUIRED`, `TAGGED_MANAGER_REQUIRED`, `BAD_TAGGED_MANAGER`): defaults are only a pre-selection on the screen, so other API callers are unchanged, a task already sent keeps the manager it was tagged to when the reporting line changes later, and the NEXT task's default follows the new records. Single form (`tasks/page.tsx`): the choice is kept as `projectPick` / `taggedPick` (null = the person has not chosen, so it follows the default; "" = they chose "none"), the shown value is `pick ?? default`, small green hints "Your main team / Your team" and "Your reporting manager / Your additional reporting manager (you can change it)" appear only while the value equals the default, and after sending the form goes back to the defaults. Quick Add: the "Default task settings" box starts from the server defaults (`startingDefaults` in `_quick/quickRows.ts`: server default first, else the last choice remembered in this browser, each field on its own, only ids still on the lists) and says "Filled in from your team and your reporting manager" when they came from the records; rows follow it as before and can be changed one by one; the same applies to Add Previous Day's Tasks. Tests: `backend/tests/task-entry-logic.test.mts` (37, 14 new for `pickDefaults`), `quick-add-logic.test.mts` (38), `task-defaults.test.cjs` (21, throwaway DB: primary / only / ambiguous team, inactive membership and team, HR / suspended / Admin / additional reporting managers, defaults change when the records change, saved selection = what was chosen, server still refuses a missing project or manager, an earlier task keeps its manager), real Chrome run (21 checks: pre-selected single form with hints, send without touching the dropdowns, change both and the changed values are saved, form back to defaults, "none" respected, Quick Add box pre-filled and 3 tasks sent untouched, ambiguous team leaves the project empty, dark, phone); the other task suites and browser runs still pass (the older defaults browser run needed its Blocked rows to carry a reason, a result of the earlier Blocked-reason rule, not of this change).
- **Dashboard workbook: charts, employee navigation, one sheet per employee (built 2026-11-17, local, NOT committed; NO migration, NO schema change; one dependency change approved by the owner):** Reports & Analytics > Export reports (Manager, HR and Admin, the same people they already see) has a new button **Download dashboard workbook (.xlsx)** next to the unchanged "Download complete team report", the one-report Excel/CSV downloads and the CSVs (all untouched). Data = the existing read-only `GET /team-attendance/team-report` (same authorization: a Manager gets only the people who report to them, HR/Admin the companies they act on via `accessFor`, an Employee gets 403; nothing is written or recalculated; deleted tasks are already excluded). Two small additive fields were added to it: people carry `status` and `joinedOn`; tasks carry `submittedAt` (real submission time = `daily_tasks.createdAt`) and `late` (`{lateByDays, outsideWindow, reason}` from `task_late_details`, null for a normal task). Presets are the card's existing ones (Last 7, Last 15 default, Last 30, This month, Custom up to 93 days). **Workbook** (`reports/_team/dashboardWorkbook.ts`, pure builder `buildDashboardWorkbook`): sheet 1 **Dashboard** (title, report name, period, export time, scope line; 17 summary tiles that are `SUBTOTAL` formulas over the table so they and the charts follow table filters; the **Employee-wise overview** table with every employee in scope, even one with no records, each name an internal hyperlink to that employee's sheet; a small "employees with rated tasks" table; 8 charts), then one sheet per employee, then "Report info". Dashboard columns: Employee, ID, Designation, **Department (team)** (the HRMS has no department field: the employee's teams stand in), Company, Reporting manager, Employment status, Working days in period (present + half + absent + leave), days present/half/absent, Leave days, Late arrivals, Late remarks, Working/Productive/Break hours (decimal hours), Meeting participation (h), Tasks submitted/completed/pending approval/approved/rejected/blocked, Tasks rated, Average rating (rated tasks only), Data note, and three 1/0 helper columns for the tiles. **Employee sheets** (sections A to G, record lists are real Excel tables with filter buttons and unique names Att/Hrs/Brk/Tsk/Mtg/Lve + employee number, top 3 rows frozen, "Back to Dashboard" link in A2 that returns to that employee's row): A information, B attendance (every calendar day, "No record" shown as such, late remark, late approval / correction details, working duration), C per-day working/productive/break hours + number of breaks + break details (washroom and discussions are not breaks), D EVERY task (task date, submission date and time, description, project, assigned reporting manager, hours, priority, progress, blocked reason, approval, rating, feedback, reason sent back, late-submission indicator), E meetings and Team Huddles (scheduled vs actual join/end, own participation), F leave (type, duration, status, approver, decision), G summary. An empty list is not an empty table: it says "No ... in this period". Status colours by conditional formatting. Sheet names: ID + name, illegal characters removed, 31 characters, no edge apostrophe, unique (case-insensitive); employees with the same name show their ID on the Dashboard. **Accuracy rules kept:** no productivity percentage or new formula (only SUBTOTAL totals of the displayed columns); **blank = nothing recorded, 0 = recorded as none** (e.g. an employee with no attendance record has blank hours, never 0); ratings are averaged over RATED tasks only (unrated is never 0 or 5, and an employee with none is blank and left out of the rating chart); meeting participation is inside productive time, break time separate; pending tasks are never counted as approved or completed. **Charts** (the owner chose real Excel charts): `exceljs` cannot draw charts, so `reports/_team/chartXml.ts` writes the chart parts (DrawingML bar charts, horizontal, clustered or stacked) into the .xlsx after exceljs produced it, using **JSZip, now a direct frontend dependency** (`jszip@^3.10.2`, already installed inside exceljs, no new download). The 8 charts: attendance (stacked present/half/absent/leave), working vs productive hours, task completion (submitted vs completed), pending vs approved vs rejected (stacked), break hours, meeting participation, leave days, average task rating (separate helper table of rated employees, axis fixed 0 to 5; left out with an explanation when nobody was rated). Each has a title with the period, a legend, axis titles, cell-range references (so it follows the table and its filters; hidden rows are skipped, a blank is a gap not a zero) and a cached copy of its values. Height grows with the number of employees (24 px each) so 70 names stay readable. Nothing about comparing employees with different periods is hidden: the "Working days in period" column and a data note explain it. **NOT verified in desktop Excel** (no Excel on this machine; no LibreOffice): the file was validated structurally only (every part is well-formed XML, relationships and content types resolve, Excel tables' header cells equal their column names, table names/ids unique, chart element order follows the OOXML schema, every chart reference is a valid range whose cells match the cached values). **Open a downloaded file in Microsoft Excel once and report any repair prompt or odd chart.** Performance: 70 employees x 15 days x 2 tasks a day builds in about 6.5 s (about 580 KB). Tests: `backend/tests/dashboard-workbook.test.mts` (79, no DB: sheet order and names, blank vs zero, hyperlinks both ways, tiles, employee sheet sections and tables, late flag, unrated blank, colours, chart parts and XML, references vs cells, no-rating case, empty and 70-employee reports), `dashboard-report-data.test.cjs` (27, throwaway DB: Manager/HR/Admin scope incl. HR with a second company, everybody listed even with no records, new fields, deleted task absent, late flag, reading changes nothing), a real Chrome run (38 checks: real downloads as Manager, HR and Admin read back, links, tables, charts, the existing complete report and CSV unchanged, phone, Employee refused) and a structural validator over every part of the downloaded files; `team-report` (43), `hr-visibility`, `hr-filters-reports`, `task-approval-visibility` still pass; `team-report-book.test.mts` had one column number updated (the Rating columns moved one place right when the "Blocked reason" column was added in the To Do work).
- **Early Logout Policy, 2nd and 4th Saturday (built 2026-11-18, local, NOT committed or pushed; migration `20261118100000_early_logout_policy` NOT applied to any real database, only the throwaway one; owner's choices: a 4 PM-or-later logout = Present, ONE setting for all companies):** on the 2nd and 4th Saturday of a month (found by `earlyLogout.logic.ts`, 5th/1st/3rd Saturdays and every other day never) the working day ends early (default 16:00). It is a SHORTER WORKING DAY, not a week-off or holiday. NEW TABLE ONLY `early_logout_policy` (one row id `global`: enabled, logoutTime, startDate); no row or no table = policy OFF and everything behaves exactly as before (`loadEarlyLogoutPolicy()` never throws, re-checks a missing table every 30 s). **Start date is required to switch it on and days before it are never affected** (attendance is recomputed on read, so without a start date old Saturdays would silently change); nothing in `work_sessions`/biometric/audit is ever written (tested byte for byte). Engine (`attendance/attendance.service.ts`): on a policy day, a person who is a working day (not holiday, week-off, leave or outside employment: those still win) whose LAST LOGOUT is at or after the time (IST, to the minute: 16:00 yes, 15:59 no, past midnight yes) is PRESENT whatever the hours; leaving earlier falls back to the normal hours rule (full 8 h / half 4 h). Literal consequence of the owner's rule: someone who clocks in at 3:30 PM and out at 4:05 PM also counts Present. `AttendanceDay` gained `earlyLogoutDay/Time/Label`. Attendance report (`/team-attendance/attendance`): rows gain `earlyLogoutDay`, `earlyLogoutLabel`, the scheduled end shown (`shiftEnd`) and the early-departure minutes use min(shift end, policy time) on those days; the "Remarks" column (screen, CSV, Excel) carries "Early Logout – 4:00 PM". Calendar (`/attendance-calendar`): rows carry the label and the response has `earlyLogout { enabled, logoutTime, label, dates[] }` so the page labels the dates even when no row exists (future); the calendar's own 09:45/10:15 arrival rule is unchanged (it never looks at logout). Reports target (`reports.service.ts`): on a policy day the expected productive hours are scaled by (min(shift end, policy time) − start) / (shift end − start) from the person's own shift (10:00-19:00 → 6/9 → 8 h becomes 5 h 20 min); unknown/overnight/already-shorter shifts are never scaled; stored hours are never recalculated. Scheduler: the LOW_PRODUCTIVITY alert uses the same scaled target. Admin setting: `GET/PUT /api/admin/early-logout-policy` (HR can read, only ADMIN can change; time 12:00-18:59; audited), card "Early logout policy" on Admin > Thresholds (`_attendance/EarlyLogoutCard.tsx`). NOT changed on purpose: payroll rules other than what the shared engine now says (a policy Saturday at 4 PM is paid Present), the live clock-in screen's productive target (`tracker.service`, timesheet `productiveHours`), shift definitions, other days. Tests: `tests/early-logout-logic.test.mts` (10, no DB), `tests/early-logout.test.cjs` (57, throwaway DB: before/after the migration, permissions, validation, exactly/before/after 4 PM, history before the start date, 3rd/5th Saturday, other company, holiday and week-off priority, Reports targets, Manager/HR/employee views, off again, sessions unchanged; drop `early_logout_policy` and re-run), real Chrome run (18: Admin card, calendar label, day panel, dark, phone, HR no card); `attendance-calendar` 43, `attendance-leave-priority` 36, `hr-filters-reports` 35, `hr-visibility` 61, `activity-policy` 81, `late-remark-report` 34, `arrival-logic` 39 still pass. Not done: the Excel/CSV attendance exports carry the label only in Remarks (no separate column), team workbook sheets were not given the label, the live clock-in screen still shows the normal 8 h target on those Saturdays, not opened in desktop Excel.
- **Early Logout Policy follow-up after the 14 Nov 2026 report (2026-11-18, local, NOT committed; same unapplied migration `20261118100000_early_logout_policy`, edited in place because it was never applied anywhere real):** ROOT CAUSE of "second Saturday still Half-Day" = the policy was only built locally; LIVE has no `early_logout_policy` table, so live still used the hours rule (full 8 h, half 4 h). Read-only check of live 14 Nov: 15 sessions, nobody logged out before 16:00, 9 people (5.3-6.6 h worked, out 16:00-18:50) read Half day under the hours rule, all 15 Present under the policy; no salary slips exist for Nov 2026; 2 correction requests for that day are PENDING (untouched); 5 LOW_PRODUCTIVITY + 2 BREAK_OVERAGE alert rows already stored for the day (history, left alone); NOBODY has a shift (0 of 62; only the Avatar company has a default shift 10:00-19:00). **Historical correction needs NO data edit**: attendance is recomputed on read, so applying the migration, deploying and saving the policy with "Applies from" 2026-11-14 fixes 14 Nov (earlier days unchanged). - **HR who is an employee's reporting manager approves that employee's tasks (built 2026-11-17, local, NOT committed; NO migration; owner's decision for Prachi TCS-05, Sadhana TCS-06, Sayli TCS-07 under Sonali Gouda CP-03; supersedes "HR never approves" ONLY for this case):** a rule from the data, no names hardcoded. `eligibleManagers()` (tasks.entry.ts) adds the employee's own reporting manager when that person is an ACTIVE HR (role shown as HR); any other HR is not offered and the server refuses to tag them (`BAD_TAGGED_MANAGER`), so the Add Task form pre-selects the HR boss through the normal default rule (reporting manager offered on the list). Approving/rejecting/deleting (`assertCanReviewTask` strict): HR may decide a task ONLY when it is tagged to them and the tag is still active; an untagged or other-tagged task is never HR's, even when HR is the reporting manager. The queue's `canAct` for HR = tagged to them (no legacy reporting-line fallback); `pendingTaskVisibleTo("HR", canAct)` shows HR a waiting task only when canAct. Notifications already went to the tagged person (the `withoutHr` filter only applies to untagged tasks). A submitted task stays with whoever it was tagged to if the reporting line later changes (HR still decides it; the employee is just no longer offered HR for new tasks). Everyone else is unchanged: other HR and non-tagged Admins view only, another Manager cannot decide it. UI: new HR sidebar entry **Task Approvals** (`/dashboard/approvals`, previously reachable only by URL or a notification link); the page already honours `canAct`. Tests: `tests/hr-task-approval.test.cjs` (30, throwaway DB), `task-entry-logic.test.mts` (41), real Chrome (9: sidebar link, Approve works and records HR, another HR sees nothing and has no button); `task-approval-visibility` 35, `hr-team-tasks` 38, `task-form-managers` 41, `task-defaults` 21 (one expectation updated: an HR reporting manager is now the default), `quick-add-tasks` 45, `late-submissions` 59, `task-delete` 55, `task-progress` 47, `hr-admin-boundary` 25, `admin-hr-separation` 110 pass.

Company, designation, logins, attendance, tasks and leave untouched. - **Edit Task and correction requests (built 2026-11-18, local, NOT committed; NO migration, NO schema change; owner chose "no migration" and, for corrections, "everything except date and manager" and "clear the rating and ask for a re-rating"):** employees edit their OWN submitted tasks. **Pending or sent back (and not rated):** `POST /api/tasks/:id/edit` (code `tasks.edit.service.ts` `editMyTask`) changes title, details, project, reporting manager, date, hours, priority, progress and Blocked reason with the SAME validators as the submit form (project/manager lists, `parseHours`, `checkBlockedReason`, and the backdated-task window: a date inside the company window is free and marked late, outside it needs a written reason, never past 14 days, never in the future; moving back to today removes the late record). It stays PENDING; a sent-back task goes back to PENDING (the old rejection reason is kept in the history, not on the task). A changed manager must be on the employee's eligible list (HR boss rule included); the NEW manager is notified (TASK_PROPOSED) and the OLD one is told it moved (TASK_MOVED). **Approved or rated:** no direct edit (409 `USE_CORRECTION`); `POST /api/tasks/:id/correction { reason, <fields> }` creates a request that changes nothing until the tagged manager decides `POST /api/tasks/review/:id/correction { requestId, approve, note }` (same strict deciders as approving a task: HR only when tagged to them, Admin only when tagged, never the owner); a correction can NOT change the date or the approving manager (400 `CORRECTION_FIELD_NOT_ALLOWED`); on approval the new details apply, the approval (who and when) is untouched, and a rating is cleared (copied into the history first) so the task returns to the manager's to-rate list; a decline needs a note. Manager list: `GET /api/tasks/corrections/pending` (only what that person may decide). History: `GET /api/tasks/:id/changes` (owner, people who can review it, HR/Admin in their company scope; others 404). **Where the history lives (no migration):** every edit and correction step is ONE row in the existing append-only `task_events` (kind EDITED, the only extra kind its CHECK allows) whose note is `TASKCHG1:` + JSON (old and new values, approval before/after, manager before/after, reason, decision, previous rejection, cleared rating); written in the SAME transaction as the change. A request is "pending" until a later row names its id (`deriveRequests`, pure, `tasks.edit.logic.ts`). Rows also appear in the audit log (before/after). WORKAROUND TO REMEMBER: if a real table is ever added, move these rows; any other reader of `task_events` ignores notes without the prefix. **Safety:** per-task advisory lock + a version token (`version` = later of the task's and its entry details' `updatedAt`, sent back with every edit; stale = 409 `EDITED_ELSEWHERE`) + a guarded update that re-checks the approval status, so two edits at once, a double click, or an edit racing the manager's approval cannot both win or half-save; one pending correction per task (second = 409); an assigned task (`assignedById`) and a manager's own self-managed task cannot be edited through this. Rows from `/tasks`, `/tasks/range` and the review queue carry `version`, `editRoute` (DIRECT | CORRECTION | null), `edited {at,count}` and `pendingCorrection`. The older `PATCH /tasks/:id` (progress/hours on an approved task, "Mark done") is unchanged. Screens (`dashboard/_taskedit/`): Edit / Request correction button on every card of Daily Tasks (today's lists and the date view), dialog with prefilled fields and a confirmation step, "Edited · date time" label with History, a manager panel "Correction requests" on Approvals (Approve / Decline with note), Edited label + History on every Approvals row (HR and Admin can read it, with no approve action). Tests: `backend/tests/task-edit-logic.test.mts` (10), `task-edit.test.cjs` (91, throwaway DB: pending/rejected/approved/rated, someone else's task, manager and project change, backdated dates, concurrent edits and edit-vs-approval races, double submit, manager approval and decline, history, audit, Reports rated count, permissions), real Chrome (32: edit with confirmation, Edited label, history, manager view, correction request and approval, stale conflict, HR no action, dark, phone); `hr-task-approval` 30, `task-approval-visibility` 35, `hr-team-tasks` 38, `task-form-managers` 41, `task-defaults` 21, `quick-add-tasks` 45, `late-submissions` 59, `task-delete` 55, `task-progress` 47 still pass. NOT done: correction requests for tasks with no tagged manager route to the project/reporting-line reviewer only; no separate HR page listing every edit (HR reads one task's history from Approvals).
- **Manager-assigned tasks inside Daily Tasks (built 2026-11-17, NO migration, NO sidebar item, local, NOT committed; owner chose "reuse daily_tasks", "leave existing alone", "separate counts only" but the Reports counts are NOT built, see below):** the existing Daily Tasks page gets a segmented control. Manager (`selfManagedMode`): My own tasks / Employee submitted tasks (the existing Approvals screen embedded) / Assigned by me (Assign task button, edit, filters, confirm or send back). Employee (and HR/Admin on their own page): My submitted tasks / Assigned by manager (badge = open count). Code `backend/src/modules/tasks/tasks.assigned{,.logic}.ts`, routes `/api/tasks/assigned/{mine,by-me,people,:id/history|progress|comment|edit|confirm|send-back|cancel}` + `POST /api/tasks/assigned`, UI `frontend/app/dashboard/_assigned/`. **Storage: an assigned task is a `daily_tasks` row with `assignedById` set.** SEPARATION LEVER: the shared `activeTasks()` filter (tasks.deleted.ts) now ALSO returns `assignedById: null`, so every reader that spreads it (approval queue, ratings, productivity, reports, task export, calendar/attendance counts, the employee's own list) leaves assigned tasks out; the new `activeAssigned()` is used only by the assigned workflow. **Any NEW query over `daily_tasks` that should mean "employee-submitted" must keep `...(await activeTasks())`; a query that must see assigned rows uses `activeAssigned()`; order matters in a spread (put it BEFORE your own `assignedById`).** Workflow: status PENDING = To do, IN_PROGRESS, COMPLETED = waiting for confirmation until `reviewedAt` is set (manager confirmed); send back = IN_PROGRESS with the reason in `reviewNote`; remove only while To do (status CANCELLED, row kept). Never touches `approvalStatus` (stored APPROVED), no rating. History, comments and the optional due date are rows in the existing append-only `task_events` (note prefix `ASGN1:`, same workaround as the task edit history). Only MANAGERS assign (to people they manage, never to another manager; HR/Admin cannot through this route); only the assigner confirms/edits/removes; only the owner moves progress; both can comment; the other side is notified (types ASSIGNED_TASK_*; popups for done/confirmed/sent back); guarded updates make two simultaneous confirmations record once. The older `POST /tasks/review/assign` still works and its rows now use this workflow (live had exactly 1 such row, from 2026-10-16, untouched; it now appears under Assigned and left the submitted lists). Not built: assigned counts in Reports/Excel (they are simply no longer inside the submitted counts), a manager-facing audit/history for HR, a due-date column (due date lives in the history rows), carry-forward. Tests: `tests/assigned-tasks.test.cjs` (75, throwaway DB), real Chrome run (29 checks: three manager tabs, no new sidebar item, assign, start, comment, complete, send back, confirm, live updates, dark, phone); `task-edit` 91 (one expectation changed to 404/403), `task-approval-visibility` 35, `hr-team-tasks` 38, `task-form-managers` 41, `task-defaults` 21, `hr-task-approval` 30, `task-progress` 47, `hr-visibility` 61, `attendance-calendar` 43 still pass; `quick-add-tasks`, `task-delete` and `late-submissions` need their tables dropped first (they apply migrations part-way), not re-run.
- **Tasks & Performance vs Daily Tasks (2026-11-18, FRONTEND + notification links, NO migration, local, NOT committed; owner's choices: approvals live only in Daily Tasks for every role, analytics computed in the browser, overdue = open and older than N days):** `/dashboard/approvals` (sidebar "Tasks & Performance" for Manager and HR, "Task Monitoring" for Admin) is now READ-ONLY analytics (`approvals/page.tsx`, rules in the pure `approvals/analytics.ts`, charts `AnalyticsCharts.tsx`): counts by approval and by progress, employee-wise and project-wise tables, workload, completion history, average rating of RATED tasks only (unrated is never 0), overdue list, one employee's history, period/employee/project filters (+ the shared HR filter bar for HR/Admin), Excel download (By employee / By project / Tasks / Report info), a manager's assigned tasks counted separately, plus the repetitive-task alerts and summary. It reads the SAME call as the queue (`GET /tasks/review/queue`), so figures match Daily Tasks. NO approve, send back, rate, delete or assign button there. **The approval queue is the component `approvals/ReviewQueue.tsx`** (the old page, unchanged) shown in Daily Tasks > "Employee submitted tasks"; HR and Admin now also get that tab (they only act on tasks tagged to them). Daily Tasks opens a tab from `?tab=submitted|assigned` (notification and dashboard links use it; `backend tasks.notify.ts MANAGER_LINK`). HR's sidebar label changed from "Task Approvals" to "Tasks & Performance". Waiting-for-approval tasks show only to the manager who decides them (earlier visibility rule), so HR's "waiting" figure is only what is tagged to HR. **Overdue for submitted tasks = open (not completed/rejected) and task day more than N days ago, N chosen on the page (2/3/5/7/14, default 3, remembered in the browser); I picked the default 3 because no number was given.** Tests: `task-analytics-logic.test.mts` (22), real Chrome `analytics-e2e` (45).
- **Repetitive task flags, part 2 (2026-11-18, local, NOT committed; migration `20261118120000_repetition_notes` NEW TABLE ONLY, NOT applied to live):** `repetition_flag_notes` (kind EXPLANATION / MANAGER_COMMENT / HR_OBSERVATION / ADMIN_NOTE / ESCALATION_ADMIN, visibility EMPLOYEE / STAFF / HR_ADMIN, append-only trigger, one ESCALATION_ADMIN per flag); NO column was added to the live flag tables, so code deployed before the migration keeps working (explaining and escalating to Admin answer 503 until it is applied; comments fall back to the first-version events). Rules in the pure `tasks/repetition.access.ts`: the employee (and the subject of a flag, whatever their role) sees why it was flagged, the matching tasks and dates, the status and outcome and their OWN explanations; never reviewer notes, reviewer names or any escalation. A manager sees manager comments + employee explanations but NOT HR observations, Admin notes or the escalation to Admin; HR and Admin see everything in their companies. Flow: detection notifies the employee (REPETITIVE_TASK_ALERT, link Daily Tasks) and the reporting manager + HR + Admin of the company once (REPETITIVE_TASK_FLAG, link /dashboard/approvals, dedupe key per flag); the employee explains (`POST /tasks/repetition/flags/:id/explain`, manager notified); manager reviews/comments and escalates to HR; **HR escalates to Admin with a required reason** (`POST .../escalate-admin`, notifies the other HR/Admin, not a judgement); Admin resolves/closes; the employee is told each new status (label only). `GET /tasks/repetition/flags?mine=1` = the person's own alerts. Nobody reviews their own flag (403). A flag never changes a task, rating, pay, attendance or productivity (tested byte for byte). Screens: employee card in Daily Tasks (`EmployeeRepetitionAlert.tsx`), reviewer card in Tasks & Performance (`RepetitionAlerts.tsx`, buttons from the server's `can`, every note labelled with who can read it). No HR/Admin dashboard tile was added (they get the bell + the card). Tests: `repetition-access.test.mts` (20), `repetition-visibility.test.cjs` (69, applies both migrations itself), `repetition-flags.test.cjs` (68, updated), real Chrome `repetition-v2-e2e` (36).
- **Query Desk with department requests (built 2026-11-18, local, NOT committed; migration `20261119100000_query_desk_departments` NEW TABLES ONLY, applied to a throwaway local DB, NOT to Neon):** "Complaint Centre" is renamed **Query Desk** for every role (sidebar, page, titles); the Admin menu gained a "Query Desk" item (same name, no new section) so the Admin can give final approval. The old queries/complaints (tickets, corrections) are untouched and live in the first tab "Queries and complaints"; the new second tab **Department requests** (`?tab=dept&id=`; notification links use it) is the approval workflow. Code `modules/queries/dept.{logic,service}.ts` + routes under `/api/queries/dept/*` (fixed paths registered BEFORE `/:id`), UI `queries/_dept/DeptRequests.tsx`. **No department master existed** (teams are projects), so owner chose a NEW `departments` table (per company, unique name ignoring capitals, Admin creates/hides and picks members in "Manage departments") + `department_members`. Workflow: employee raises (title, description, department REQUIRED and must be an active department of their OWN company, priority Low/Medium/High/Urgent, optional file) -> **reporting manager** (only `users.reportsToId` when that person is an ACTIVE MANAGER; none/Admin boss = no manager step, straight to Admin) approves or rejects (reason 3+) -> **Admin** final approval/rejection (reason 3+) -> only then the tagged department is notified and sees it -> a member **accepts** (one winner, guarded update) -> owner sets Not Started / In Progress / On Hold / Completed (On Hold and Completed need a note; start and completion times kept) -> employee **Confirm resolved** or **Reopen** with a reason (status Reopened, owner kept, dept + manager + Admin told). Two SEPARATE columns: `approvalStatus` (PENDING_MANAGER, PENDING_ADMIN, APPROVED, REJECTED) and `workStatus` (null until approved, then AWAITING_ACTION, NOT_STARTED, IN_PROGRESS, ON_HOLD, COMPLETED, REOPENED); a DB CHECK forbids a work status without approval. **Approver rule (owner's choice: the employee's mapped Admin only):** the Admin ABOVE the manager (manager's `reportsToId`), or the requester's own boss when there is no manager step, is stored in `adminApproverIds` when the query is raised (later line changes do not move it); ONLY that Admin can decide, other Admins/HR only monitor. **My safety net, say if unwanted:** when no Admin is mapped, every active Admin who acts on the company is asked (the CREATED event says so), otherwise the request would be stuck. The Admin can never skip the manager step (409 `WAITING_FOR_MANAGER`). Concurrent double-clicks record ONE decision (guarded `updateMany`). Reassign: owner or an Admin of the company, new owner must be an active member of that department and not the requester. Visibility (pure rules in `dept.logic.ts`, 39 tests): requester; asked manager / manager of the requester's team / named Admins / Admin and HR of the company (monitor only, HR is never an approver); department members ONLY once approved; unseen = 404. Notes carry a visibility: ALL, APPROVERS (manager/Admin comments, hidden from employee and department; rejection reasons are ALL), DEPT (hidden from the employee). History = append-only `dept_request_events` (DB trigger), every row has actor + time. Notifications reuse the inbox with `dedupeKey = dq:<event id>:<type>` (new `DEPT_QUERY_*` types, labels in `_notifications/types.ts`); the sidebar badge now also counts department requests waiting for you. Admins do not see "New department request" (they have no manager chain). Before the migration lists answer `{ready:false}`, writes 503 `FEATURES_NOT_READY`, everything else is as before. Tests: `tests/dept-logic.test.mts` (39), `tests/dept-requests.test.cjs` (135, throwaway DB: the whole Ritika/IT example, rejection at both stages, wrong department tagging, unauthorised access, reassignment, reopen, double-click and notification duplicates, old tickets still work), real Chrome `dept-e2e` (26: five people, light/dark/phone). Not built: Admin re-tagging a department during approval; attachments are per comment/progress update, not a separate list; the legacy ticket screen has no department field. The throwaway DB cluster had to be rebuilt this session (Windows temp cleanup removed its files).
- **Query Desk form redesign (2026-11-18, local; NO new migration):** department tagging is reachable from the normal "Raise a query" form (first Category option "Request to a department", then Continue) as well as from the Department requests tab. New form `queries/_dept/RaiseForm.tsx`: a read-only "Your details" card (name, employee ID, company, reporting manager, who gives final approval) from the new `GET /queries/dept/form-preview` (`formPreview` in `dept.service.ts`; it shares ONE `routeFor()` with the real submit, so the preview can never disagree with where the request actually goes), per-field validation messages, searchable department list, four priority buttons with colour dots, attachment type/size checks with an image thumbnail or file card and a Remove button, and a confirmation screen with the unique reference (DQ-000123), department, priority and who decides first. The six department names in the owner's list are NOT hardcoded: an Admin creates them under Manage departments (none exist on live until then). Tests: browser `dept-form-e2e` (32 checks); backend unchanged (135).
- **Today dashboard follows ONE selected date (2026-11-18, FRONTEND ONLY, local):** `TodayView.tsx` now derives a single `selectedDate` (the dropdown's Today / Yesterday / one custom date; a longer range has none). A day BEFORE today is a recorded view: banner + "Back to today", welcome line says "Viewing yesterday, <date>", the Clock-in card shows that day's stored figures from `/tracker/timesheet` (COMPLETED / NOT CLOCKED OUT / ON LEAVE / holiday / DAY OFF / NO CLOCK-IN, clock-in and out times, time on site = productive + break + washroom, break budget, washroom, discussion, Team Huddle) with NO live timer and NO Break / Clock-out / Clock in buttons, the live huddle join card, meeting invites and the today-only "Today's tasks waiting for approval" card are hidden, and the manager's Team Huddle section requests `/meetings/huddles/overview?date=<day>` (that endpoint already took a date). Multi-day ranges keep the live card and label it "Live · today". Planned hours, Worked hours, Breakdown and My timesheets already followed the range. Not covered: a past day that HAS a huddle record (checked at request level only).
- **Meetings with a manager's cross-company team (2026-11-19, NO migration, local, backend + one frontend list):** a MANAGER's invite list (`GET /meetings/people`) and the invite check (`checkAttendees`) now use ONE rule, `invitableWhere`: the same people Attendance shows (`visibleUserIds`: projects he runs, direct and extra reports) in ANY company, minus himself; before, the list was "everyone in my own company", so his people of another company (e.g. Take Health) were missing and the own-company people who did not report to him were offered. HR/Admin/others keep their own-company list. A meeting still belongs to the organizer's company (`meetings.platformId`), so every lookup an INVITED person makes must not filter by the person's own company: `listMine` (list + counts), `getOne`, `join`, `respond`, `history` (own), minutes `meetingFor` now use organizer-or-invited only; a manager's correction stores the participation row with the PERSON's company and notifies them under it. Organizer-only paths still require the organizer's company. Frontend: the people dropdown is taller (the old ~4-row window looked like "only 4"), no 50-name cap, a "N people available" line. Tests: `tests/meeting-cross-company.test.cjs` (36, throwaway DB; it found the missed `listMine` filter). `huddle-attendance` (11 failures), `admin-meeting-participation` (2) and `huddle-productive` (6) fail identically on the code BEFORE this change (stale expectations: accepting a live huddle already joins it; 12-hour meeting limit), so they need updating, not this change.
- **Payroll lists every eligible employee in the companies the HR/Admin acts on (2026-11-19, NO migration, local, not committed; SUPERSEDES "Payroll: own company only"):** ROOT CAUSE of "HR sees only 5": `payroll.service.ts` filtered `platformId: viewer.platformId` (the older own-company rule), so HR Sonali (CP-03, Colab Platforms, which has exactly 5 active people, all-companies grant) saw one company although company access gave her all. NOW one rule, `reach()` (`accessFor`/`scopeWhere`/`canAccess`), is used by the month list, upload, publish, publish-month, delete, download, generate, release, unrelease, review, deduction, approve and the payslip PDF; an HR/Admin with no grant still sees only their own company (tested); the old owner-only cross-company switch (`viewerIsOwner`) is no longer used in payroll. `GET /payroll/month` returns the people of ALL companies in scope, no `take`/paging: ACTIVE people plus anyone who already has a slip that month (an exited person's old slip stays visible; suspended/invited/candidate with no slip are not in the cycle); rows carry `company`, `companyId`, `employmentStatus`, `payroll {ready, status READY|SALARY_MISSING, reason}` (never the amount) and totals `employees`, `eligible`, `setupRequired`, `companies[]`, `uploaded`, `published` (all of the same list). **A person with no (or zero) `users.monthlySalary` is shown as "Payroll setup required · salary details missing", not hidden:** generation skips them with reason "Payroll setup required: no monthly salary set" (was "No monthly salary set"), and a NEW upload for them answers 400 `PAYROLL_SETUP_REQUIRED` (replacing an existing slip is still allowed); only an ADMIN can set a salary (Edit Employee), so HR sees who is missing but cannot fix it. Generation with no `platformId` now covers every company in scope (one attendance pass per company; slips are stored under the EMPLOYEE's company, never the viewer's); the department picker lists teams of every company in scope ("Team · Company" when more than one). One slip per person per month stays enforced (upload replaces, generate updates the DRAFT, approved slips are skipped; tested twice-run). Unrelease deletes the PAYSLIP_PUBLISHED notifications by slip key, not by the viewer's company. UI (`dashboard/payroll/page.tsx`): summary "N employees · N ready for payroll · N uploaded · N released", an amber "N need payroll setup" chip, a Company filter when more than one company, company under each name, an amber chip + disabled Upload button for setup-required people, and friendly error text.
