- **Query Desk: permanent Delete (built 2026-11-19, NO migration, local, NOT committed; owner's choices: remove permanently, everyone who can see a query may delete it):** a red Delete button on every row and a "Delete query / Delete request" button in the footer of the open query, for BOTH the classic queries (`POST /api/queries/:id/delete`, `deleteQuery`) and department requests (`POST /api/queries/dept/:id/delete`, `deleteRequest`). Rules: the body must carry `confirm: true` (else 400 `CONFIRM_REQUIRED`); visibility is the existing one (`load()` -> `canViewQuery` / `canView`), so an employee can delete only their own, a manager the queries of his reporting team, HR/Admin the companies they act on, a department member only an APPROVED request they can see; an id the person cannot see answers 404. The old attendance-correction ticket (category ATTENDANCE_CORRECTION) cannot be deleted (400 `CANNOT_DELETE_CORRECTION`; it is evidence for an attendance change and has its own screen). The row is really deleted in one transaction (replies, internal notes, `query_meta`, `query_events` / `dept_request_events` go by cascade; the history triggers only block UPDATE) together with every notification whose link contains the id; attachment files are removed from the private store afterwards (best effort). **Because it is permanent, a readable copy is written to the immutable audit log first** (`auditNote` before: reference, subject/title, first 280 chars of the description, status, who raised it, reply/note/attachment/history counts, dates; action DELETE, module Queries, catalog lines added in `auditCatalog.ts`). Risk accepted by the owner: a manager or HR can erase an employee's query, including an HR matter; only that summary survives. Screens: `queries/DeleteQuery.tsx` (`DeleteButton`, `DeleteDialog` with "This cannot be undone" and Cancel / Delete permanently), used in `queries/page.tsx` and `queries/_dept/DeptRequests.tsx`; rows became `li` flex with a separate button (no button inside a button). Tests: `tests/query-delete.test.cjs` (33, throwaway DB: confirm rule, own / manager / HR / Admin, not-visible = 404, legacy correction refused, replies and history gone, notifications gone, audit copy, department requests); `dept-requests` 135 and `dept-logic` 39 still pass; real Chrome (19: dialog, Cancel, delete from list and from inside, department requests, manager / HR / Admin see the button, dark, phone).
- **Fix: Employee Summary "Late arrivals" showed 0 (2026-11-19, FRONTEND ONLY, no migration, local, NOT committed):** ROOT CAUSE in `reports/_team/teamReportBook.ts` `summaryOf` (feeds the team workbook's per-employee sheets and the dashboard workbook's tiles/columns): `lateLogins` counted rows with `lateMinutes > 0` (the shift + grace rule), but nobody has a shift on live, so `lateMinutes` is empty even on days the daily rows classify as Late by the 09:45 arrival rule; `lateRemarks` counted `!!lateReason`. The "Leave and Late Remarks" sheet in the SAME workbook already used the arrival rule, so the two disagreed. FIX: ONE rule in `reports/_team/lateRemark.ts`: `isLateArrival(row)` = arrival Late or Half Day (the arrival rule is the authority; the shift-based `lateMinutes` decides ONLY when a row carries no arrival classification at all) and `hasTypedRemark(row)` = a non-blank typed reason; a remark never makes or prevents a late arrival, each day is one row so nothing is counted twice; HR-approved corrections already change the check-in time the classification is made from. Used by `summaryOf` and the late-arrival rows of the sheet. Half Day arrivals (after 10:15) count as late arrivals too. NOT changed: the Reports page "Late days" and My performance "Late arrivals" read `/reports` `lateDays` (the stored shift-based `wasLateLogin`), which follows the payroll/alert rule and can still differ from the calendar's arrival rule (known, documented earlier). Test: `tests/late-arrival-summary.test.mts` (20, no DB: the owner's 09:34 / 10:01 / 09:57 example gives 2 late and 0 remarks, remark vs no remark, blank spaces, HR-corrected both ways, a 31-day month, sheet and summary agree); `team-report-book` 25, `dashboard-workbook` 79, `late-remark-text` 10, `report-xlsx` 30 still pass.
- **Dashboard workbook: full reconciliation + cold-start rating bug (2026-11-19, backend one-file fix, no migration, local, NOT committed):** `backend/tests/dashboard-workbook-e2e.test.mts` (86 checks, throwaway DB on 54329 + backend on 5012): seeds the team, calls the real `/team-attendance/team-report`, builds the real workbook with `buildDashboardWorkbook`, reads the .xlsx back and checks every figure against independent SQL on the raw rows (present/half/absent/leave days, late arrivals and remarks, productive and break hours, meeting hours, every task count, rated count and average, leave taken), the summary tiles (formula ranges point at the right column; cached values equal the column totals, read from the raw XML because exceljs drops a cached 0), each employee sheet's section G against the dashboard row, the charts' cached values against the table cells, and the late-arrival rule on the data. ALL PASS. Findings: (1) **REAL BUG FIXED:** the first report request after a server start returned NO ratings (rated tasks 0, average blank): `ratingReady()` (`tasks.rating.ts`) set its "checking" stamp and a concurrent caller arriving during that first check was told "not ready". Now concurrent callers share the one in-flight check. **The same copy-pasted pattern (`readyCheckedAt < 15_000`, answer false while another caller's first check runs) exists in 18 other files and is NOT fixed: companyAccess (an HR/Admin's first request could be limited to their own company), tasks.deleted (deleted tasks counted), tasks.entry, tasks.late, lateRemarks, leaveFlow, managerStep, queries, participation, photos, policies, preferences, supervisor, employeeCode, management, tasks.bulk, tasks.self.** Fix them the same way if the owner agrees (cold-start window only, silent). (2) Not bugs, by design: a day under 8 productive hours counts as a Half day (the seeded 7.5 h days show as half days), an employee invited to meetings but joining none shows a recorded 0 meeting hours (blank only when never invited), a person with no sessions still has attendance rows (absent days), so only hours and ratings are blank. (3) exceljs reads `[h]:mm` durations as Dates and a cached 0 as missing: test-reader quirks, the file is right.
- **Network access policy: sign-in and sessions only from an authorised IP (built 2026-11-19, NO migration, local, NOT committed, NOT deployed; the live server has NO setting for it, so nothing changes until the owner edits `backend/.env` IN THE EDITOR):** owner's address `206.84.235.169`; owner's choices: monitor first then enforce, server setting (not a screen), existing Audit log, no exceptions for any role. SETTINGS (read from the environment on every check): `LOGIN_IP_MODE` = `off` (or unset: nothing checked or logged) | `monitor` (nobody blocked; every sign-in and every request from a non-allowed address is logged as "would be blocked") | `enforce`; any other value = monitor (loudly, never silently off). `LOGIN_ALLOWED_IPS` = comma-separated addresses or IPv4 ranges (a /0 or anything wider than /8 is refused). **Enforce with no valid allowed address does NOT enforce** (it would lock out Admins too): it behaves as monitor and logs the problem on the server console. Code: `lib/ipPolicy.ts` (pure rules: modes, list, `normalizeIp` incl. `::ffff:` IPv4, `ipBehindProxy` = Express's own hop rule for sockets), `lib/securityLog.ts` (writes to `audit_logs`, module `Security`, action LOGIN, never throws), enforcement in THREE places: `POST /auth/login` (checked BEFORE the password, so a wrong address cannot probe passwords or learn which accounts exist; 403 `{error: <exact message>, code: "IP_NOT_ALLOWED"}`; no token is issued), `requireAuth` (every authenticated request, so a token taken elsewhere stops working), and the Socket.IO handshake. The address is `clientIp(req)` = `req.ip` with `trust proxy` = `TRUST_PROXY_HOPS` (default 1): counted from the proxy side, so a header the client writes cannot choose its address (tested: `X-Forwarded-For: <allowed>, <other>` is refused; the allowed address on the RIGHT, i.e. what the proxy saw, passes). **Unknown hosting hops are the lock-out risk: if the host puts more than one proxy in front, `req.ip` is a proxy address and everyone is "not allowed"; that is why Monitor comes first (read the logged address and `forwardedForHeader` for a day; set `TRUST_PROXY_HOPS` if needed).** NOT covered: public routes that are not a sign-in (health check, the offer-acceptance links candidates use), the browser is not told the allowed address. `middlewares/audit.ts` now records `clientIp(req)` in ordinary audit rows (it used the first X-Forwarded-For entry, which a client could write); live audit IPs may therefore change style once the hop count is right. LOG ROW: username as typed (never a password), the address, time, result (`Allowed` / `Blocked` / `Allowed (monitor mode: would be blocked)`), reason, mode, `credentialsOk` (null when refused before the password was looked at; the field is NOT named "password" because the Audit log hides such keys), the raw forwarded header (for diagnosing only); linked to the account when the name matches; session requests from a non-allowed address: one row per person + address per 10 minutes; Admins read them in Audit log > module "Security" (detail view shows the address, Admin only; the Audit log is Admin-only already). FRONTEND: the sign-in page shows the server's message; a session refused mid-use is signed out and sent to `/login` with the same message once (`lib/api.ts` `leaveIfBlocked`, `sessionStorage hrms-login-notice`). **TO GO LIVE (owner's step each time; edit `.env` only in the editor, then restart):** 1) deploy; 2) add `LOGIN_IP_MODE=monitor` and `LOGIN_ALLOWED_IPS=206.84.235.169`, restart; 3) read Audit log > Security for a day: check the logged address of an office login is `206.84.235.169` and note who signs in from elsewhere (live history already shows sign-ins from 103.212.140.53 (26 times), 42.108.74.210, 27.97.160.54 and others, who WILL be blocked); 4) change to `enforce`, restart. **EMERGENCY (office IP changed, or a mistake):** edit `.env` to `LOGIN_IP_MODE=off` (or fix the address) and restart the backend; no code or database change is needed. Existing sessions are only affected when `enforce` is switched on (JWTs are stateless; the check is per request). Tests: `tests/ip-policy.test.mts` (26, no DB), `tests/ip-access.test.cjs` (HTTP, throwaway DB, run once per mode with the backend started in that mode: off 26, monitor 39, enforce 49, enforce-with-empty-list 40; five roles incl. Supervisor, forged header, direct connection, IPv6-mapped, neighbouring address, existing sessions, the log, Admin reads it, HR and employees cannot), `tests/ip-socket.test.mts` (5, enforce mode), real Chrome `ip-e2e.cjs` (9: exact message, signed out mid-session, shown once, phone). Regression: role-identity 18, hr-admin-boundary 25, admin-hr-separation 110, hr-visibility 61, productivity-supervisor 82 pass (policy off).
- **Login Activity and device tracking (built 2026-11-20, local, NOT committed or deployed; migration `20261120100000_login_events` NEW TABLE ONLY, NOT applied to live; owner's choices: no location source for now, keep 6 months, HR sees IP and location):** every authentication attempt is one row in the NEW table `login_events` (result SUCCESS | FAILED | BLOCKED, reason, mode, username as typed, employee ID, IP, browser/OS/device type, User-Agent text capped at 300 chars, and for a success: `sessionId`, `expiresAt`, `lastActiveAt`, `loggedOutAt`). Server-side times; IP = `clientIp(req)` (trusted-proxy rule from the network policy). **Why a new table and not the Audit log:** last activity and logout are filled in LATER, so a database trigger protects the table instead of making it append-only: a row can never be edited except `lastActiveAt` forward, `loggedOutAt` once, and the FK clearing of a deleted person; rows younger than 90 days can never be deleted (the 6-month purge, every 6 hours, removes older ones; `LOGIN_ACTIVITY_RETENTION_DAYS` may lengthen it, never below 90). **Sessions:** there are still no server-side sessions (JWT stays stateless, 1 day). The token now carries `sid` (this sign-in's id, `randomUUID` made in `auth.routes.ts`); `requireAuth` stamps last activity at most once a minute; `POST /api/auth/logout` stamps the logout once (the browser then drops the token; the token is NOT revoked on the server, so a copied token stays usable until it expires, as before); a session never ended whose time ran out reads as Expired (worked out when read, nothing stored); sign-ins from before this change have no `sid` and show as "not tracked". Recorded: success, wrong password, suspended account, blocked by the network rule (before the password is looked at, as before), and a lockout (one row per address+name per minute). `modules/login-activity/` (`loginEvents.ts` recording + `loginEventsReady` + purge, `loginActivity.service.ts` reading + the Admin-only "needs attention" flags, routes `GET /api/login-activity/mine`, `GET /api/login-activity`, `GET /api/login-activity/:id`; no route writes, edits or deletes). `lib/userAgent.ts` reads browser/OS/device from the User-Agent with no new package; unknown = empty, never guessed. **Visibility (server-side):** everyone reads their OWN history only (id from the sign-in, a `userId` in the address is ignored); HR = rows of the companies they act on, with IP and device, never an unknown-username attempt, no analysis; Admin = same plus unknown-username attempts (only with the all-companies grant) and the flags (BLOCKED, MANY_FAILURES = 3+ wrong passwords within an hour, NEW_ADDRESS = first sign-in from this address after earlier ones elsewhere, OFF_NETWORK = signed in outside the network in monitor mode; plain rules, nothing scored); managers, the Supervisor and employees get 403 on the organisation list. Filters: name, employee ID, username (Admin), date range (company time zone), IP, location, device, status, company. **Location:** NO source exists, so country/region/city stay empty and every screen says "Location not available" and that any location would be approximate (IP-based, not verified); a source (MaxMind GeoLite2 offline = the private option) can fill those columns later without a schema change. Device text is labelled as what the browser reported, never as proof of one computer. Screens: `dashboard/_security/LoginActivity.tsx` (one table for both uses: Employee | Login date & time | IP | Approximate location | Device | Browser | Status; click a row for details), My Profile tab "Login Activity" (staff) and a block in the Admin profile's Security tab, Admin/HR page `/dashboard/login-activity` + sidebar entry. Before the migration: the network policy keeps writing its own record to the Audit log, every list answers `{ready:false}` and nothing breaks (tested). Adding the migration needs the server to be restarted or 15 s to pass. TO GO LIVE (owner's go-ahead for each): back up Neon, `npx prisma migrate deploy`, deploy. Tests: `tests/login-activity.test.cjs` (94, throwaway DB, backend in enforce mode; proves before/after the migration, all attempt kinds, session life, the trigger, role scopes, flags, filters), `tests/login-activity-purge.test.mts` (15), `tests/user-agent.test.mts` (20), real Chrome `login-activity-e2e.cjs` (40: employee tab, HR, Admin filters and details, logout stamped, dark, phone). `owner-admin-documents.test.cjs` shows 1 failure (previously documented as failing without these changes). Test-server quirk: Next's dev badge covers the Logout button, so the e2e triggers the click on the element.
- **Fix: phone menu stayed open over My Profile (2026-11-20, FRONTEND ONLY, `dashboard/layout.tsx`, no backend or database change, local, NOT committed):** ROOT CAUSE, reproduced with real touch taps in headless Chrome: the mobile drawer closed only in an effect on `pathname`, so tapping a menu item for the page you are ALREADY on (My Profile from My Profile, or from a profile tab `?tab=...`, or Daily Tasks from Daily Tasks) changed nothing, the drawer (`fixed inset-0 z-40`) stayed open over the whole screen with the page dimmed behind it, and the "Open menu" button sat under it, so the person saw only the sidebar and thought the content was missing. The page itself, the desktop layout and every width measured fine (content fills the screen, no sideways scroll, sidebar becomes a drawer below 1024 px). FIX: the drawer wrapper closes on ANY link tapped inside it (`onClickCapture` on the sidebar box), on Escape, and when the screen becomes wide enough for the permanent sidebar (a matchMedia listener, so a turned phone or resized window leaves no overlay); the drawer is now `role="dialog" aria-modal`; the "My Profile" link got a taller tap area (was 18 px high) with `-my-1.5 py-1.5`, no visual change. Kept: the desktop sidebar, the highlight of the current page (`aria-current` on My Profile), dark theme. Tests (browser scripts outside the repo): `profile-repro.cjs` (8 real-tap checks: from another page, already on it, on a tab, current page, dimmed area, Escape, widen/shrink; the 3 same-page checks FAILED before the fix), `profile-e2e.cjs` (139: Employee, Admin, HR, Manager at 390 / 820 / 1440: profile content and details, every tab, highlight, no sideways scroll, tapping My Profile twice, Daily Tasks and Notifications still open, no page errors). NOT covered: a real phone (headless touch emulation only), and the owner's original screenshot did not arrive with the request, so the diagnosis rests on this reproduction.

- **Weighted task performance (built 2026-11-20, local, NOT committed or pushed; NO migration, no schema change, nothing touched on Neon):** ONE pure server module, `backend/src/modules/tasks/tasks.weighted.ts` (`weightedPerformance`), decides every weighted figure; screens only display what the backend sends (`frontend/app/dashboard/_weighted/Weighted.tsx`). WEIGHTS (the only table, also now used by the older star score, so `weightValue`, Reports and My History changed from 1/2/3/5 to this): Critical 5, High 5, Medium 4, Low 1; a task with no/unknown priority = Medium. EARNS: Completed = full weight, **Partially completed = half, only when a manager marks it** (stored in the existing `daily_tasks.outcome` = PARTIALLY_COMPLETED), To Do / In Progress / Blocked = 0 (a legacy FAILED grade = 0; a To Do task cannot be partial). SCORED: approved, not cancelled, not deleted (`activeTasks()`), not manager-assigned, not a manager's self-managed task; each task id once. Pending and rejected tasks earn nothing, are NOT in the possible points, and are shown as their own counts (`notScored`). Percent = earned / possible x 100, 2 decimals; **null = "N/A"** (never 0% or 100%). Star ratings never change it; the old star score stays beside it. New manager action: `POST /api/tasks/review/:id/partial { partial: true|false }` (`markPartial`; same reviewers as rating, never the owner or a self-managed task; guarded update so a repeat is a no-op; audit line; employee notified once; 400 `NOT_STARTED_YET` for To Do). Buttons "Mark partial / Undo partial" on the Completed rows of Daily Tasks > Employee submitted tasks. WHERE THE FIGURE APPEARS: `weighted` on `GET /tasks`, `/tasks/range`, `/tasks/day`, per day and in the summary of `/tracker/history`, per person and totals of `/reports` (managers left out of totals like the rest), per person + `weightedTotal` of `/team-attendance/productivity`, `weighted` + `weightedByUser` on the review queue; per-task `weightPoints`/`earnedPoints`/`partial` in `/team-attendance/productivity/tasks` (so the team report). Screens: Tasks & Performance (removed from the Daily Tasks page on 2026-11-21 at the owner's request) (card + Weighted score column), Reports (Weighted score column + CSV), Task productivity (column, CSV, Excel People sheet incl. per-priority points, Tasks sheet columns), My History (tile, row, CSV), team workbook (summary + tasks sheets) and dashboard workbook (3 columns after Average rating). Not done: HR/Admin home Team Performance charts, My performance (manager) and the supervisor dashboard still show the old figures; the Tasks & Performance weighted column ignores the page's own project box (shows a dash while a project is chosen). Tests: `backend/tests/weighted-performance.test.mts` (40, no DB; includes the owner's 8.5 / 15 = 56.67% example), `weighted-performance.test.cjs` (51, throwaway DB: example end to end, who may mark partial, pending/rejected/deleted/assigned excluded, same figure on every screen as Employee/Manager/HR/Admin, date filters, N/A); task suites, `team-report-book` 25, `dashboard-workbook` 79 and `dashboard-workbook-e2e` 86 still pass. Not opened in a real browser. Gotcha: the throwaway test backend (`start-be-5012.sh`) runs plain `tsx`, so restart it after a backend edit.

It does have separate content that is configuration, not user management: the Shifts and Thresholds tabs (alert thresholds, early logout policy, previous-day tasks policy). Hold is not a decision: no leave days are written, nothing is deducted, the first-level approval row stays open for the same person, the employee is notified (type LEAVE_DECIDED, "Your leave request is on hold", with who and why), an audit line is written. A held request stays in every approver's list and can later be approved or rejected by the same person (first-level approve returns it to PENDING for HR; HR approve/reject closes it; both use `status in [PENDING, ON_HOLD]` guards); holding twice = 409 `ALREADY_ON_HOLD`; the employee can cancel a held request; a held request still blocks overlapping dates. Everywhere "open" used to mean `PENDING` it now means PENDING or ON_HOLD (`openIn()` in `leave.service.ts`, lists, counts, overview `onHold`, `leaveSummary`, filters by status and by stage `ON_HOLD`). Stage `ON_HOLD` "On Hold" (`leaveFlow.logic.ts`) with `hold {by, role, step, at, reason}` from `decorate()`. **LIVE-SAFETY RULE FOUND BY THE TEST:** Postgres refuses the value `ON_HOLD` even inside a READ filter before the migration, so every query asks for it only after `holdReady()` (enum value AND history constraint present, in-flight shared check, re-checked every 15 s); before the migration Hold answers 503 `FEATURES_NOT_READY` and everything else is exactly as before. **Corrections are NOT given Hold** (leave only; `ManagerDecisionBox allowHold` is opt-in). - **Break Management, Admin only (2026-11-21, local, NOT committed; NO migration, NO new table, nothing written):** sidebar "Break Management" (Admin menu, after Attendance) -> `/dashboard/break-management` (`useRoleGate(["ADMIN"])`; HR / Manager / Employee are sent home) on the read-only `GET /api/break-monitor?from&to&<HR filters incl. department>&breakType&status&defaulter&userId` (router `requireRole("ADMIN")`, service re-checks; module `modules/break-monitor/`). People = the Admin's reach (`visibleUserIds`: every company they act on, Admin accounts left out), narrowed by the shared filters; reads the EXISTING `tracker_breaks` + `work_sessions` rows. Rules (pure, `breakMonitor.logic.ts`): counted breaks only (activityClass: Lunch, Personal, Other, older Tea; Washroom and discussions are not breaks); duration = the HRMS's own (`correctedDurationSeconds` wins, else timestamps; a running break counts to now; overlapping intervals of a day counted once; a duplicate row once); **Break Time is DAILY**: the person's first team's `TeamConfig.breakBudgetMinutes`, else the company's `platforms.defaultBreakBudgetMins` (live: 12 of 48 active employees have a team value, every company has 60), none or 0 = "Not configured" (never a defaulter); **exceeded day** = day total > that day's Break Time (UPDATED 2026-11-21 by the owner: NO tolerance; the team's `breakOverageAlertMinutes` only affects the BREAK_OVERAGE alert, never who is a defaulter); day excess = total - Break Time; a break's excess = the part of it beyond the allowance in time order (status Exceeded), an open break = "Currently on break" (no end time, elapsed ticks on screen); **defaulter** = at least ONE exceeded day in the week (Monday-Sunday containing the range end) / month / range, judged day by day against the daily allowance (never a weekly / monthly total; repeats not required); each person line carries `violations` (date, allowed, actual, excess), shown as "Dates over Break Time" on the Weekly / Monthly tabs, in the drawer and in the exports (extra Excel sheet "Violation days"); permitted = Break Time x days with a work session. Cards (whole-card buttons; number = the list shown): Employees with break activity, Currently on break, Total breaks today, Average break duration (completed breaks), Employees exceeding Break Time, Weekly defaulters, Monthly defaulters. Tabs Break activity (paginated 50, 5,000-row cap with a note) / Weekly defaulters / Monthly defaulters (previous / next period), employee drawer (dates, daily totals, every break, week + month summary, CSV), presets Today / Yesterday / This week / Last week / This month / Last month / Custom (max 93 days), filters break type, status, defaulter + company / project / manager / department / name-ID; exports CSV and Excel (Break activity, Daily totals, Period summary, Week, Month, Defaulters; same data as the screen). Monitoring only: no attendance, productive-time, pay or warning change. Tests: `backend/tests/break-monitor-logic.test.mts` (20, no DB), `break-monitor.test.cjs` (34, throwaway DB: Admin only, washroom/discussion excluded, team vs company allowance, tolerance, correction, open break, overlap, other company visible, weekly/monthly numbers = rows, filters combined, employee view, 404 outside reach, read writes nothing), real Chrome `breaks-e2e.cjs` (23: menu, page, live ticking, cards = lists, defaulter tabs, drawer, real .xlsx download, status filter, phone, HR / Manager / Employee have no entry and are redirected); `admin-sidebar-routes` 52, `page-title` 12. Not done: "Department" in the table is the person's first team (the system has no department field; the Department FILTER is the Query Desk departments); the Excel file was not opened in desktop Excel.
- **IP restriction bypass, root cause and fix (2026-11-21, local, NOT committed or deployed; live settings NOT changed):** WHY MOBILE SIGN-INS SUCCEEDED: (1) the restriction was not switched on: this machine's `backend/.env` (the one that points at Neon) has no `LOGIN_IP_MODE` / `LOGIN_ALLOWED_IPS`, and an unset mode means OFF (nothing checked); a host set to `monitor` (the rollout step) also only logs; (2) the code FAILED OPEN: a misspelt mode fell back to monitor, and enforce with an empty/invalid list fell back to monitor; (3) a forged header could pass once enforced: `trust proxy` = 1 hop meant that a client reaching the backend DIRECTLY could send `X-Forwarded-For: 206.84.235.169` and be taken for the office. Checked and already right: one token issuer only (`/auth/login`, IP checked before the password), every authenticated request re-checks (existing sessions refused), the Socket.IO handshake checks, an undetectable address is refused, IPv6 never matches an IPv4 entry, `::ffff:` mapping handled. FIX (`lib/ipPolicy.ts`, `server.ts`, `lib/realtime.ts`, `auth.routes.ts`): FAIL CLOSED: an unknown mode = ENFORCE; enforce with no valid address = EVERYONE refused (incl. Admins; emergency = `LOGIN_IP_MODE=off` + restart); monitor with no list still only logs; unset/`off` is still off but the server now prints `[network policy] sign-in IP restriction: OFF/MONITOR/ENFORCE, N allowed address(es)` at start. TRUSTED PROXIES BY ADDRESS, not by hop count: X-Forwarded-For is followed only through proxies on a private network (loopback, link-local, 10/8, 172.16/12, 192.168/16, fc00::/7, i.e. a hosting load balancer) plus `TRUSTED_PROXIES` (comma list, e.g. a CDN range); walking inwards, the first address that is not a trusted proxy is the client (`trustedProxyCheck`, `clientFromChain`; the same rule for Express `trust proxy` and the live socket). A public client connecting directly can never choose its address; an unknown PUBLIC proxy in front makes everyone "that proxy" = refused (fail closed) and the logged address names the proxy to add. `TRUST_PROXY_HOPS` is ignored (warning). NEW Admin-only diagnostic `GET /api/auth/ip-check` (detected IP, connection address, raw X-Forwarded-For, mode, number of allowed entries, problem, whether this request is allowed; never the list itself) to verify on the live server. Blocked attempts are recorded in Login Activity (`login_events`: username, user when it matches, detected IP, mode, browser/OS/device text, time, reason IP_NOT_ALLOWED); there is only one sign-in method (password) so no method column; session requests from another network still go to Audit log > Security. Possible trap to check on live: if the HRMS server itself sits INSIDE the office network, office users reach it with a private LAN address, not 206.84.235.169; then the office LAN range must be added to `LOGIN_ALLOWED_IPS`. TO TURN IT ON (owner, in the host's settings or `backend/.env` in the EDITOR, then restart): `LOGIN_IP_MODE=enforce`, `LOGIN_ALLOWED_IPS=206.84.235.169`, plus `TRUSTED_PROXIES=<proxy range>` only if `/auth/ip-check` from the office shows a proxy address instead of 206.84.235.169. Tests: `ip-policy.test.mts` (38: fail-closed cases, the proxy rule incl. a directly-connecting phone forging the office address, IPv6, unknown public proxy, TRUSTED_PROXIES), `ip-access.test.cjs` per mode: enforce 55 (all five roles, mobile/home/other-office/neighbouring/IPv6/private addresses refused, forged header refused, existing sessions refused, ip-check), enforce-empty 12 (everyone refused, office too), monitor 39, off 26, and a misspelt mode enforcing; `ip-socket.test.mts` 5; `login-activity` 91/94 (its 3 "before the migration" checks need the table dropped first).

Candidate / Invited / Suspended stay in the enum as LEGACY: never assignable, kept and shown on records that hold them, Suspended still blocked. `lib/userStatus.ts` is the one place: `ASSIGNABLE_STATUSES`, `isBlockedStatus` (sign-in, `requireAuth`, socket refuse SUSPENDED/EXITED/TERMINATED/ABSCONDED), `statusReady()` (pg_enum check, re-checked every 15 s until true) + `inactiveStatuses()` / `leftStatuses()` that add the new values to every `notIn` list only once the database has them (LIVE-SAFETY: Postgres refuses an unknown enum value even in a read filter, and the 5001 backend reloads against Neon). Edit Employee (`employeeEdit.service.ts`): only a CHANGE is validated (400 `STATUS_NOT_ALLOWED` for a legacy/unknown value, 503 `FEATURES_NOT_READY` for Terminated/Absconded before the migration); any left status sets `exitDate`, returning to Active clears it; audit before/after already existed. TO GO LIVE: backup, `npx prisma migrate deploy`, deploy, then the script after a dry run (4 Suspended incl. the System Admin account). Test `tests/employment-status.test.cjs` (34 checks; its before/after phases need a database without the two values, so recreate the throwaway DB to re-run; first run 30/34, the 4 failures were wrong test expectations (login answers 403, dropdowns need companyId), fixed afterwards but not re-run).
- **Holiday Management (built 2026-11-22, local, NOT committed; migration `20261122110000_holiday_management` NEW TABLES ONLY, applied ONLY to the throwaway DB, NOT to Neon; owner said "go" to my recommendations):** BUILT ON the existing `holidays` table: Fixed = dayType PUBLIC_HOLIDAY, Optional = OPTIONAL_HOLIDAY (attendance, reports and the timesheet already treat optional as a working day); **published = a row in `holidays`** (every existing holiday is already published, none was changed); **drafts live ONLY in `holiday_drafts`** so an unpublished calendar can never reach attendance, reports or leave. New tables (raw SQL, no Prisma models, read only after `holidayReady()`): `holiday_imports` (history: file, year, status DRAFT/PUBLISHED/DISCARDED, counts, who/when), `holiday_drafts`, `holiday_details` (religion/category, calendarYear, importId, createdBy/updatedBy; no row = an older holiday), `holiday_archive` (deactivated holidays as full copies, restorable), `holiday_policy` (optional allowance per company, default 2, Admin sets, HR reads), `optional_holiday_requests` (PENDING_MANAGER / PENDING_HR / APPROVED / REJECTED / CANCELLED; one live request per person per holiday). Before the migration: the old screens work, new reads say `ready:false`, writes answer 503 `FEATURES_NOT_READY`. Code: `modules/holidays/holidayImport.logic.ts` (pure: header mapping, date parsing 01-Jan-2026 / 01-01-2026 / ISO / Excel serial, Day derived and checked, Fixed/Optional, duplicates in file, year check, nothing silently changed), `holidayCalendar.service.ts`, `holidayCalendar.routes.ts` (`/api/holiday-calendar/*`; own MESSAGES map), the old `/api/holidays` routes untouched. Flow: **preview** (nothing saved; xlsx/csv only, `.xls` refused with advice because the reader and no new dependency) -> HR excludes rows -> **confirm** (re-reads the file, one transaction, advisory lock so two confirmations make one draft, `DRAFT_EXISTS` unless replaceDraft) -> **Publish** (confirm; dates in the past need `confirmPast`; claim the import, skip any holiday that already exists, never replace or delete; notifies the company's people once, dedupe key per import). Same sheet uploaded again = every row ALREADY_EXISTS. Manual add (single company or applyToAll), edit (past holidays: category only), deactivate (not past, not with live requests) and restore. **Optional holidays:** a normal working day unless approved; request -> reporting manager (an active Manager/Admin) -> HR final (no manager = straight to HR); allowance counted at request time and again at HR approval; approval writes a `leave_days` row (type OTHER, note "Optional holiday: <name>", `on conflict do nothing`) which the attendance engine already shows as Leave; cancelling an approved request takes only that row back (matched by its note); religion/category is description only, never decides eligibility. Leave day counting fixed: `leave.service.ts workingDates` now treats only PUBLIC_HOLIDAY/WEEKLY_OFF as off (it used to treat an OPTIONAL holiday as off too). Timesheet quiet row no longer says "Holiday" for an optional day. Screens: `/dashboard/holidays` (every role; sidebar "Holidays" for all; HR/Admin = "Holiday Management": Calendar (upcoming, year grid, draft bar with Publish/Discard), List (search/type/status filters, Edit/Deactivate/Restore), Optional requests, Imports & settings (allowance, import history); Employee/Manager: Calendar, My optional holidays, Team requests). Year selector offers year-1..year+1. Not built: a holiday-work/compensation rule (none configured), an Excel export of the calendar, drafts editing row by row (re-upload instead), notifications on single add/edit (only on publish), an Upcoming Holidays card on the Today / HR dashboards (the Holidays page has it). Tests: `holiday-import-logic.test.mts` (23, no DB), `holiday-management.test.cjs` (106, throwaway DB; runs before and after applying the migration itself; drop the six tables and restart the backend before re-running; use TEST_API=...5012), real Chrome `holiday-e2e.cjs` (20: HR upload -> preview -> draft -> publish, employee request, manager then HR approval, phone width). Other suites: attendance-leave-priority 36, hr-visibility 61; attendance-calendar 42/43 (one "weekend is Week-off" check fails, looks date/data dependent, NOT checked against the old code) and early-logout fails only because the throwaway DB already holds its policy table.
- **Employees upload their own documents; HR verifies (built 2026-11-22, local, NOT committed; NO migration, no new table):** reuses `employee_documents`, `document_requirements`, the private store and the existing routes. Rules now enforced on the SERVER (`onboarding.service.ts`): an employee uploads only for their OWN record (id from the sign-in, a `userId` in the body is ignored) and only requirements marked uploaded-by-employee; **HR can no longer upload a document the employee must upload** (`uploadForCandidate` answers 400 `EMPLOYEE_UPLOADS_THIS`; HR can still upload HR-owned requirements, e.g. a signed contract, and an HR upload of those is still auto-verified); a VERIFIED document cannot be replaced (409 `ALREADY_VERIFIED`), a pending or rejected one can; a re-upload goes back to SUBMITTED ("Pending Verification") and clears the old review on the row. **History is kept without a migration:** the previous FILE is no longer deleted from the store on replace (it was before), and every upload, re-upload, verification and rejection writes an audit row (`recordType EmployeeDocument`, recordId = the document, before/after status, file name, reason, who, when) read by the new `GET /api/documents/history/:id` (HR/Admin of the company or an owner; file names, statuses and reasons only, never a storage key). Notifications (existing inbox): HR/Admin of the company on submit (`DOCUMENT_SUBMITTED`, once per submission), the employee on verify (`DOCUMENT_VERIFIED`) and on reject (`DOCUMENT_REJECTED`, body carries HR's reason, link to My Profile > My Documents; both pop up). Reject still needs a reason. Screens: My Profile > My Documents (`_documents/MyDocuments.tsx`, shared with the Documents page) has an "Upload document" bar with a document-type selector (only types not yet verified), chips Pending verification / Verified / Rejected, HR's reason and "Upload corrected document"; HR/Admin Employee Documents (`documents/page.tsx` checklist dialog): no Upload/Replace on employee-owned rows ("Not submitted yet. The employee uploads this document from My Profile."), View / Verify / Reject, upload date, verified-by and date, and a History list per document; the tracking tab already shows who has missing / pending / rejected documents. Fixed on the way: the reject box showed under every document with no file (pre-existing `null === null`). Not changed: download rules (owner employee, HR, Admin; manager and other employees refused), employee-owner Admin exemption, requirements screen, warning letters. Old files replaced BEFORE this change are gone (nothing to recover). Tests: `tests/employee-document-upload.test.cjs` (43, throwaway DB), real Chrome `docs-e2e.cjs` (12: employee upload, HR reject with reason, history, employee sees reason and re-uploads, phone width); `warning-letters` 60 still passes; `documents` (1 audit check) and `owner-admin-documents` (people count) fail exactly as before this change.
- **Employee Overview cards open a side drawer (2026-11-22, local, NOT committed; NO migration):** on `/dashboard/employee-overview` the four cards (Total, Active, Inactive, Not started yet) now open `EmployeeListDrawer.tsx` (right-hand drawer, portal, `role="dialog"`, Escape / Close / backdrop click, body scroll locked, focus returns to the card, the page stays visible behind; on a phone the rows become cards) instead of changing the directory below (the status chips under the cards still do that). **The list is served by the new `GET /api/admin/users/page`** (`admin.userPage`, Admin/HR via the router guard): server-side view (`ALL|ACTIVE|INACTIVE|NOT_STARTED`), `status`, `departmentId` (= a team, the system has no department field), `search` (name tokens or employee ID), `sort=name|joined` + `dir` (no joining date sorts last), `page`/`pageSize` (max 100, 20 in the drawer) plus the dashboard's own filters (company, project, manager, designation, employeesOnly), all built on the SAME `peopleWhere` the card counts use, so a card number equals its list total (tested). Definitions: Inactive = Exited + Terminated + Absconded (+ the older Suspended so the cards still add up; Suspended shows as "(older)" and its sub-filter appears only while somebody holds it), Not started yet = the existing Invited/Candidate records, Total = everyone in scope. Legacy statuses are never rewritten; Terminated/Absconded are only queried once `statusReady()` is true. Inside Inactive: chips All inactive / Exited / Terminated / Absconded with counts; Total has an Employment status dropdown. View Profile opens the EXISTING Employee profile dialog (with its "Manage in User Management" link); no new profile page. Bug found by the browser test and fixed: the sort select was reading the wrong array positions. Tests: `backend/tests/user-page.test.cjs` (37, throwaway DB), real Chrome `drawer-e2e.cjs` (33: all four cards, count = list, Escape, paging, search, sort, inactive filters, profile, phone). The older `cards-e2e.cjs` (cards choose the directory) no longer matches and was not updated.

Faizan Qureshi (CE-04, Colab Esports) is the named approver (`request_approvals.approverIds`) of Take Health employees (reporting lines may cross companies by decision), so those requests were dropped while the badge (`awaitingMyLeaveApproval`, counted from the approval rows) still counted them. Corrections change is not covered by a new test. NOTE: the local backend on :5001 runs `tsx watch` against Neon, so the fix is live there as soon as the file was saved.

- **Organization Hierarchy dark theme fixed (2026-11-22, FRONTEND ONLY, local, NOT committed; light theme untouched):** ROOT CAUSES (found by reading the CSS and then measuring in Chrome): (1) the row hover is `hover:bg-[var(--brand-50)]` and `--brand-50` (#e7f6f1, pale green) was NEVER redefined in the dark block, so a hovered row went pale green under light text; (2) Change manager and the expand/collapse buttons use `hover:bg-white`, and dark only remapped plain `bg-white`, so they turned WHITE on hover; (3) rows with a reporting warning used `bg-amber-50/50` with no dark treatment of their own (a brown wash); (4) the dark muted text step `--color-slate-400` (#6f8a82) measured 3.76:1 on the hover row (employee IDs). FIX, all inside `html.dark` in `globals.css` (nothing outside dark changes): new tokens `--row-bg #111f1b`, `--row-hover #1a3029`, `--row-selected #1b4035`, `--row-border #29443a`; `--brand-50` now equals the dark hover; `html.dark .hover\:bg-white:hover` uses the dark hover; `.org-row` (added to `OrgHierarchy` `Row`, no light styling) gets the row background and border, hover and keyboard `:focus-within` the hover colour, `aria-selected`/`data-selected` and a picked `[role=option]` the selected colour; `.org-row-issue` is a faint amber wash with a muted-amber left edge (the amber chip keeps the warning readable); `--color-slate-400` is now #819990 (the owner's suggested muted text). NOTE the wider effect: the `--brand-50` and `hover:bg-white` fixes also correct dark hover on every other page that uses them (they were wrong there too), and muted text is slightly lighter across dark. Role badges (Admin/Manager/HR/Employee), the warning chips and "Top level" were already readable. Pitfall hit while patching: a selector written through a node/JS string lost its backslash (`.hover:bg-white` is invalid CSS and the browser silently drops the whole rule); write CSS escapes with the editor tool. Verified in real Chrome with seeded Admin/Manager/HR/Employee and warning cases, colours and WCAG contrast measured per row in each state (canvas colour parsing, because Chrome reports Tailwind colours as oklab): `orgdark-e2e.cjs` 26 of 26 (all rows dark and >= 4.5:1, all chips >= 4.5:1, hover, hover on a warning row, Change manager hover, expand control, visible keyboard focus and focused row, collapsed tree, Needs attention list, live theme switching without reload both ways, light rows still light with pale-green hover (#e7f6f1) and amber issue tint, phone width with no overflow or clipped buttons). Not covered: the "selected" row state exists only in the Change manager dialog list (the tree has no selected row); other pages were not re-audited.
- **Leave approval lists refresh live + why Faizan still saw "Nothing is waiting for you" (2026-11-22, FRONTEND ONLY for this step, local, NOT pushed):** the screenshots from the live site show the OLD code: Render had not received the cross-company fix (`leave.service.ts managerRequests` no longer filters by the manager's company, committed locally in `ec5ccf1`), and `/api/auth/ip-check` (same batch) still answers 404 there. Also found: LOCAL `main` has 2 commits that are not on GitHub and GitHub's `main` has 1 commit the local copy lacks (diverged), so a plain push will be refused: `git pull --no-rebase origin main` (resolve if asked), then `git push origin main`; check Render's branch (Settings > Branch). New in this step: `leave/page.tsx` and `_approvals/LeaveApprovalQueue.tsx` call `useRealtime(["notifications:changed"], reload)`. Every leave step already notifies exactly the people concerned (approver at submit, HR when the manager approves, the employee on each decision, HR directly when there is no manager), each notification pings that person's private inbox channel, so the manager's Team requests, HR's All requests and the Admin Approval Centre update by themselves. Not covered: the manager is not pinged when HR gives the final answer (his "Answered by you" label updates on his next reload). Test: real Chrome `leaveflow-e2e.cjs` with a manager whose team is in ANOTHER company: 23 of 23 (empty list first, employee submits a half day, it appears for the manager with no refresh showing name, ID, type, date, 0.5 days, reason, "Waiting for <manager>", Approve / Reject / Hold; HR sees it as pending manager and gets 409 if it tries to approve first; another manager gets 404 and sees nothing; manager approves in the UI, it moves to Answered by you and the HR row updates live (history 1 to 2); HR approves, leave day written only then, employee notified and sees Approved, history has 3+ events; hold keeps it open; manager rejection closes it and HR can no longer approve it; other people's leave rows untouched). `manager-leave-cross-company` 23 still pass.
- **Break Activity ranked by employee total (2026-11-22, FRONTEND ONLY, local, NOT pushed; no backend change, no break record, allowance, attendance or Excess/Status calculation touched):** `_breaks/BreakManagement.tsx` activity table was one row per break sorted by start time. NOW it is one row per EMPLOYEE (the same 12 columns, same dark styling): Duration = the sum of every break of that person in the period, shown as hours, minutes and seconds (`hms()`, e.g. "2h 10m 00s"); a running break counts up to now (the existing per-second tick, `secsOf`), so the total and the ORDER move live while it runs (verified: a person at 19m 56s overtakes one at 20m 00s without a refresh). The row opens (chevron, `aria-expanded`) to that person's individual breaks, newest first, in the original break-row format with their own Duration / Break Time / Excess / Status; the name still opens the full history drawer. Sort control "Sort by": Highest Break Time First (DEFAULT), Lowest Break Time First, Latest Break First (by the most recent break start); ties break by name. Totals are computed from the rows left after the date range, company, project, manager, department, employee, break type, status and card filters (the server and `cardRows` already apply them), so a filter changes the totals (Lunch only: A 2h 10m becomes 1h 30m). The employee row's Excess = the existing period excess from the server aggregate (`res.range`), its Status is only a summary of the breaks' own statuses (Currently on break if any is running, else Exceeded if any is, else Completed); the break rows keep their own unchanged. Paging is by employee (50). The Excel/CSV exports are unchanged (still one row per break). Test: real Chrome `breakrank-e2e.cjs` (21 of 21): the example 2h 10m / 1h 35m / 1h / 45m / 20m, the two-break person summed, hms format, descending order, expand and collapse, live overtaking, all three sort orders, type filter recalculation, unchanged column headers, no break rows touched, phone width. The older `breaks-e2e.cjs` (one row per break) no longer matches this table and was not updated. Needs a push to reach the live site.
