The raise form has an optional "Tag an Admin" box fed by `adminChoices()` in `dept.service.ts` (ACTIVE Admin accounts that act on the employee's company, never the employee, read from the database by id; `GET /queries/dept/form-preview` returns `adminChoices`). `POST /queries/dept { taggedAdminId }`: not on that list = 400 `BAD_TAGGED_ADMIN`, nothing created. A tag REPLACES only the Admin stage (`adminApproverIds = [tagged]`, no new column); the reporting-manager step is untouched (Sanjay cannot approve before the manager; only the tagged Admin can give the final approval; other Admins and HR stay view-only with organisation-wide visibility). Notifications: no manager step = one `DEPT_QUERY_ADMIN_REVIEW`; with a manager step the tagged Admin gets one `DEPT_QUERY_TAGGED` FYI at raise and the final-approval notice after the manager approves (dedupe keys per history row). Admin Query Desk (Department requests tab): five clickable status cards Pending / Approved / Rejected / On Hold / Resolved (`summary`, `statusBucket` in `dept.logic.ts`; every request is in exactly one, so they add up; filter `?status=`), view buttons "All queries" and "Assigned to me" (`TAGGED` = Admin is in `adminApproverIds`), rows show company, project (requester's main team), department, priority, approval status, "Admin: <name>" and a "Tagged to you" chip; the open request shows "Assigned Admin"; the list refreshes on `notifications:changed`. **Bug found and fixed by the test:** `listRequests` used `{}` inside a Prisma `OR` for an Admin/HR with all-company access, which does NOT match everything, so such an Admin saw only requests they were an approver of; now no filter is applied for them. Tests: `backend/tests/dept-admin-tagging.test.cjs` (53, throwaway DB), `dept-requests` 135, `dept-logic` 39, `query-delete` 33 still pass; real Chrome `tagadmin-e2e` (18: employee tags, Admin cards/filters/detail, other Admin, phone). Not built: tagging on classic queries, a Query Desk tile on the Admin home, an Admin raising a request.
- **Fix: HR could not see or work an approved HR Department request (2026-11-23, NO migration, local, NOT committed; found with a read-only look at live):** the live request DQ-000003 (Ankit, Avatar India, HR Department, Approved / Awaiting department action) was invisible to Sonali (CP-03, HR of Colab Platforms with an all-companies grant, "Raised by me (1)" only). TWO causes: (1) the old `listRequests` put `{}` inside a Prisma `OR` for an all-company HR/Admin, which does NOT match everything (fixed in the Admin-tagging work the same day: no filter at all for them), so live (not yet deployed) still showed her only what she raised; (2) Sonali is not a MEMBER of Avatar India's HR Department (live: that department has NO members, in fact no live department has any), and department membership is what lets someone accept, update progress, comment and complete; the member rule (`updateDepartment`) allowed only people of the department's OWN company, so an HR of another company could not even be added. FIX: `eligibleMemberIds()` also accepts an active HR/Admin who has access to the department's company (`accessFor`); `GET /queries/dept/admin/departments` returns `outsiders` per department and Manage departments lists them as "Name (from Company)". An HR with no grant of that company, and employees of other companies, stay refused (400 `BAD_MEMBERS`). Visibility is unchanged: HR/Admin with company access SEE every request of those companies under All (monitoring), the department queue views (Awaiting / Assigned / In progress...) only show departments she is a member of, Raised by me stays separate. No data was changed on live: **after deploying, an Admin must open Query Desk > Department requests > Manage departments and add Sonali (and whoever else works for it) as a member of the HR Department of each company she handles (Avatar India first); a department with no members means nobody can accept its requests.** Tests: `backend/tests/dept-hr-queue.test.cjs` (22, throwaway DB, reproduces the exact case: Approved HR request visible under All, filter by department, Raised by me separate, cannot accept until a member, ineligible people refused, then accept / start / comment / complete, approval history untouched, no duplicate), `dept-requests` 135 and `dept-admin-tagging` 53 pass.
- **Check-up fixes (2026-11-23, local, NOT committed):** (1) PRODUCT: the Tasks queue (`manageableUserIds`, `tasks.service.ts`) counted Admin accounts in the organisation total while the shared filters (which leave Admins out, "Admins are not tracked") never listed them, so HR/Admin saw "Showing 22 of 127" with 3 people that could never appear; Admin accounts are now left out of that company-wide part (`NOT_ADMIN`). (2) TESTS only (the engine was right): the default week for people with no shift is Monday-Saturday (`attendance.service.ts`), so `dashboard-workbook-e2e` now treats only Sunday as off (it assumed Mon-Fri and broke whenever a Saturday had company sessions) and `attendance-calendar` checks a SUNDAY (2026-10-25) for Week-off instead of Saturday 2026-10-31; `hr-team-tasks` counts people without Admin accounts. (3) `login-activity-purge` and `ip-socket` only need their environment (DATABASE_URL; backend in enforce mode): 15/15 and 5/5. Re-run results: dashboard-workbook-e2e 86, attendance-calendar 43, hr-team-tasks 38, task-approval-visibility 35, hr-task-approval 30, task-form-managers 41, hr-visibility 61.
- **Admin dashboard: My Query Desk (2026-11-23, FRONTEND ONLY, no backend/database change, local, NOT committed):** `_admin/MyQueryDesk.tsx`, placed right under the KPI cards. It reads `GET /queries/dept?view=TAGGED` (the SAME call as Query Desk's tagged filter; the signed-in Admin's own id comes from the token, so each of the four Admins sees only what is tagged to them, nothing hardcoded) and counts the returned rows in the browser, so the widget and the Query Desk list cannot disagree and a deleted request (hard delete) is simply not there. Tiles: Tagged to me, Action required from me (the existing `needsYou`: a pending decision of his, or an approved request he can accept as a department member; rejected, resolved and closed never count), Pending my approval, With the manager first, Approved awaiting department, With the department / on hold, Closed (resolved or rejected); they add up to the total. Rows (action-required first, up to 6, "Showing x of y"): department, reference, priority, "Action required" chip, title, employee, company, project, date, status badge (approval step while pending, then the department's work status or Rejected / Completed); a row opens the existing detail (`/dashboard/queries?tab=dept&id=`) with the normal approval actions; "View all my queries" opens `/dashboard/queries?tab=dept&view=TAGGED` (new `initialView` prop on `DeptRequests`, read once). Refreshes with the dashboard's existing `notifications:changed` signal (a newly tagged query appears with no reload). Renamed the Query Desk filter "Assigned to me" (TAGGED) to **"Tagged to me"** because a department member also has a different filter called "Assigned to me" (work they own). Also fixed a phone-width sideways scroll on the Admin dashboard (the Pending approvals / Recent activity grid had no `min-w-0`). Tests (real Chrome, throwaway stack): `mqd-e2e` 27 checks (placement, counts for four statuses, rows, detail, View all, Jayesh sees only his, quiet Admin empty state, live update, phone, dark), `tagadmin-e2e` 18 still pass. Not done: a department-level "Tagged to me" count in the sidebar badge (it already counts requests waiting for the Admin).
- **Chat drawer layout cleanup (2026-11-23, FRONTEND ONLY, no backend/Socket.IO/database change, local, NOT committed):** same two components as before (`_chat/ChatDrawer.tsx` frame, `_chat/ChatPanel.tsx`), no new component or API. ROOT CAUSES found by measuring the real drawer at 1920/1440/1366/1024/768/390 with a seeded group chat: (1) the message bubble used `break-words`, which does not shrink a long word or link, so a long URL widened the column and the message area scrolled SIDEWAYS (20 elements past the panel edge, `scrollWidth > clientWidth`); (2) the composer was a fixed 40 px, user-resizable textarea (`resize-y`) that grew its OWN inner scrollbar as soon as text wrapped, i.e. a second scrollbar next to the message list; (3) no backdrop and the page behind kept scrolling. FIX: bubbles use `[overflow-wrap:anywhere]` with `min-w-0` on the row, column and bubble (action buttons `shrink-0`), the message list is the ONE scroll container (`overflow-x-hidden overscroll-contain`, `data-testid=chat-messages`); the composer auto-grows to its text up to 160 px (border added back to `scrollHeight`, otherwise it stayed 2 px short and showed a scrollbar), `resize-none`, `min-h-[44px]`, Send is a 44 px button, an Enter / Shift+Enter hint sits under it, header and composer are `shrink-0`; the thread header is compact (chevron + "Chats", avatar, name, member count, an info icon + "Details" button for groups that opens the existing group settings); date separators are smaller (`my-2`, 10 px). Drawer: a light backdrop (`bg-slate-900/25`, `data-testid=chat-backdrop`, below the 64 px header so the header icons stay usable, desktop only; phones are full screen) closes the chat on click, and while it is open `document.documentElement.style.overflow = "hidden"` (the previous value is restored on close). Kept: Enter sends, Shift+Enter new line, stay-at-bottom unless reading history, load earlier, replies, edits, reactions, seen-by, Socket.IO refresh, unread badge, Escape layering. Behaviour change on purpose: the dashboard behind the open chat is no longer clickable (a click on the dimmed page closes the chat), so the older `chat-e2e` step "the drawer stays open across pages" (it clicks a sidebar link) no longer applies. Tests (real Chrome, throwaway stack): `chat-audit.cjs` (one scroll container, no sideways scroll, composer 44 px, `wide` 0 at all six sizes), `chat-fn-e2e.cjs` (24 checks: backdrop and page lock, header/composer fixed while history scrolls, reading history is not interrupted, live message scrolls into view at the bottom, Shift+Enter, growth without a scrollbar, Enter sends multi-line, long link stays inside, Details, direct chat has no Details, backdrop and Escape close and unlock, phone full screen, dark theme); `chat-logic` 14 passes.
- **My Query Desk / Tagged to me = INDIVIDUAL tags only (2026-11-23, NO migration, no schema change, local, NOT committed):** ROOT CAUSE of departments and routing leaking into the Admin's widget: "tagged" meant "is in `adminApproverIds`", but that list is also filled by ROUTING (the Admin above the manager, or EVERY Admin of the company when nobody is mapped), so untagged requests showed in every Admin's list. NOW the individual tag is stored explicitly as one extra history entry (`dept_request_events` kind `ADMIN_TAGGED`, the Admin's account id in `toValue`, written in the same transaction as the request, so no column and no migration; read by `taggedAdmins()` in `dept.service.ts`; requests tagged before that entry existed are recognised by the sentence "Tagged to a specific Admin by the employee." in their CREATED entry plus exactly one approver). `Facts.taggedAdminId` drives the `TAGGED` view (`dept.logic.ts`): only the Admin the employee picked sees it under Tagged to me; a department, a mapped Admin and the all-Admins fallback do NOT. The list rows and the detail now carry `taggedAdmin {id,name}` (null when nobody was tagged) and the screens show "Tagged Admin: <name>" only from that. Unchanged: who may approve (`adminApproverIds`), organisation-wide visibility (All queries / Waiting for my decision), department work queues, notifications, audit. The widget's counts (Tagged to me, Action required, Pending my approval, ...) are counted from those tagged rows only; empty state "No queries tagged to you." Tests: `dept-admin-tagging.test.cjs` 63 (new: untagged fallback and mapped-Admin requests are in nobody's Tagged to me but stay in everyone's All and Waiting for my decision; one ADMIN_TAGGED entry per request), `dept-requests` 135, `dept-hr-queue` 22, `query-delete` 33, `dept-logic` 39, real Chrome `mqd-e2e` 28 (adds two untagged requests that must not appear) and `tagadmin-e2e` 18.
- **Events and Meetings widget: one row per real holiday (2026-11-23, FRONTEND ONLY, no backend/database change, local, NOT committed):** ROOT CAUSE (checked read-only on live): NOT duplicate records. Holidays are stored once PER COMPANY, and live has Christmas 25 Dec 2026 in all 12 companies (11 public, 1 optional in Co-Lab) with no duplicate inside any company; `GET /holidays` for an HR/Admin who acts on several companies returns every company's row, and the widget listed each row, so 6 of the 12 Christmases filled the list (capped at 6). FIX: pure `_dashboard/events.ts` (`holidayEvents`, `upcomingEvents`, `uniqueById`) used by the Admin and the HR dashboards: rows fold into ONE event per same date + same name (capitals/spaces ignored) + same observance (public / optional); the second line says "11 companies" / "All companies" / the company name for an optional one; a different name, date or observance stays its own row; meetings are never merged by name or date (only an identical id arriving twice); past holidays dropped, today kept; date order; 6 rows. `/holidays` answers for ONE calendar year, so from December New Year's Day (1 Jan) was never in the answer: both dashboards now also read `/holidays?year=<next year>` (the HR week strip too). The panel's **Calendar** button now opens `/dashboard/holidays` (the holiday calendar; it used to open the Attendance Calendar). Empty state unchanged ("No upcoming holidays or meetings."). Live data right now: only Christmas exists for the future (no New Year row is in the database, none was invented), so the widget shows "Christmas · 11 companies" and "Christmas · Optional · Co-Lab". Tests: `backend/tests/dashboard-events.test.mts` (14, no DB), real Chrome `events-e2e.cjs` (18: 6 companies with the same holidays in a throwaway DB, Admin and HR show each holiday once with the right labels and order, unchanged after refresh, two same-name meetings both shown, HR without a grant, empty state, Calendar link, phone).
- **Weighted score now uses the manager's stars (option A, 2026-11-24, NO migration, no schema change, local, NOT committed; SUPERSEDES "star ratings never change the weighted score" in the 2026-11-20 note):** `tasks.weighted.ts`: a COMPLETED task earns `weight x stars / 5` (5 stars = all, 4 = 80%, 3 = 60%, 2 = 40%, 1 = 20%); a completed task with NO rating earns full credit and is reported as "awaiting rating" (it is NOT treated as 5 stars, and the score moves when the manager rates it); a manager-marked PARTIAL stays 50% whatever the stars (that mark is the manager's own judgement, so no double penalty); To Do / In Progress / Blocked earn 0 whatever the stars; FAILED earns 0; possible points are still the full weights, so 100% now means every counted task is completed AND rated 5 stars (or not yet rated). `WTask.rating` is a REQUIRED field (number | null) so a caller cannot forget it and silently ignore ratings; every caller now attaches the stars it already reads (`withRatings` / `starsFor`): Reports (`reports.service.ts`, person rows and totals), the review queue (`weighted`, `weightedByUser`), Task productivity (people, total, per-task Excel points), My History (day rows and summary), Daily Tasks day/range/today. New additive fields on every `weighted` object: `rated`, `awaitingRating`, `lostToRatings` (points taken off by stars below 5); the score note on screen says "N pts lost to ratings below 5 stars" and "N awaiting rating (full credit until rated)"; the Reports / Productivity / workbook column notes were reworded. The "Tasks completed" COUNT column is unchanged: it still leaves out tasks rated below 4 stars, so that count and the score can still differ for a person with low-rated tasks (a count vs points). Ratings read through `starsFor`, which includes the old-grade read-through (old "Completed" = 5 stars = full credit). Live effect (read-only, same period 20-24 Nov): Komal 100% -> 65.33%, Vishwaa 83.33% -> 71.11%, Junaid 100% -> 65.38%, Itisha 100% -> 88.48%, Devashree 100% -> 95.56%, Muzammil 100% -> 80%, Bushra 30.77% -> 18.46%; the managers had re-rated Komal's tasks that same morning (most to 2-4 stars), and Junaid's tasks are mostly rated 3. Tests: `backend/tests/weighted-ratings.test.mts` (17, no DB), `weighted-ratings.test.cjs` (35, throwaway DB: rate through the real API, every screen and export shows the same figure, re-rating moves it, partial not double counted); `weighted-performance` 40 + 51, `task-edit` 91, `task-approval-visibility` 35, `task-progress` 47, `hr-task-approval` 30, `assigned-tasks` 84, `team-report-book` 25, `dashboard-workbook` 79 pass. `task-productivity-report` (3), `report-export-data` (1), `team-report` (2) and `dashboard-workbook-e2e` (3) have the SAME failures with and without this change (checked against a stash of the previous code: date/data-dependent expectations in the throwaway DB).
- **Overall Performance Score: 60% completion + 40% manager rating, priority removed (built 2026-11-24, NO migration, no schema change, local, NOT committed; SUPERSEDES the 2026-11-20 priority-weighted score and the 2026-11-24 "stars scale points" option A):** the owner decided task priority must not influence any score (everyone would pick High). ONE pure function, `weightedPerformance()` in `tasks.weighted.ts` (the API field is still called `weighted`; every screen words it "Overall Performance Score"). **Overall = Completion % x 0.60 + Manager rating % x 0.40.** ELIGIBLE tasks = approved, not cancelled, not deleted, not manager-assigned, not a manager's own self-managed task, dated inside the period (waiting-for-approval and rejected tasks are NOT eligible and are never completed; they show as separate counts). COMPLETED = eligible, status Completed, not graded FAILED, not a manager-marked PARTIAL ("partly done" is not completed), and finished on or before the report's CUTOFF (end of the period's last day in the company time zone; a task finished later counts as unfinished in that report and is reported as `completedAfterCutoff`; a completed task with no completion time counts; live views like Daily Tasks have no cutoff). Completion % = completed / eligible. Rating % = sum of stars / (rated tasks x 5) over COMPLETED tasks that have a real manager rating 1..5 (`withRatings`/`starsFor`, which still reads an old graded "Completed" as 5 stars); a completed task WITHOUT a rating is never 5 and never 0: it is "pending evaluation" and left out of the rating average. STATUS: `NO_TASKS` (nothing eligible: N/A), `AWAITING_EVALUATION` (tasks completed, none rated: overall is null/"Awaiting evaluation", completion % still shown), `PROVISIONAL` (some rated, some not: score from finalized ratings only, `awaitingRating` outstanding, computed on read and never stored), `FINAL` (all completed tasks rated, OR nothing completed). **Decision I took where the brief was silent: eligible tasks but nothing completed = 0% Final (there is nothing to rate), not N/A.** Priority: `PRIORITY_WEIGHT` is now 1 for every priority, so the OLDER "Task score" (`scoreOf`, Reports "Task score" column, My History day score, Daily Tasks) also counts every task once; the priority field, forms and filters are untouched. API additions on every `weighted` object: `status`, `provisional`, `tasks` (the sample size), `completed`, `notCompleted`, `partial`, `completedAfterCutoff`, `completionPercent`, `completionContribution`, `ratedTasks`, `awaitingRating`, `ratingSum`, `averageRating`, `ratingPercent`, `ratingContribution`, `notScored`; the old `earned`, `possible`, `byPriority`, `rated`, `lostToRatings` are GONE. Cutoff passed by `getProductivity` (+ `weightedTotal`), `getTeamReport` (Reports), `getHistory` (My History). Task rows in `/team-attendance/productivity/tasks` replaced `weightPoints/earnedPoints` with `inScore`, `scoreCompleted`, `ratingStatus` (RATED | PENDING_EVALUATION | NOT_APPLICABLE); `/team-attendance/team-report` now also returns `performance` per person (the same server figure; the workbooks read it and calculate nothing). SCREENS: Task productivity table = Employee, Team / Project, Total eligible, Completed, Pending / incomplete, Completion %, Avg rating, **Overall Performance Score** shown as "84% (12 tasks)" and clickable, Evaluation status chip (Provisional shows "(n pending)"), Daily target, Productivity; clicking the score opens `ScoreBreakdown.tsx` (formula with the real numbers, contributions 60/40, each task's original date, completion date, status, "counted as" and rating or "Pending evaluation"); CSV/Excel (People, Daily, Tasks sheets), the team workbook and the dashboard workbook, Reports, My History, Tasks & Performance (card with formula) and the Daily Tasks note were reworded/updated (Excel notes explain every column; priority-point columns removed). The "Tasks finished in period" count (by completion day) and Productivity % (against the daily target) are kept for the target feature and are labelled as such: they can differ from "Completed" by design (that is the day-of-completion count, this is the period-of-task-date count). KNOWN LIMITS: the Tasks & Performance queue figures have no cutoff (live view), ratings given after a report's cutoff still count for a historical report, carry-forward of unfinished tasks to a later period does not exist in the system (a task belongs to its own date only), and a manager's own Reports row includes his self-managed tasks (team figures exclude them, as before). Live effect, 20-24 Nov (read-only): Komal Sonar eligible 7, completed 7 (100%), ratings 3,3,2,5,4,3,3 = 65.71% -> **86.29%**; Vishwaa Reddy eligible 18, completed 15 (83.33%), ratings average 4.07 = 81.33% -> **82.53%**. All 48 people checked against an independent SQL recomputation (405 checks over four periods, Task productivity + Reports + My History): identical except Ankit Kale's own Reports row (his self-managed tasks, by design). Tests: `backend/tests/performance-score.test.mts` (29, the ten required scenarios + edges), `performance-score.test.cjs` (50, throwaway DB: through the real API, all screens equal, priorities irrelevant, cutoff, permissions), `performance-score-ui.test.cjs` (15, real Chrome: table, breakdown, provisional/awaiting/single-task), the old `weighted-performance`/`weighted-ratings` tests were replaced; task, report and workbook suites pass; `task-productivity-report` (3), `report-export-data` (1), `team-report` (2), `dashboard-workbook-e2e` (3), `dashboard-report-data` (3 people-list expectations about Admin accounts) have failures that are unrelated to scoring (leftover throwaway-DB data and the "Admins are not tracked" rule).
- **TASK_ASSIGN_COMPANY: Productivity Supervisors assign tasks (built 2026-11-24, local, NOT committed; migration `20261124100000_user_capabilities` (NEW TABLE ONLY, with down.sql) applied ONLY to the throwaway DB, NOT to Neon; the grant script was NOT run on live):** Saurabh Jadhav (CP-01, Colab Platforms Limited) and Chirag Parmar (AWL-06, Aayush Wellness Limited) stay EMPLOYEES; each gets one row in the NEW table `user_capabilities` (userId, capability TASK_ASSIGN_COMPANY, CHECK-limited to that value, unique per person; written ONLY by `scripts/provision-capabilities.cjs`, no API writes it; permission is by immutable user id, never by name or e-mail). `lib/capabilities.ts` (`hasCapability`, `capabilitiesReady`: shared in-flight check, re-checks a missing table every 15 s, a vanished table reads as "nobody holds it"; honoured ONLY while the account is ACTIVE and role EMPLOYEE, so a promoted person falls back to their role's rules and a leftover row is ignored; read from the database on every call, so removing the row stops the rights at once). It EXTENDS the existing manager-assigned workflow (`tasks.assigned.ts`, tasks stored as `daily_tasks` rows with `assignedById`, history in `task_events`): `managerContext` now also accepts a capability holder (`assigner: true`). RIGHTS of an assigner: list (`GET /tasks/assigned/people`) = every ACTIVE EMPLOYEE of their OWN company except themselves (not another company, not a manager/HR/Admin, not suspended); assign (`POST /tasks/assigned`: `NOT_SAME_COMPANY`, `ASSIGN_TO_EMPLOYEES_ONLY`, `CANNOT_ASSIGN_SELF`, inactive = 404, a manager = `MANAGER_TASKS_ARE_PERSONAL`) with description, the employee's own project, due date, priority (organising only; assigned date = today, automatic); follow what THEY assigned (`GET /tasks/assigned/by-me`, now also carries `rating` (blank, assigned work is confirmed, not rated)), edit or remove one that has not started, comment, read history; notified when the employee progresses, completes or comments; the employee is notified by the assigner's NAME (notice wording no longer says "Your manager"). NOT given: approving or rating tasks, the approval queue, and **confirming or sending back finished assigned work (403 `ASSIGNER_CANNOT_CONFIRM`, my decision: confirming is a manager's call; consequence: a task the employee completes stays "Completed by employee" for the supervisor and "waiting for confirmation" for the employee unless a manager who assigned work confirms; say if the employee's reporting manager should be able to confirm)**. `GET /tasks` returns `canAssignCompany` (true only for a holder) and Daily Tasks shows a third tab **Assign Tasks** (`?tab=assign`) with sub-tabs **Assign New Task** (the shared `TaskForm`, now exported, `inline` card, searchable employee list, read-only "Assigned on: today") and **Tasks Assigned by Me** (the shared `AssignedByMe` with `assigner`: no Confirm / Send back, shows assigned date, completion date and rating when present); other employees see two tabs and `?tab=assign` shows nothing. SCORES: assigned tasks stay OUT of the Overall Performance Score and out of every submitted-task reader (`activeTasks()` filters `assignedById`), and appear only in the separate "manager-assigned" counts, so they are never counted twice (existing owner decision). Tests: `backend/tests/task-assign-company.test.cjs` (68, throwaway DB: before/after the migration, the script (dry run, wrong name/role/inactive, live refusal, apply, idempotent, audited), both supervisors separately, refusals, monitoring, no approval/rating/confirm rights, score untouched, plain employees refused, capability removal/role change), real Chrome `assign-ui-e2e.cjs` (16); `assigned-tasks` 84, `task-edit` 91, `task-approval-visibility` 35, `hr-task-approval` 30, `task-progress` 47, `admin-hr-separation` 110 pass (`productivity-supervisor` has 1 date-dependent leave check failing, unrelated). LIVE FACTS (read-only): both accounts exist, EMPLOYEE, ACTIVE, and are Productivity Supervisors; Saurabh's company has only 2 other active employees, Chirag's has 9; `user_capabilities` does not exist on live. TO GO LIVE (owner's go-ahead each step): back up Neon, `npx prisma migrate deploy`, deploy, then `CAP_DB_URL=<live> node scripts/provision-capabilities.cjs` (preview), then the same with `--apply --allow-live`.
- **Fix: the Assign To list showed only 2 people (2026-11-24, NO migration, local, NOT committed or pushed):** ROOT CAUSE (backend, not the screen, no hardcoding): for a capability holder `assignablePeople`/`assignNew` (`tasks.assigned.ts`) used "same company AND role EMPLOYEE". Saurabh's company (Colab Platforms Limited) has only Anand Yadav (CS-01) and Junaid Shaikh (Vak-01) as other active Employees, so the list was "2 of 2". NOW the scope is `assignScope()`: a Productivity Supervisor (Saurabh and Chirag are) gets the SAME reach as their monitoring view (`monitoringWhere`: every company, a pinned deployment keeps its one); a capability holder who is NOT a supervisor stays inside their own company. In both cases: status ACTIVE only (so Exited, Terminated, Absconded, Suspended, Invited, Candidate are out), role not ADMIN (Admin accounts are never selectable; the exclusion is by role, not by name), never themselves; **Managers and HR are eligible** (only Admin accounts are excluded); no project, department or reporting-line restriction. The same rule is enforced on assigning (`NOT_SAME_COMPANY` now means "outside the companies you can assign in", `ASSIGN_TO_EMPLOYEES_ONLY` means "not an Admin account"). `GET /tasks/assigned/people` now returns `total` (counted by the server from the full list, never a loaded page) and each person carries `company` and `role`; there is no paging, so nobody is silently omitted; alphabetical. Screen: the label reads "Assign to (47 eligible employees)" (+ " · N match" while searching), search matches name, ID, project, company and role, an empty-result message, the chosen person's company/role/project shown under the box, options read "Name (ID)". Because assigned work can now reach MANAGERS (their Daily Tasks is the self-managed screen, which had no place for work assigned TO them), managers got a fourth tab **Assigned to me** (`?tab=received`, the existing `AssignedToMe`) and every assigned-task notification link is role-aware (a manager's opens `?tab=received`, everyone else's `?tab=assigned`; `notice()` in `tasks.assigned.ts` and the "assigned" event in `tasks.notify.ts`). Unchanged: a manager/Admin assigning keeps its old rules, approvals and ratings, performance scoring, the supervisors' roles. LIVE FACTS (read-only 2026-11-24): 52 active records = 4 active Admins (Aayush Master AM, Devang Master, Jayesh jayesh.ahire, Sanjay Singh Bhati; the System Admin AVT-0001 is TERMINATED) + 48 others (42 Employees, 5 Managers, 1 HR) in 11 companies; the real list function returns 47 for each supervisor (everyone eligible except themselves), identical to an independent SQL count, with no Admin, no inactive person and not themselves. Tests: `task-assign-company.test.cjs` now 75 checks (list equals the database for both supervisors, other company/manager/HR assignable, Admin/self/inactive refused, a non-supervisor capability holder stays in their own company, a manager sees it under Assigned to me and the notification opens that tab; as a supervisor he may READ the task queue but nothing is actionable), real Chrome `assign-ui-e2e` 19; `assigned-tasks` 84, `task-edit` 91, `task-approval-visibility` 35, `hr-task-approval` 30, `task-progress` 47 pass.
- **Automatic task timer (built 2026-11-24, local, NOT committed; migration `20261124120000_task_work_timer` NEW TABLE + TRIGGERS, applied ONLY to the throwaway DB, NOT to Neon):** a task's time follows its STATUS with no Start button. `task_work_sessions` is written by database triggers on `daily_tasks` (insert / status change), `task_entry_details` (blocked flag) and `task_deletions`, using the database clock: In Progress opens ONE session (partial unique index), leaving it closes it, returning opens a new one (history kept). To Do and Blocked act as pause (no Paused/On Hold status exists). A task inserted In Progress for an earlier day (late submission) is not timed. READ-TIME figures (`tasks.timer.logic.ts` pure, `tasks.timer.ts`): active time = sessions intersected with the person's clocked-in intervals minus break/washroom rows (attendance and breaks are only read, never changed; discussions are not subtracted); elapsed = first start to completion/now; paused = elapsed - active; `pausedReason` BREAK / CLOCKED_OUT shown as "Paused due to break/clock-out". Tasks running together are counted ONCE per person (`trackedSecondsByUser`, union). Tasks with no session read "Not tracked" (no backfill: tasks already In Progress at go-live stay Not tracked until their next status change). A manager send-back restarts the timer. `timer` rides on employee lists, assigned lists (both directions), the review queue and the productivity task rows; people rows carry `trackedSeconds/trackedTasks`. Time is a SEPARATE metric: never part of the Overall Performance Score. UI: `_timer/TaskTimer.tsx` (live ticking from the server's figure) on Daily Tasks, Assigned by manager / by me; Task productivity has a "Task time" column and per-task "Time spent". Task priority and Hours spent inputs were removed from the single form, Quick Add, edit dialog and assigned form (stored data kept; sends default MEDIUM). NOT done: tracked-time columns in the Excel/CSV exports and Tasks & Performance page, browser run. Before the migration everything reads "Not tracked". Tests: `task-timer-logic` 19, `task-timer.test.cjs` 43; `task-assign-company` 75, `assigned-tasks` 84, `task-edit` 91, `task-approval-visibility` 35, `hr-task-approval` 30, `task-progress` 47 pass. TO GO LIVE: back up Neon, `npx prisma migrate deploy`, deploy.
- **Work Efficiency Index, policy EFFICIENCY_V1 (built 2026-11-24, local, NOT committed; migration `20261124130000_efficiency_evidence` NEW TABLES ONLY, applied ONLY to the throwaway DB, NOT to Neon; the existing Overall Performance Score and Productivity % are UNTOUCHED and stay beside it):** Reports > new tab **Work efficiency** (`reports/_team/EfficiencyReport.tsx`, API `GET /team-attendance/efficiency?from&to&<HR filters>[&userId]`, same people/permissions as Task productivity; `userId` adds the task lines). Rules are ONE pure file, `tasks/efficiency.logic.ts` (docs at its top): eligible = employee-submitted, approved, not cancelled/deleted, dated in the period (assigned and self-managed tasks never); ONE outcome per task (Completed / Partial (verified) / Returned (quality, still open) / Not completed) so nothing is penalised twice; weights Completed 20, Ratings 25, Time 20, Attendance 10, Partial 5, Not completed 10, Reverted 7, Deleted 3; priority is not used. Time = actual timer hours vs the time the tagged manager CONFIRMS at rating (<=110% of it = full, >=200% = 0, linear between, no early bonus). Attendance reads the one engine (present 1, half 0.5, over scheduled working days; leave/holiday/week-off/not-tracked are not scheduled). Not completed = overdue unfinished / tasks whose date passed (blocked with a written reason = dependency, left out; the cutoff day is not overdue). Reverted = first-pass accepted / (completed + returned); a return marked "requirement changed" never counts; older returns with no recorded reason are only flagged. Deleted = only reason Other with a written note counts. COMPONENT STATES: SCORED / NOT_APPLICABLE (nothing of that kind happened; its weight is shared) / MISSING_EVIDENCE (work to judge, no evidence: the FINAL IS BLANK, owner's choice, e.g. completed tasks with no rating, or none with a confirmed time). Status: NO_TASKS, INCOMPLETE_EVIDENCE, INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE (< 5 eligible tasks: number shown, not trusted), PROVISIONAL, FINAL. NEW EVIDENCE (tables `task_review_attempts`, `task_partial_progress`, `task_time_reviews`; module `tasks.evidence.ts`): the rating box has "Reasonable time for this work (minutes)" (prefilled with the tracked time; `expectedMinutes` on `/review/:id/rating`), "Mark partial" asks for a verified % (1-99) and milestone (currently two `window.prompt`s, a proper dialog is still to do), the revert dialog needs a reason Quality / Requirement changed (`reason`; once the tables exist a revert without it is 400 `REVERT_REASON_REQUIRED`; a partial without progress 400 `BAD_PARTIAL_PROGRESS`). Before the migration every write works as before and only the new inputs answer 503. LIVE FACT (read-only, 25 Oct - 24 Nov): no confirmed times exist, so until managers start confirming time almost every final percentage is blank; older scores (Overall Performance Score) were Vishwaa 98.05, Komal 96.36, Junaid 86.78 and a what-if that ignores the missing time spread people from 10% to 96% (5 in 90-95, 7 in 80-90, 3 in 70-80, 4 below 70, 2 in 95-100) against the old 95-100% bunching; the drivers are completion measured against ALL eligible tasks (Amish 79.6, Rohit 79.6, Bushra 78.3, Muzammil 66.7), ratings below 5 (Junaid 69.0, Amish 69.5, Bushra 62.2), attendance (many 26-91%). Old returns (22 in 30 days) have no reason, so they are flagged not counted. Tests: `efficiency-logic.test.mts` (32), `efficiency-api.test.cjs` (32, throwaway DB); `task-timer` 43, `task-progress` 47, `performance-score` 50, `task-approval-visibility` 35, `task-edit` 91, `assigned-tasks` 84 pass. NOT done: Excel/CSV export of the index, a before/after sign-off screen, a browser run, a policy-version table (the version is a constant carried on every payload). Test-server pitfall: `evidenceReady()` caches true, so restart the test backend after dropping the tables.
- **Manager review shows the tracked time (2026-11-24, no migration, local, not committed):** every Completed row on the existing review screen (Daily Tasks > Employee submitted tasks, `approvals/ReviewQueue.tsx`) has `approvals/TimeReviewBox.tsx`: Started, Submitted, **Actual time taken** (read-only, "Not Tracked" when the task has no work session, never 0), **Reasonable time** (the manager's estimate, "Not set" until entered) with "Set after completion" and a Difference line when both exist. Actual time is the existing timer (`timer` on the queue row, same `timersFor` figure the employee screen and the Work Efficiency report use; tested equal); queue rows also carry `timeReview {expectedSeconds, reviewedAt, afterCompletion:true}` (`timeReviewsFor`, tasks.evidence.ts). The reasonable-time box is NO LONGER prefilled with the actual time (that anchored the estimate) and its text says it is the manager's assessment, entered after the work, never a pre-approved target. Estimates are only ever captured while rating, so every one is a post-completion estimate; a before-work estimate would need a new step at assignment/approval (not built). Efficiency report column renamed "Reasonable (post-completion)". Test: `efficiency-api.test.cjs` now 37 checks.
- **Sessions: "Invalid Token" fix (2026-11-25, local, NOT committed or pushed; NO migration, no data or secret changed):** INVESTIGATION (read-only, live): `login_events` shows live tokens last **7 days** (the code default 1d is overridden on the host); sessions of 44-48 h are active, so no secret rotation happened; Awl sign-ins cluster in the same half hour each morning, but nothing in the logs singles Awl out (no way to see 401s: the server logged none). WHAT THE CODE DID WRONG for everyone: (1) the frontend never ended or refreshed a session: after expiry (or with a junk value like "undefined" in storage) pages showed the raw text `INVALID_TOKEN`, the token stayed, nobody was sent to login; (2) no renewal for people who are working; (3) no reason for a refusal was recorded or returned. NOW: backend `lib/session.ts` (pure rules) + `middlewares/auth.ts`: 401 answers keep `error: "INVALID_TOKEN"` and add `code` = TOKEN_EXPIRED / TOKEN_MALFORMED / TOKEN_SIGNATURE / TOKEN_NOT_ACTIVE / TOKEN_PAYLOAD / NO_TOKEN / ACCOUNT_INACTIVE, each logged once a minute per person+path (`[auth] REASON user(unverified)=<id> issued= expires= METHOD path`; never the token); tokens carry `sst` (original sign-in time); `POST /auth/refresh` (valid token only, same `sid` and `sst`, role/company from the live record, refused after `JWT_MAX_SESSION_DAYS` (default 30) from sign-in with `SESSION_TOO_OLD`; old tokens are NOT revoked, so tabs cannot invalidate each other; an expired token can never be refreshed); the server refuses to start without `JWT_SECRET` and warns on a short/sample one; startup prints the token lifetime. Frontend `lib/session.ts` + `lib/api.ts` (one request core for every `api*` call): a token past half of its life is swapped by ONE shared refresh; a refused call ends the session only if the server confirms it is gone (one shared `/auth/me` probe; network trouble never signs anyone out), the token is already expired, the account is switched off, or another tab signed out; if another tab replaced the token mid-call the call is repeated with it; the person is sent to /login once with a plain notice ("Your session has expired...", "This account is no longer active..."); signing out in one tab signs out the others; a non-token value in storage is dropped. Calls that use `fetch` directly (file downloads, photos, realtime socket) read the stored token without the central handling, they benefit because normal traffic keeps it fresh. NOT changed: role checks, expiry policy (JWT_EXPIRES_IN), logout stays stateless (a copied token works until it expires, as before), no secret or env change. If the Awl team still sees it after deploying, the `[auth]` log line now says exactly why. Tests: `backend/tests/auth-session.test.cjs` (35, throwaway DB + test backend: reasons, refresh, cap, concurrency, tamper/alg-none, switched-off account, three companies), `backend/tests/session-client.test.mts` (38, real api.ts with a stubbed browser); role-identity 18, hr-admin-boundary 25, ip-access (off) 26 pass. Not run in a real browser.
- **Sign-in "Email or password is incorrect" for AWL-03 (diagnosed and fixed 2026-11-25, local, NOT committed; no migration, no account touched):** DIAGNOSIS (live, read-only): AWL-03 is ACTIVE with a valid hash, the ID matched their account on all 23 failed attempts (23-24 Nov), so the password was simply wrong: a bulk temporary password was issued 15 Nov, used 16 Nov 22:44, changed 22:49, never signed in since; the screenshot shows about 12 dots = the length of a temporary password (inference). NOT connected to the Invalid Token issue (no token is ever issued). Other failures were typing slips: `AWL -03`, `AWL- 03`, `Avance07`, `ID-Avance-07`, `ssb0004`, or names. FIX: `lib/employeeIdMatch.ts` (`findLooseUserId`): as the LAST resort after exact ID / email / username, an ID typed with spaces, hyphens or underscores in the wrong place matches when EXACTLY ONE person fits (two or more = no match, never a guess; password still required, same generic error); the same resolver files a failed attempt under the right person in Login Activity (`loginEvents.findAccount`). The sign-in page adds a hint under the generic error (check ID, use the NEW password if the temporary one was changed, ask HR to reset). Still needed: HR/Admin must reset AWL-03's password (forced change at first sign-in); there is NO self-service "forgot password" (needs a decision). Also found: live sees Cloudflare edge addresses (104.22.66.x, 172.7x) as the client IP, so before enabling LOGIN_IP_MODE=enforce add Cloudflare's ranges to `TRUSTED_PROXIES` and check `/api/auth/ip-check`. Test: `backend/tests/login-identifier.test.cjs` (22, throwaway DB); auth-session 35, session-client 38, role-identity 18, hr-admin-boundary 25, ip-access (off) 26 pass.
  - **Sign-in screen follow-up (2026-11-25, FRONTEND ONLY, local, NOT committed):** live check showed no failed attempts after 04:48 IST and AWL-03's record still unchanged (password reset still pending on the owner's side). FOUND: the sign-in lockout (HTTP 429) sends `error: "TOO_MANY_ATTEMPTS"` plus a readable `message`, and the screen would have shown the bare code; `lib/api.ts` now shows the `message` (or a plain sentence). `app/login/page.tsx`: ID trimmed before sending (the password is sent EXACTLY as typed, spaces allowed), password box `autoCapitalize/autoCorrect=off, spellCheck=false` (phones must not alter a password), ID box `autoCorrect=off` and a placeholder with an employee ID, Caps Lock warning, error is `role=alert`, the wrong-password hint grows (temporary-vs-new password, a space at the start or end of the password, "ask HR to reset" after two misses), and a "Forgot your password or can't sign in?" help panel (instructions only: there is still NO self-service reset, which needs the owner's decision). Tests: real Chrome `audit/signin-e2e.cjs` (18, isolated stack; note Next's own route announcer is an empty `role=alert`, so select `form [role=alert]`), `session-client.test.mts` now 40. Test frontend pitfall: 3100 is a PRODUCTION build made in `scratchpad/fe3200` (copy `app` and `lib`, `next build --webpack`, `next start -p 3100`); the first build can stop with a Next.js "SecurityFatalError / upgrade" notice and succeeds on the same command retried. Not done: upgrading Next.js (the notice recommends it; a dependency decision for the owner).

API `GET/POST /api/tasks/previous`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/tasks/previous/:id` (`tasks.previous.ts`; owner = the sign-in, never the body; other managers, HR, Admin, employees: 404/403; audited, the delete line keeps a copy). **Storage trick (must be kept): the rows are `daily_tasks` rows marked `carryCount = -1` (carry-forward was never built, so it is otherwise always 0), status COMPLETED, self-approved.** The shared `activeTasks()` filter now also requires `carryCount >= 0`, so every reader that spreads it (employee lists, approval queue, ratings, productivity, Reports, exports, calendar counts, efficiency, timer) ignores them; the one unguarded lookup (`deleteTaskAsManager`) got the same condition. **Any NEW query over `daily_tasks` must keep `...(await activeTasks())`.** Tests: `backend/tests/previous-tasks.test.cjs` (70, throwaway DB: rules, owner-only, 16 screens/reports identical before and after adding entries, 40 task actions by 5 people cannot reach them); task-approval-visibility 35, hr-team-tasks 38, task-form-managers 41, hr-task-approval 30, task-defaults 21, task-progress 47, task-edit 91, assigned-tasks 84, performance-score 50, hr-visibility 61, attendance-calendar 43 pass. `task-timer.test.cjs` fails its own setup ("expected 4 sessions") with and without this change (time-of-day dependent). Not opened in a browser. The throwaway Postgres (scratchpad `pg/data3`) was rebuilt on 2026-11-26 after Windows cleanup removed table files: fresh `migrate deploy`, `users.monthlySalary numeric(10,2)` added by hand, seed run, an `avance` platform inserted; npm skips embedded-postgres' postinstall, so reinstall that package folder if `initdb.exe` is missing.
- **Global pop-up notifications (2026-11-26, local, NOT committed; NO migration, no new table; reuses the one inbox `lib/notify.ts` + Socket.IO `notifications:changed` + `NotificationsProvider`):** INSPECTED FIRST: ~50 events already notified (tasks, assigned tasks, leave, corrections, both Query Desks, meetings, documents, policies, payslips, warning letters); gaps were attendance/break/reminder events and the pop-up design. NEW triggers (`modules/tracker/tracker.notify.ts`, every one with a `dedupeKey`, failures swallowed): own **clock-in, clock-out, break started, break ended** -> the person only (owner chose pop-ups for all four; called from `tracker.routes.ts` after success, key bucketed to 10 s so a double click makes one); **late arrival, not clocked in, Break Time exceeded** -> the person AND their reporting managers (`managersOf`, HR excluded, once per person per day per kind, fired from the scheduler's `raise()`; owner chose PROBLEMS ONLY for managers, never every normal break); reminders on the 15-minute scheduler: **missing clock-out** (open session > 10 h, once per session), **meeting starting soon** (within 15 min; organizer + invitees who did not decline), **open approved tasks from earlier days** (ONE daily roll-up per employee and ONE per manager), **assigned-task due tomorrow / today / overdue** (due dates come from the assigned-task history; the assigner is told once when overdue); **manager ends a meeting** -> invitees told (`MEETING_ENDED`, `meetings.service.ts end`). `tasks.notify.ts` now exports `managersOf` and `send`. Frontend (`_notifications/`): pure rules in `popup.ts` (category, severity info/success/warning/urgent from the type, duration 7/10/12 s, **`claimToast`: with several tabs open only the first tab that claims a notification in localStorage shows its pop-up; every tab's bell still updates**, a claim older than 2 min is ignored, blocked storage = show), card shows icon, category · label · time, severity ring colour; `POPUP_TYPES` extended (attendance, break, overdue, meeting soon/ended, corrections, optional holiday, warning letter, query approved/assigned/comment...); a "Show pop-ups on this device" checkbox in the bell panel (localStorage `hrms-popups`; history always keeps everything; no sound anywhere). NOT built / by decision: break approval/rejection pop-ups (no such workflow exists; break CORRECTIONS keep their old behaviour), "manager edited an employee's task" (managers cannot edit employee tasks; assigned-task edits already notify), a per-user server-side mute (browser only), a deadline for submitted daily tasks (they have none; overdue = still open after their day). Role mapping: Employee = own actions, reminders, task/leave/query/meeting answers; Manager = team problems (late, not clocked in, Break Time exceeded), overdue roll-up, team task/leave/query items; HR = leave, corrections, documents, HR queries (existing rules, no attendance pop-ups); Admin = individually tagged queries and approvals addressed to them (existing rules). Tests: `backend/tests/notify-events.test.mts` (30, throwaway DB + test backend: recipients, other team/company/HR get nothing, repeats silent, persistence, read/unread), `popup-rules.test.mts` (14), real Chrome `audit/popup-e2e.cjs` (27: pop-up with no refresh, severity, click-through marks read, refresh, two tabs = one pop-up, mute remembered, colleague sees nothing, manager, offline then reconnect, phone width); notifications-read 61, realtime 30, huddle-section 21, activity-policy 83 pass. Not tested: assigned-task due reminders (code path only), sound (none), Admin and HR roles in the browser.
- **Long-break alarm (2026-11-26, local, NOT committed; NO migration):** a person ON a counted break (lunch, personal, other; not washroom or discussion) who is **50 minutes past their daily Break Time** (`LONG_BREAK_OVER_MINUTES` in `tracker.notify.ts`, a constant, not a TeamConfig setting because the owner gave the number; same allowance as BREAK_OVERAGE: team Break Time, else the company default, else 60) triggers `watchLongBreaks()`, run **every minute** by `startTrackerScheduler` (the 15-minute alert job was too slow). ONE alert per break row (`break-over50:<breakId>`). Employee: `BREAK_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` with the exact sentence "Your break time has exceeded the permitted limit by 50 minutes. Please end your break and resume work." plus "Break so far / allowed / over by"; the pop-up is urgent, stays 30 s, and `ALARM_TYPES` (`_notifications/popup.ts`) makes `alarm()` (`lib/chatAlert.ts`, three double-tone bursts) play, respecting the existing sound switch `chat.sound` and the pop-up mute; browsers only allow audio after the person has clicked once on the page. Staff copy `BREAK_LIMIT_EXCEEDED_TEAM` (no sound): reporting managers (not HR through the reporting line), active HR and Admin of the company (own platform or a `company_access` grant), so Admin can monitor; the normal BREAK_OVERAGE alert rows still feed the alerts panel. It NEVER ends the break or clocks anyone out. Tests: `backend/tests/long-break-alarm.test.mts` (18), real Chrome `audit/longbreak-e2e.cjs` (15 of 16 first run; the one miss was a wrong pattern in the test, the text is right): employee on another page gets it with no refresh, exact text, urgent, audio started, manager/HR/Admin see it, unrelated employee nothing, still one alert a minute later, sound off = silent, phone width. Not covered: the sound on a real speaker, and the alert for a break that is already over the limit when the server starts (it fires on the first minute after start).
- **Manager Evaluation in the Task productivity table (2026-11-26, local, NOT committed; NO migration; owner chose audit-log storage, exact From-To period, adjusted values on every screen that shows the score):** Reports > Task productivity: the "Productivity" column (finished tasks / daily target) is replaced by **Manager evaluation** (`reports/_team/EvaluationModal.tsx`, cell in `ProductivityReport.tsx`); the calculation and the Daily target / Evaluation status columns are untouched. A MANAGER (only) gets an Edit button for people in his own reporting scope (never himself); the window edits Overall Performance Score %, Evaluation status (the four EXISTING values: No tasks, Awaiting evaluation, Provisional, Final) and Productivity %, with a required reason (5-300 chars), shows the system value beside each field, has "Reset to system values" and a history list. HR, Admin and supervisors see an "Adjusted" tag (tooltip = system values + reason) and read the history, never edit (403 `EVAL_MANAGERS_ONLY`). **Storage = the immutable audit log**: one row per save (module `Evaluation`, recordType `EvaluationAdjustment`, `targetUserId` = the employee, `before` = the SYSTEM values, `after` = {from, to, score, status, productivity, reason}; a value equal to the system value is stored as null = "use the system value"; all null = reset). The LATEST row for employee + exact period is the effective adjustment (`tasks/evaluation.adjust.ts`, `adjustmentsFor`, `overlayWeighted`); it is laid over `weighted` / `overallPercent` in `getProductivity` (so the table, CSV/Excel and the team workbooks follow), in `/reports` person rows and in the employee's own `/tracker/history` summary, ONLY when the period matches exactly. The service writes its own audit row synchronously and `auditNote({skip:true})` stops the middleware writing a second (the middleware row lands after the response and a reload could miss it). Authorisation = the person must appear in the manager's own `getProductivity` result (`raw: true` = unadjusted), so another manager / company is 404. Routes `POST /api/team-attendance/evaluation`, `GET /api/team-attendance/evaluation/history`. NOT applied (no matching period or not a report): Daily Tasks cards, the review queue, organisation totals (`weightedTotal`, Reports `totals`) keep the system figure; the CSV/Excel do not yet carry a "manager adjusted" marker; the score breakdown dialog still shows the system formula. Tests: `backend/tests/manager-evaluation.test.cjs` (44, throwaway DB, rerunnable: roles, scope, validation, effective values on 4 screens, exact-period only, history, one audit row per save, trigger blocks edit/delete, reset, task records unchanged), real Chrome `audit/eval-e2e.cjs` (24: column swap, modal, validation, save with no refresh, tooltip, history, reset, phone width, HR read-only).
- **Tasks pause with breaks and resume after them, with visible timestamps (2026-11-26, local, NOT committed; NO migration, no new table; owner chose "derive from the break records" and "counted breaks + washroom only"):** INSPECTED FIRST: the automatic task timer already took breaks out of task time and resumed by itself, because a task's active time is its In-Progress sessions minus the person's break rows, computed when read (so the backend is the source of truth and refresh / second tab / logout cannot disagree); the task STATUS stays In Progress during a break. What was missing was explicit pause/resume moments and manager visibility. NOW `breakPauses()` (`tasks.timer.logic.ts`, pure) derives one pause per break/washroom row that overlaps a time the task was running: paused = later of break start and session start, resumed = earlier of break end and session end (null while still on that break), deduplicated, latest 30; discussions are productive time and never pause anything; a To Do / Blocked / completed task has no running session so it is never "paused" or "resumed". `timer` (in `tasks.timer.ts`, so on every screen that already carries it: Daily Tasks, assigned tasks, the review queue, reports) gained `pauses[]` and `pausedSince`. Break start/end and clock-in/out now emit the existing `tasks:changed` signal for the person (`tracker.routes.ts`, reaches the employee and the managers who may see them), so cards refresh at once. UI: label "Paused – On Break since <time>" (was "Paused due to break"), a "N break pauses" list (Paused time · Resumed time (length), "not resumed yet" while on break) in `_timer/TaskTimer.tsx`, and the manager's Employee-submitted-tasks > To do / In progress rows now show the timer (`ReviewQueue.tsx`). Logout during a break: clock-out already closes an open break, the pause gets its resume moment and the task reads "paused due to clock-out"; signing in only (no clock-out) leaves the break running, as before. Tests: `backend/tests/task-break-pause.test.mts` (11, pure rules), `task-break-pause.test.cjs` (29, throwaway DB: two running tasks + a To Do + a finished one, exact pause/resume moments, time excluded, manual pause untouched, several breaks, discussion ignored, refresh, clock-out during a break, manager and unrelated manager, no task records created), real Chrome `audit/taskpause-e2e.cjs` (11 of 12; the miss was a wrong pattern in the test, the text "Paused 2:33 PM · Resumed 2:45 PM (12m)" is right); task-timer-logic 19, efficiency-logic 32, activity-policy 83, task-approval-visibility 35, task-edit 91, assigned-tasks 84 pass. Note: `task-timer.test.cjs` DROPS `task_work_sessions` at its start and fails (time-of-day setup); re-apply `prisma/migrations/20261124120000_task_work_timer/migration.sql` to the throwaway DB afterwards.
- **End Huddle for everyone, finished and made visible (2026-11-26, local, NOT committed; NO migration):** INSPECTED FIRST: the server side already existed (`POST /meetings/:id/end`, organizer only: sets the meeting's end to now, closes every still-open participation at ONE server reading with `endedBy = MANAGER`, each claimed with `updateMany where leftAt null` so a simultaneous End by the person is recorded once, audit line, `meetings:changed` signal to the private rooms of the organizer and the invitees; a person who already ended keeps their own time; no one who never joined gets a record; the join window closes at the new end and one record per person is unique, so a rejoin is refused). What was missing was the manager-facing wording and the employee message. NOW: (1) `meetings/_participation/EndHuddle.tsx`: "End Huddle" button + a dialog asking exactly "Are you sure you want to end this Team Huddle for all participants?" (Cancel / End Team Huddle; non-tracked meetings keep "End meeting"); used on the Meetings page (replaces the inline `EndInline`) and on the organizer's dashboard huddle card (`StandupCard`, only for the organizer: `canEnd` comes from the server). Visible ONLY to the organizer (server `canEnd`); another manager does not even receive the meeting (404). (2) Participants: `me.endedBy` (SELF | MANAGER) is returned by `GET /meetings`; an ENDED card with `endedBy = MANAGER` shows "The Manager has ended the Team Huddle." (`data-testid=ended-by-manager`) above their recorded participation, header "Team Huddle ended"; the Join and End buttons are gone. (3) A huddle the manager just ended stays in the participant's `upcoming` list for 30 minutes (`recentlyEndedByManagerIds`, `StandupCard.relevant`), so the message shows live AND after a reconnect / refresh (without this the ended meeting dropped out of the list and the card vanished). (4) Notification `MEETING_ENDED` ("Team Huddle ended: <title>", body exactly "The Manager has ended the Team Huddle.", link to the meeting) now goes to the PEOPLE WHO JOINED (not every invitee, not the organizer), once (`meeting-ended:<id>`), popping up wherever they are in the HRMS. Time: each person's duration = their own join to the shared end moment; Reports / timesheet read the same participation rows (`huddleSecondsByDay`), so nothing after the end is counted; no work session or attendance row is created. Tests: `backend/tests/huddle-end-all.test.mts` (30, throwaway DB + sockets: owner-only, 3 joined/one self-ended/one never joined, single end moment, durations, live signal to each joined employee, exact message, reconnect view, rejoin and re-end refused, report huddle time, no sessions created), real Chrome `audit/endhuddle-e2e.cjs` (19, twice: dialog text, Cancel, end, E1 on the dashboard updates with no refresh, E2 on another page gets the pop-up, E3 offline then back sees it ended, other manager sees nothing, phone width); `notify-events` (30, updated for joined-only), meeting-cross-company 36, huddle-section 21, participation-logic 28 pass; `huddle-attendance` still has its 11 older stale-expectation failures (same as before this change). Not covered: a participant who never joined is not told (by design).
- **Sound for alert pop-ups (2026-11-26, FRONTEND ONLY, local, NOT committed; supersedes "no sound anywhere" in the pop-up note and extends the break-alarm note):** every pop-up that appears now plays a sound chosen by `soundFor()` (`_notifications/popup.ts`): urgent types (`BREAK_EXCEEDED`, `BREAK_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`, `ATTENDANCE_NOT_CLOCKED_IN`, `TASK_OVERDUE`) = the three-burst `alarm()`, every other pop-up = the soft `ping()` (both in `lib/chatAlert.ts`), and the person's OWN actions (clock-in/out, break start/end, `SILENT_TYPES`) are silent because they just pressed the button. A batch plays one sound (the strongest). Whether anything plays is the EXISTING sound switch `chat.sound` (also used by chat), now shown in the bell panel as "Play a sound with alerts" (switching it on plays a test ping); with it off, or pop-ups muted, nothing sounds. Browsers only allow audio after the person has clicked once on the page. - **Manager Evaluated column: Not Evaluated / Evaluated / Updated (2026-11-26, local, NOT committed; NO migration; extends the "Manager Evaluation in the Task productivity table" note above):** the column header is now **Manager Evaluated** (last column, right of Daily target; the Productivity column stays removed). Each row shows a status chip plus the compact **Edit** button (managers only, own reporting scope). Status = how many evaluations are saved for that person and the EXACT period (counted from the same audit rows, `evaluationStatesFor` in `tasks/evaluation.adjust.ts`): none = **Not Evaluated**, one = **Evaluated** (the manager reviewed and saved, with OR without changing a value; saving the system values back records a review with all overrides null), two or more = **Updated** (also true after a "Reset to system values", which is another save). A chip with changed values reads "Evaluated · adjusted" / "Updated · adjusted"; its tooltip names who saved it and when, how many saves, the system values and the reason; clicking it (any role) opens the history. `getProductivity` returns `evalState {state, count, lastAt, lastById, lastByName}` per person (the older `evaluation` adjustment object is unchanged). HR/Admin/supervisors see the chip and history, never edit. The CSV and the Excel People sheet got a "Manager Evaluated" column (Not Evaluated / Evaluated / Updated). Modal: "Save evaluation", explains that saving marks it Evaluated/Updated even with the system values kept. Tests: `manager-evaluation.test.cjs` now 54 (states per save, HR/Admin see the same, review-only save, other period stays Not Evaluated), real Chrome `audit/eval-e2e.cjs` 25 (chip states, tooltip, history, reset = Updated, HR read-only, phone), dark-mode screenshot checked; report-xlsx 30, hr-visibility 61, hr-filters-reports 35 pass. NOTE: the 11 columns make the table scroll sideways inside its own box (as before; it never overflows the page). The owner's screenshot (Productivity column still shown) was the older running build, the code no longer has that column.

- **Attendance report: people with no clock-in are Absent (fixed 2026-11-28, NO migration, local, NOT committed):** ROOT CAUSE (found in `getAttendanceReport`, `team-attendance/teamAttendance.service.ts`): for TODAY, an eligible person with no clock-in was relabelled "Not clocked in yet" and counted in neither the Absent card nor the percentage's denominator (`counted = present + halfDay + absent`), so 7 expected / 5 present gave Absent 0 and 5/5 = 100%. Finished days were already right (engine: a working day with no clock-in = ABSENT when the company was using the tracker that day; Sunday/no-shift default week Mon-Sat; before joining = Not employed; Admin accounts are not tracked). NOW: the not-yet-in rows are still shown per row, labelled "Absent (not clocked in yet)", but are COUNTED as absent in the cards, the percentage (`(present + half x 0.5) / (present + half + absent)`, now 2 decimals: 5 of 7 = 71.43) and the CSV/Excel totals; `kpis.notYet` is a footnote already inside `kpis.absent`; the status filter "Absent" also finds them; the team workbook summary counts them too (`teamReportBook.ts`). Reports and the HR dashboard ("Absent Today") already counted them. NOT changed: the Attendance Calendar's "Not in yet" (a view with its own rules), My History, payroll. A later clock-in turns the person Present. **"Show your working":** `GET /api/team-attendance/attendance/diagnostic?from&to[&userId]` (same people and filters as the report, employees refused) returns per person and date: employee ID, name, joining date, clock-in, status, whether it is counted and why (leave, holiday, weekly off, nobody clocked in that day, before joining, ...), plus `notInReport` (people the viewer may see who are left out and the account status that explains it); the report has a "Diagnostic CSV" button. To find the two people on live: open Reports > Attendance report for the date or range, press Diagnostic CSV and look at the Reason column (a status such as Terminated/Absconded/Exited, a joining date after the range, or "Nobody in the company clocked in" would explain a missing absence). Tests: `attendance-absent.test.cjs` (30, throwaway DB: all present, two absent = 8/6/2/75, 13-day range checked against independent SQL, Sunday, joiner, filters, today, diagnostic, permissions), late-remark-report 34, hr-filters-reports 35, attendance-calendar 43, hr-visibility 61, attendance-leave-priority 36, team-report-book 25, dashboard-workbook 79 pass. Not done: a real-browser look at the cards.
- **Update Employees: a blank Employee ID is looked up by email or name (2026-11-28, NO migration, local):** the HR sheet's Emp Id cells were blank, so every row said "Employee ID is required". Now (`employeeUpdate.service.ts`, update flow only; Add/Import still need the ID): a row with a BLANK Employee ID is matched to ONE existing person by work email, else by exact name (capitals, dots and spacing ignored; the sheet's Company column narrows it), among the people the viewer may act on (company access; Admin accounts and people without a code are never matched). The existing ID is used as if typed and the row says how it was found ("found by work email" / "matched by exact name, existing ID X used. Check it is the right person"). Refused with a clear message, nothing guessed or created: two people with the same name, an email and a name pointing at different people, no match, another company's person, a row with no ID/name/email. The same person on two rows is still refused (duplicate in file). The upload screen no longer demands the Employee ID column when a name or work-email column is mapped. Tests: `update-by-name.test.cjs` (15), personal-update 27, import-sheet-layout 24, admin-hr-separation 110.
- **HR sheet errors fixed (2026-11-28, NO migration, local):** (1) **dates written in words** such as "05th October 2026", "5 Oct 2026", "October 5, 2026", "05-Oct-2026" are now read as written (`readWordDay` in `personal.logic.ts`, used by the joining date and by dates of birth; the month is a WORD so day/month order can never be mixed up; an impossible day or an unknown month word is still refused). (2) **HR repeating the CURRENT reporting manager** (a sheet's Primary Manager equal to the saved one) is no longer an error, just no change; naming a DIFFERENT manager is still Admin-only (`ADMIN_ONLY_FIELD`, now says "The sheet names a different manager than the one saved"). (3) "Shift end 06:30 is not after the start 09:30" is the existing rule that an end before the start is only read as PM when HR ticks **"Read an early end time as PM"** in the preview; unchanged by decision (the answer could be a real mistake); AM/PM written out ("9:30 AM to 6:30 PM") or the words morning/evening need no tick. Tests: `sheet-errors.test.cjs` (17), `personal-logic` 18, personal-update 27, update-by-name 15, import-sheet-layout 24.
- **Update Employees: an ID that no account has is looked up by email/name too (2026-11-28, NO migration, local):** the HR sheet's ID column held old usernames such as `vishwaa_avatar` (no HRMS account has them), so every row said "No employee has the ID ...". A login account and an employee are ONE row in `users` (Employee ID = username; attendance, tasks, leave, documents link by the internal id, so nothing needs "reconnecting"; IDs never change in the app). In the Update flow a typed ID that matches NO account (any company) is now treated like a blank one: ONE person by work email, else exact name (company-access scope, never an Admin account) and the real HRMS ID is used, with a note: `The ID "vishwaa_avatar" in the sheet is not an HRMS ID: matched by exact name, existing ID X used. Check it is the right person.` An ID that EXISTS is used exactly as typed and never second-guessed; two people with the same name, a name that matches nobody, or another company's person are refused with the reason and what was tried. Nothing is created and no username or password is touched. Tests: `update-by-name.test.cjs` (18), sheet-errors 17, personal-update 27, import-sheet-layout 24.
- **Reporting manager written with a form of address (2026-11-28, NO migration, local):** the HR sheet said "jayesh sir" (Jayesh is an Admin). In Update Employees a "Reports to" NAME is now matched like this: the exact full name first; else a trailing/leading form of address (sir, madam, mam, ma'am, mr, mrs, ms, miss, dr, shri, smt) is dropped and the full name matched again; else, when what is left is ONE word, it is accepted only if exactly ONE ACTIVE Manager or Admin of the person's company has that first name (an ordinary employee's first name never counts). The preview says so ("read as X (ID): the only active Manager or Admin here with that first name. Check it is right"). Two such people = `MANAGER_AMBIGUOUS` (write the full name or the ID); none = `MANAGER_NOT_FOUND`. Changing a reporting line is still Admin-only. Tests: `sheet-errors.test.cjs` (23).
- **Office timings: an earlier end is the evening, no tick (2026-11-28, NO migration, local; owner's decision: "morning 9:30 to evening 6:30"):** in the Update flow's single **Office timings** cell, an end time that is earlier than the start with no AM/PM written (9:30 to 6:30, 10:00 to 6:30) is read as the evening (09:30 to 18:30) without the "Read an early end time as PM" tick; the row carries the note "Office timings: 9:30 to 6:30 read as 09:30 to 18:30 (morning to evening)" and the preview does not ask for the tick. Still refused: a night shift written in 24-hour time (22:00 to 06:00), where adding 12 hours would still be before the start, and the SEPARATE Shift start / Shift end columns, which keep the tick. Words (morning/evening) and AM/PM still work. Tests: `sheet-errors.test.cjs` (28).
- **Reporting manager lookup across companies (2026-11-28, NO migration, local):** the sheet's "Reports to / Primary Manager" NAME was only searched in the EMPLOYEE'S company, so an Admin sitting in another company (Jayesh Ahire) was "not found". Now (`employeeUpdate.service.ts`): the employee's own company is searched first; then every ACTIVE Manager or Admin of the companies the viewer may act on (`viewer.access`; all companies for top management, own company otherwise). Steps per list: exact full name, then the name with a form of address dropped ("jayesh sir", "Mr Jayesh"), then a single remaining word as a first name accepted only when exactly one active Manager/Admin has it. The preview note says where the manager was found ("was found in X, another company you manage", "read as Jayesh Ahire (code, company)"); two matches = `MANAGER_AMBIGUOUS`; none = `MANAGER_NOT_FOUND` (names both searches). A viewer limited to their own company still cannot see another company's manager. The change line now shows the cross-company manager's name. Changing a reporting line is still Admin-only, so HR who name a different manager still get `ADMIN_ONLY_FIELD`: upload as an Admin (e.g. an Admin with the all-companies grant). Tests: `sheet-errors.test.cjs` (33).
- **HR upload: Admin-only parts are skipped, the rest of the row saves (2026-11-28, NO migration, local; owner asked to solve "Only an Admin can change who a person reports to"):** in Update Employees, when HR (not Admin) uploads a row that names an Admin-only change (a DIFFERENT reporting manager, system role, team, projects, secondary manager), that part is NOT applied and is no longer an error: the row gets a note "<Field> was NOT changed: only an Admin can change it ... Everything else on this row is saved; an Admin can apply this part." and the other columns (personal details, shift, employee type, email, ...) are saved as usual. HR still can never apply those changes (the rule is unchanged, enforced because nothing is proposed or committed for them); an Admin uploading the same sheet applies them. A sheet that repeats the CURRENT manager is simply no change. Tests: `sheet-errors.test.cjs` (35, incl. an HR commit that saves the city while the manager stays), `import-sheet-layout` (24), update-by-name 18, personal-update 27, admin-hr-separation 110, hr-admin-boundary 25.
- **Task timer vs manager approval (investigated 2026-11-28, local; one real defect fixed, the reported symptom NOT reproduced):** the timer is written ONLY by database triggers when a task's STATUS changes (`task_work_sessions`, migration 20261124120000) and is read per task (`tasks.timer.ts`, `computeTimer`); `approveTask` writes only `approvalStatus/approvedById/approvedAt/rejectionNote`, so it never fires a timer trigger. Reproduced through the real routes (`tests/task-timer-approval.test.cjs`, 40 checks; stored sessions compared byte for byte before/after each step): two running tasks, approving A leaves A's and B's sessions identical (same session, same start, still open, no extra rows), time carries on; approving a completed task, a To Do task, several in a row, with a comment, a Blocked task, a correction request, rating, a pending correction request and parallel page reads all leave every other task's sessions untouched; only a manager's Revert / Reopen opens a NEW session, on that task alone (by design). Real Chrome on the demo stack (`audit/timer-e2e.cjs`, 14 checks): the employee's page stays open while the manager approves A, then a refresh, a second tab and a dropped connection: A and B keep running, times only go up. **Defect found and fixed:** `approveTask` checked "still PENDING" and then wrote unconditionally, so three simultaneous approvals (double click, two tabs) ALL returned 200 (decision and notices recorded more than once, last write wins); the write is now `updateMany where approvalStatus = PENDING` and a loser answers 409 `ALREADY_ANSWERED`. Look-alikes to check first when someone reports "approval restarted/stopped a timer": the manager's separate **Revert / Reopen** (restarts that task by design), an assigned task's **Send back**, the employee's own **Progress** dropdown, and a **break or clock-out** (running tasks show "Paused - On Break / clock-out" and resume by themselves). A task that was already In Progress before the timer migration reads "Not tracked" until its next status change.
- **Five designations added; new arrival rule: late after 9:45, half day after 10:00, 3rd consecutive late = half day (2026-11-28, NO migration, local):** (1) `lib/designations.ts` gained AI Developer, AI Senior Developer, AI Team Lead, Fullstack Developer Intern, Performance Marketing Executive (list now 37, alphabetical); the matcher also ignores a hyphen with spaces ("AI- Team Lead" = "AI Team Lead"); nobody's saved designation changed. (2) **Arrival rule** (`team-attendance/arrival.logic.ts`, `ARRIVAL_RULES`): on time up to **09:45** (shift 9:30 + 15 min buffer), Late up to **10:00** (was 10:15), Half Day after 10:00. (3) **Late streak** (`lateStreakHalfDays`, owner's answers): 3 late WORKING days in a row turn the 3rd into a Half Day and the count then restarts from zero (late x6 = half days on the 3rd and 6th); an on-time day, a half-day arrival or an absence breaks the run; approved leave, half-day leave, holidays, week-offs, "not tracked" and today-before-check-in are skipped (neither count nor break); `STREAK_LOOKBACK_DAYS` = 31 earlier days are read so a run that began before the window counts (a run older than that could change the phase). **Scope = the arrival VIEW only** (owner chose): the Attendance Calendar (status + `lateStreakHalfDay`, remark "3rd late working day in a row: counted as a half day") and the attendance report's Late Remark / `arrival` (+ `arrivalStreak`, Half Day filter); payroll, Reports, alerts and the report's own Status column keep their own rules (shift + grace, hours worked), so the same day can read Present in Status and Half Day in Late Remark. NOT applied to the live Attendance board's per-person arrival chip (it would need the previous 31 days on every refresh). Tests: `late-streak.test.mts` (15), `late-streak-api.test.cjs` (19, real calendar and report: 6 lates, a run broken by an on-time day, a run crossing a Sunday and starting before the window), `designation-sheet.test.mts` (11), arrival-logic 39, attendance-calendar 43, late-remark-report 34 (old 10:15 expectations updated to 10:00/10:01), attendance-absent 30.
